# ⚠ DO NOT REMOVE
SCOPE: the **Project Order / CAPEX dashboard** — `capex/` in bim-ootb (vanilla HTML/JS, no framework, no build step, no client name in code) — its data, its twin with legacy iDempiere, and its road to
being **editable without code or AI**. Every number comes from rows of a real DB (SQLite ERP twin = Source A, legacy iDempiere via stock WebServices = Source B). An empty population prints
"no data" / INCONCLUSIVE, never 0 % or green. **Read the log after every run (`§DASH_*`, `§SHED_*`, `§FILM_SHOT`); the exit code is not evidence.** Spec before code; every witness names the issue it
proves and has a break switch that must FAIL. Honour this block until the lane is closed. Full history (sessions 88 + 77, 2026-11-02 → 11-03): `prompts/archive/CAPEX_DASHBOARD_history_2026-11-02_to_11-03.md`.

## ▶ RESUME HERE 2026-11-11 — the Sysnova dashboard and the BIM-ERP Project Order are ONE flow (the ONLY current block; the 2026-11-05 block below is history)
**Owner rulings of 2026-11-11 (quoted where they decide something):** the project is alpha, *"new documents version tagged, old simply replaced"* · *"our local machine is our test and dev server, so you can apply it"*; refresh it from the base seed each time *"so we can trace from base"*; *"leave it for ongoing Sysnova work"* · *"the 2Pack zip is only pure metadata; the Java is in the src folder of the OSGi component"*; the classes are made from the Excel data model too · the user guide carries no internal-script (JS) wording, install steps live in its Developer's note · Sysnova's dashboard is a prototype: *"as long we define each such field in the user guide, the Sysnova team can read and review it"* (our defaults, labelled, one question each).
**The design in one picture** (each arrow is a place a witness can stand):
`BIM model (IFC)` → Modeller/Viewer **push** → `C_Project` + phases + lines (the **Project Order**, keyed by the model's name; spec `BIM_TO_PROJECT.md` / bim-compiler `docs/BIMtoProject.md`) → **BIM Document** files drawings and models against that project (table `BIM_Document`: project, task, deliverable no, **revision**, status DR/IP/CO/RE; the newest CO supersedes the older; spec bim-compiler `ERP_IDEMPIERE_UX_PARITY.md` §BIMDOC*) → the **register** (latest revision of each deliverable + the superseded ones, as JSON with each file's sha256) → the Modeller **walker's input gate** (`DISC_WALK_DRAWN_INTEGRATION.md` §12a: CO placed, IP provisional, DR/RE refused) · and the same `C_Project` rows → this **dashboard** (tiles/panels/Home goals as PA rows). Two runtimes carry the same model: **Kernel-ERP twin** (a JS module, built in, started from Plugins & Releases) and **real iDempiere** (a metadata-only 2Pack + the OSGi component `org.idempiere.bimdocument`, both generated from `erp/plugins/models/BIM_Document.xlsx` by `erp/tools/xlsx_pack.js`).

**Landmines learned 2026-11-11 (do not re-learn):** never run iDempiere's `ImportIdempiere.sh` on this shared Postgres (it runs `dropuser adempiere`) · a copied server dir's `utils/myEnvironment.sh` must name the instance's own DB and `RUN_SyncDB.sh` must run from its `utils` folder · a 2Pack creates a physical column only when `IsSyncDatabase=Y` · a workbook sheet's columns are the union over ALL rows (ColumnSQL lives on later rows) · a bundle with the pack activator must not be lazy-activated · `pkill -f` with a pattern inside your own command kills your shell: kill by PID · a half-killed refresh leaves a late-starting server holding the DB (the script now uses `dropdb --force`) · the model layer cannot run in a standalone JVM, tests run inside the server (console command) · docs deploy only from a full worktree via `scripts/safe_gh_deploy.sh`; `docs/FastScriptGuide.md` has CRLF bytes: edit byte-safe, write versions as <x.y.z>.
**⛔ Owner / Sysnova questions open (one each; none blocks T1-T3):** Q2 the five stage names and order · Q3 their forecast formula (ours: EAC = Spent + (Budget − Done) ÷ CPI) · Q4 is "verified" delivery or invoice (ours: completed Project Issues, which overlaps Actual Cost) · whether the document tile counts only Issued for construction (our default) or also Issued for review separately.

link-back Description (`BIM src db=…`) with the plan (`viewer/erp_push_plan.js` + the `BIMOOTB_Create_C_Project` whitelist), re-Pack-Out, reinstall, re-run B3 headless. Then the film "Installing BIM to
iDempiere" (storyboard §8.5 — ⛔ owner OK of the storyboard + language set still pending). Later: the Tycho/Maven build (the convention) replaces `tools/build_bundle_poc.sh`.
**Machine state (older detail):** local pilot `~/idempiere-pilot` (:8088, DB `idempiere_pilot`, RUNNING) holds the POC types (Filter Constant `1=1`), the BIMOOTB_* types + master role, the R0 PA rows + goal, role
"BIM Push Test" 1200003, and test projects (SHED #1000007 + SHED_preR1 #1000004, HHS #1000010 + HHS_Office_Federated_preR1/_preKeys #1000005/#1000006, HHS_PLUGIN_POC #1000009, witness runs
HHS_VIEWER_PUSH_* #1000011-#1000014 — never deleted: the safety gate refuses deletes; rename + IsActive=N instead). Clean plugin-only test instance `~/idempiere-bimplugin` (:8098/:8454, DB
`idempiere_bimplugin` = TEMPLATE pilot_snap, STOPPED; `start.sh`/`stop.sh` — its stop pattern matches ONLY that path; install = 2 `bundles.info` lines). Scratch: bundles under the session scratchpad
(rebuild with bim-compiler `iDempiereOOTB/org.idempiere.bimootb/tools/build_bundle_poc.sh <server> <bim-ootb> <out>` — it must print `§BIMOOTB_BUNDLE`).
**Landmines learned this session (do not re-learn):** iDempiere refuses a FREE `Filter` WS param on any model save (IDEMPIERE-1784) → find by DataRow keys · a WS access refusal is cached 60 min per
(role, type) → restart or Cache Reset after including a role · Pack Out exports only the LOGIN client's rows, and role 0 is not allowed on web services (use a System WS role) · a web bundle that
Require-Bundles org.adempiere.ui.zk fails to start (split the viewer into its own bundle) and the tab bundle must Require-Bundle zcommon/zk/zul/zweb · headless Chrome: `--disable-gpu` kills WebGL —
use `--use-gl=swiftshader --disable-gpu-sandbox --enable-unsafe-swiftshader` · never `pkill -f <text in your own command>` · a copied server's stop script must be re-pointed BEFORE first start.
**Rules that bind:** PRIME RULE · CARDINAL TWIN RULE (SQLite == legacy incl. L&F; fixes on the SQLite side; exemptions only in the owner's words) · AD-LAYER LAW (one generic engine, X of N) · one
owner per relation (4D: `bim-compiler prompts/4D_MODEL_INTEGRITY.md §I`) · never move the Team base hash (`erp/patches/ad_seed.db.sql`) without the owner — use a boot overlay · every legacy-server config
change needs the owner's go (local pilot / test instance only so far) · never `git stash` · never claude-in-chrome · bim-ootb only in a `/tmp/wt-*` worktree · no `.db` committed · agents = Sonnet.
**LEGACY CONVENTION (owner 2026-11-03: "at the legacy side, all changes, add-on be following its convention ie as a plugin or migration script if there is no processes")** —
anything we change or add on a legacy iDempiere (pilot or client) ships the way iDempiere ships its own: DATA-only (AD/PA/WS rows, no Java) = a migration script in its format
(`migration/iD13/postgresql/<yyyymmddHHMM>_<ticket>.sql`, first statement `SELECT register_migration_script('<file>') FROM dual;`, plain DML with IDs ≥ 1000000 + `*_UU`, as written by ZK's
"Log Migration Script"; measured in `~/idempiere-dev-setup/idempiere/migration/iD13/postgresql/202601130707_IDEMPIERE-6815.sql`); anything with a process / callout / model validator = an OSGi
plugin (2Pack in the bundle). **Staging (owner, same day: "we do things adhoc as POC first, quick and dirty. But once proven or clearer, we evolve to convention"):** a POC on the LOCAL pilot may be
ad-hoc; once it is proven — and always before it reaches a client server — it is re-expressed in the convention. ✅ Done for the WS types (§5.29): the six ad-hoc `ws_capex_*.sql` are now five migration scripts and are deleted.

## 2. Architecture (unchanged)
`adapter.load() → data` (projects, phases, tasks, lines, orgs, phaseRef, currencies, wf, unavailable[]) → `tiles.compute(data, filters)` (pure) → page draws / twin compares flattened maps.
`?today=` injectable, `?project=<Value>` scopes one project on both sources, currencies never summed together. Source B in a browser needs a snapshot (no CORS on legacy).

## 3. Tile → SQL table (the thing the client reviews)
`P` = C_Project rows after the Org (`AD_Org_ID`) and Category (`ProjectCategory`) filters; `S` = `P` after the Status filter; `A` = `S` that are Active (the "(Active)" tiles). Status filter: Active = `IsActive='Y' AND Processed='N'`,
Closed = `Processed='Y'`, Inactive = `IsActive='N'`, All. "Today" = the page date. Legacy column = same name through WS read type (§4).
Tiles marked (D) use a formula the client never stated: the page prints it as a footnote under the tile; the thresholds are ⛔ questions (§7).

| # | Tile | Formula over iDempiere columns (SQLite SQL form) | Population |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Total Projects (All) | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM C_Project` (Org/Category filters only); sub "Closed" = `COUNT(*) WHERE Processed='Y'` | P |
| 2 | Active Projects | `COUNT(*) WHERE IsActive='Y' AND Processed='N'`; sub = of those, `C_Phase.Name='Closed'` (D) | P |
| 3 | Approved Budget | `SUM(PlannedAmt)` per `C_Currency_ID` | S |
| 4 | Actual Cost | `SUM(ProjectBalanceAmt)` per currency; sub "% of plan" = Actual / `SUM(PlannedAmt)` (no data when plan is 0/NULL) | S |
| 5 | Committed Cost | `SUM(CommittedAmt)` per currency; sub = `SUM(InvoicedAmt)` invoiced (the client's "verified / contract" split has no stated column: ⛔ Q4) | S |
| 6 | Forecast Cost at Completion | **definition pending (client)** — ⛔ Q3 | — |
| 7 | Projects Completed this year (D) | `COUNT(*) WHERE Processed='Y' AND strftime('%Y',DateFinish)=strftime('%Y',today)` | P |
| 8 | Project Health (D) | per project in A: BAC=`SUM(phase.PlannedAmt)`, PV=`SUM(phase.PlannedAmt WHERE EndDate<=today)`, EV=`SUM(phase.PlannedAmt WHERE IsComplete='Y')`, AC=`ProjectBalanceAmt`; SPI=EV/PV, CPI=EV/AC when AC>0. **No EVM data** = PV is 0/NULL. Else Green = every defined index >=0.9, Red = every defined index <0.9, Amber = mixed. 0.9 is the only threshold the client stated (At Risk SPI<0.9) | A |
| 9 | Project Stage (pipeline) | `SELECT C_Phase.Name, COUNT(*) FROM C_Project JOIN C_Phase USING(C_Phase_ID) GROUP BY 1`; NULL → "(no phase)". The client's five stages (Concept…Closing) have no stated mapping onto `C_Phase`: ⛔ Q2 | A |
| 10 | Physical vs Spent | Physical = `SUM(phase.PlannedAmt WHERE IsComplete='Y') / SUM(phase.PlannedAmt)` per currency (client caveat: "planned-weighted phase progress"); Spent = `SUM(ProjectBalanceAmt)/SUM(PlannedAmt)`. Client caveat shown verbatim: "Physical = planned-weighted phase progress. Spent = issued material cost + budget (payment-based financial progress is not in the ERP)." | A |
| 11 | Schedule Performance (D) | per project in A: **No schedule data** = no phase with both StartDate and EndDate; **At Risk** = SPI<0.9 (client's number); else **Delayed** = any phase with `IsComplete='N' AND EndDate<today`; else **On Time**. Average Delay = mean over Delayed+At Risk of `max(today-EndDate)` over its overdue incomplete phases (days) | A |
| 12 | Budget Status (D) | per project in A: **Not started (no cost)** = `ProjectBalanceAmt` NULL/0; **Over** = `ProjectBalanceAmt > COALESCE(PlannedAmt,0)`; else **Within**. Actual Overrun = `SUM(ProjectBalanceAmt-COALESCE(PlannedAmt,0))` over Over, per currency | A |
| 13 | Approval Pending (sidebar badge) (D) | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM AD_WF_Activity WHERE WFState='OS' AND Processed='N'` (the client's 514 has no stated definition: ⛔ Q5) | system |
| 14 | Last Updated | `MAX(C_Project.Updated)` (data, never the clock) | P |
| 15 | Sidebar: Projects | table of P: Value, Name, Org, Currency, Planned, Actual, Committed, Phase, "BIM" link | P |
| 16 | Sidebar: Budget & Cost | per-project Planned / Committed / Invoiced / Actual rows | P |
| 17 | Sidebar: Procurement Risks, Handover | **definition pending (client)** — ⛔ Q6 | — |

BIM link (row action): `../viewer/viewer.html?db=<buildingsBase><Value>_extracted.db&record=<C_Project_ID>` (viewer `config.js` reads `db` and `record`; the integration spec's
`index.html?building=<name>&record=<id>` form is the landing page's, which the Viewer does not read — recorded, ⛔ Q7). Shown ONLY when the file answers HEAD 200.


## 4. FRAMEWORK — the dashboard as iDempiere's own data (session 77's take, measured 2026-11-03)
**Why this is the finale:** every lane meets here — DXF2BIM/IFC → 4D/5D (computed, never typed) → Project Order (ERP) → push (WebServices) → twin (parity) → dashboard (decision). A number on a tile is
only as good as the chain behind it; the twin is what lets us say so with evidence. So the dashboard must not become a second, private formula engine.
**Measured:** legacy pilot holds `PA_Measure` 4 · `PA_MeasureCalc` 6 (columns `SelectClause, WhereClause, DateColumn, KeyColumn, OrgColumn, BPartnerColumn, ProductColumn, AD_Table_ID`) ·
`PA_Goal` 4 · `PA_ColorSchema` 2 (e.g. "Red (50) - Yellow (100) - Green": mark1 50 / mark2 100 / mark3 9999) · `PA_DashboardContent` 12. The SQLite seed has `pa_measure`, `pa_goal`,
`pa_dashboardcontent`, `pa_ratio*`, `pa_report*` (+ `pa_measurecalc`, `pa_colorschema`, `ad_chart` from `erp/patches/build_ad_seed_patch.sh:40`) — ~~**but no SQLite code runs them**~~ ⚠ WRONG, see §5.18: `erp/overlay/goal_engine.js` runs them. So: legacy's design is "dashboard = rows"; ours is "dashboard = `tiles.js`".
**Consequences (to verify in R0, not assumed):** (a) much of a CAPEX dashboard is expressible in legacy WITHOUT Java — counts/sums as MeasureCalc SQL, thresholds as a ColorSchema (= client Q1), charts as
AD_Chart; (b) per-project logic (EVM health, schedule buckets, average delay) needs either SQL in MeasureCalc or stays named in `tiles.js`; (c) once tiles are PA rows, the SAME definition runs on both
sides — the twin compares a definition's result, not two hand-written formulas — and the "editor" is the generic AD windows (AD-LAYER LAW) — "without coding or AI" is literally true.
**Our value, stated honestly:** not that a dashboard is impossible in legacy, but (1) the DATA that fills it comes from BIM (the empty "no schedule / no cost" buckets), (2) it runs local-first on the twin,
(3) one definition is proven equal on both sides, (4) it is editable as data — and if Sysnova prefers, the same PA rows also run in their legacy ZK.

  persistent harness twin = SQLiteIDEMPIERE lane proposal (`bim-compiler prompts/SQLiteIDEMPIERE.md §CAPEX-TWIN`).

### 5.1 R1 BUILD SPEC (2026-11-03, written before code) — the fold takes the 4D owner's programme
**Measured first (logs: `cache_4d_run.js HHS_Office_Federated` at code key `b1ea8c3659df` — the persisted run was stale code `dda4439f69cd`):** `§CPM_RUN makespanDays=34.4`; 16 tasks, 6 phases,
day 0 → 53 (Superstructure 0-23, Architecture Envelope 14-25, MEP Rough-in 21-48, Architecture Closeup 30-41, MEP Final 33-52, Finishes 45-53). All 6,839 priced elements sit in a 4D task (0 without).
Fold line keys whose elements span TWO 4D phases: `IfcSlab` (Superstructure 79 / Envelope 4) and `IfcPlate` (Superstructure 191 / Envelope 438) — so a line cannot be keyed by product alone.
**Input (the owner's own objects, no new shape):** `opts.schedule = { start:'YYYY-MM-DD', tasks:[{id, phase, storey, sDays, eDays, guids}], phaseSeq:{phase→sequence} }` = `materializeZones(...).tasks`
(what `cache_4d_run.js` persists as `run.json.tasks`); `phaseSeq` from `rates/4D_template.json phases[].sequence` (owner of the task grid). Days → date = `start + days` (the owner's `_addDays`).
Priced rows gain `guids` (the elements of the row); a producer that has a schedule groups its rows by (disc, cls, storey, 4D task) so no row spans two tasks.
**Rules:**
- R1-a phases = the 4D phases present; SeqNo = template sequence; StartDate/EndDate = start + min(sDays) / start + max(eDays) over that phase's tasks.
- R1-b one C_ProjectTask per 4D task, Name = `<phase> — <storey>` (the name the owner writes into `tasks.name`), under its phase; SeqNo = template sequence.
- R1-c a row's phase and task = the 4D task of its guids. A row with no guids, a guid in no task, or guids in >1 task → phase `Unsequenced`, no task, note `ROW_NOT_IN_ONE_4D_TASK` (never a guess).
  With a schedule, C_ProjectLine natural key = (project, product, 4D task) — every line sits in exactly one Gantt bar; a product over two bars is two lines. Without one: (project, product) as today.
- R1-d no `opts.schedule` → phases grouped as today (classify) with NULL StartDate/EndDate and note `NO_4D_SCHEDULE`; tasks per resource as today. No duration is computed on ANY path.
- R1-e deletion: the duration loop (`LR.productivity`, `Math.ceil(count/20)`, the serial cursor) goes; `laborRates` is no longer read by the fold.
- R1-f producers: `capex/tools/fold_demo.js` reads the schedule via `scripts/cache_4d_run.js read()` (current code key; builds if missing); browser callers (`find_erp_push.js`,
  `proj_order_state.js`) pass none yet → `NO_4D_SCHEDULE` (honest); wiring them to the Time Machine's `materializeZones` result is NAMED, not in this build.
**Witness W-FOLD-ONE-SCHEDULE (`capex/tests/witness_fold_one_schedule.js`, names the defect: "the Project Order carries a programme the 4D owner never produced"):** HHS from the cache — every phase's
Start/End == start + min/max of its 4D tasks; phase set == 4D phase set; task count == 4D task count; every line's phase == the 4D phase of its rows' guids; elements conserved; project PlannedAmt with vs without a
programme differs by at most the per-line 3-dp quantity rounding (bound computed); no `NO_4D_SCHEDULE`. Negative: no schedule → 0 dated phases + `NO_4D_SCHEDULE`. Break switch `BREAK=1` (shift one task) must FAIL. RED on main (main ignores `opts.schedule`:
its phase dates come from its own serial duration loop). Regression before/after: `poc_proj_push`, `poc_fin_evm`, `poc_fin_period`, `witness_s9_pure` (baseline on main: proj_push FAIL and
fin_evm FAIL — both ONLY the stale `navigate_find.js` source-contract check, pre-existing; fin_period PASS; s9_pure 8/0/0).

### 5.3 R0 BUILD SPEC (owner "go" 2026-11-03, written before code) — can legacy compute a CAPEX tile as PA data, with no Java?
**Legacy mechanism, read in source (`~/idempiere-dev-setup/idempiere/org.adempiere.base/src/org/compiere/model/`):** a Calculated measure's value =
`MMeasureCalc.getSqlPI` (`:153-189`: SelectClause + " " + WhereClause, date filter only for data type T) → `addRestrictions` → `role.addAccessSQL(sql, null, true, false)` (`:612`, the role's client/org
access) → `DB.getSQLValueBD` → `MGoal.setMeasureActual` (`MMeasure.java:500-541`), performance = actual ÷ target, 0 when target is 0 (`MGoal.java:428-436`). It is computed ONLY on a model save of the
goal (`MGoal.afterSave` new record / target / scope changed → `updateGoal(true)`, `:597-605`) or on a ZK dashboard render (`WPAWidget.java:54` → `updateGoal(false)`, which runs when DateLastRun is null
or older than the refresh interval, `:390-399`). A plain SQL insert computes nothing.
**Rows (the tile = "Approved Budget", USD line; `tiles.js` population S with status=all → `SUM(PlannedAmt)` of C_Project where C_Currency_ID=100):**
- migration script (LEGACY CONVENTION, data only) `capex/legacy/migration/iD13/postgresql/202611031900_CAPEX-R0.sql`: PA_MeasureCalc "CAPEX Approved Budget (USD)" (AD_Table C_Project,
  `SELECT SUM(PlannedAmt) FROM C_Project` / `WHERE C_Currency_ID=100`, OrgColumn AD_Org_ID) + PA_Measure (type C, data type S) — client 11, IDs from the 1000000 band + fixed UUs; then the stock
  post-migration `update_sequences` so later saves cannot collide.
- the GOAL is user data (a goal belongs to a user/role, as in the window): created through stock `create_data` (a model save → legacy computes it). Target: **none stated by the client** → 0, so
  GoalPerformance = 0 and the colour is NOT judged (INCONCLUSIVE, §6 Q1). Colour schema = the stock "Red (50) - Yellow (100) - Green" (mandatory column).

approved_budget USD over a fresh legacy snapshot (same role 102), to the cent; DateLastRun set by the save. Negative: a goal on a measure whose SelectClause is broken must NOT yield a number
(an error, or MeasureActual unset) — that negative measure is inserted pilot-only by the probe and deleted after. Break switch `BREAK=1` compares against the tile + 1 cent → FAIL.

Legacy's own measure SQL (`SELECT SUM(PlannedAmt) FROM C_Project WHERE C_Currency_ID=100` + role 102 access) == `tiles.js` approved_budget USD over a fresh legacy read: 4,996,920.87.
**Three legacy behaviours, each from the log AND the source — they bind R2 (the SQLite PA engine must twin them, CARDINAL RULE):**
- (a) **A goal is not computed when it is created.** `MGoal.afterSave` → `updateGoal(true)` → `MMeasure.updateCalculatedGoals` → `MGoal.getMeasureGoals` (`MGoal.java:150-163`) reads with NO trx, so the
  goal still inside its create transaction is invisible: 0 goals updated. MeasureActual stays 0; DateLastRun is set by the constructor default (my probe's first "computed = DateLastRun set" check was
  a bad proxy and is gone). It computes on the next model save that CHANGES target/scope, or on a ZK render once the refresh interval (default 30 min, `MGoal.java:390-399`) has passed.
- (b) **Performance uses the previous committed target.** The same no-trx re-read means a save of target 1→0 scores actual ÷ 1 (GoalPerformance 4,996,920.867 with target 0). The probe resets it to
  legacy's rule for target 0 (= 0, `MGoal.java:428-436`) and logs it.
- (c) **A broken measure shows 0, never an error.** `DB.getSQLValueBD` (`DB.java:1393-1404`) catches the SQL error, logs, returns null; `MMeasure.java:530-535` turns null into 0. No error reaches the caller.
  ⚠ This collides with this lane's PRIME RULE ("an empty / failed population prints no data, never 0") — the twin of a legacy PA tile shows 0 where our page shows "no data". Not decided here (R2 design).
**HHS re-push (pilot write, owner go):** the deletion of the old tree was refused by this session's safety gate, so the old project was kept and made reversible instead: #1000005 Value →

### 5.5 DECISION + R1-f SPEC (2026-11-03; owner "you can decide") — written before code
**Decision (the §5.4 (c) collision):** the SQLite PA engine (R2) returns what legacy returns — a measure whose SQL fails is **0**, as in legacy (CARDINAL TWIN RULE, incl. L&F) — and logs
`§PA_MEASURE_ERROR measure=<id> sql=<…> err=<…>`. The PRIME RULE is kept by the witness, not by a different number: the twin judges any measure whose SQL errored on EITHER side as INCONCLUSIVE,
never MATCH, and a page built on PA rows shows the error mark beside the 0 only where legacy ZK also does (to be read from the ZK source when R4 builds it). `tiles.js` tiles keep "no data" until R3
moves them onto PA rows. (a) and (b) are twinned too: no compute on create, performance against the previously committed target.
**R1-f — the viewer's push takes the programme the viewer already has.** Owner of "which schedule is active": `ScheduleAuthor.activeSchedule(db)` (authored `SCH_AUTHORED` first, else the imported
one). Owner of the rows: what `materializeZones` / the import wrote — `tasks` (non-summary, dated) + `task_elements`. New `ProjFold.scheduleFromDb(bdb, template)` → the fold's `opts.schedule`
shape: `start` = the schedule's earliest task start; per task `sDays/eDays` = days from it; `phase` = the template phase whose name prefixes the task name as `<phase> — <storey>` (the name
`_writeTemplateSchedule` writes), else the task's WBS parent (summary) name, else none → its rows go to `Unsequenced` (`TASK_PHASE_UNKNOWN`); `phaseSeq` from the template, else by earliest start
(note `PHASE_SEQ_FROM_START`). No active schedule → null → `NO_4D_SCHEDULE` (as today). Callers: `find_erp_push.js _pushToErp` and `proj_order_state.js generate` pass it (`A.db`, `window._4dTemplate`);
their priced rows gain `guids` (both already start from a guid set). Phase dates in the order = the Gantt's own dates.
**Witness W-FOLD-SCHEDULE-FROM-DB (`capex/tests/witness_fold_schedule_from_db.js`; issue: "the viewer's push ignores the programme on screen"):** run the shipped pipeline on HHS (`cache_4d_run.build`
with a read-only `onDb` hook, no second harness) → `scheduleFromDb(db, template)` == the run's own `res.tasks` (same ids, phase, storey, guid sets, day offsets); then the fold over it gives phase
dates == the DB's task dates. Negative: a DB with no schedule → null. Break `BREAK=1` (drop one task_elements row) must FAIL. Regression: `witness_s9_pure`, `witness_civil_find_erp_push`, W-FOLD-ONE-SCHEDULE.

### 5.8 No FREE `Filter` — legacy lookups by DataRow KEY fields (2026-11-03, agent) — SPEC
**Issue:** iDempiere refuses a FREE `Filter` web-service parameter (`MWebServicePara.beforeSave :114-121`, IDEMPIERE-1784); a properly made 2Pack of our types failed with it. Our pilot types were inserted by SQL (rule skipped), and the push + CAPEX tools sent
`Filter` strings. **Mechanism (read from source, not guessed):** `ModelADServiceImpl.validateParameter :307-338` (a Filter may only be Constant; a type with no Filter param refuses a sent Filter: "invalid parameter Filter"); `queryData :1470-1500` (each DataRow
field whitelisted as `WS_WebServiceFieldInput` becomes `col LIKE ?` for CHAR columns, `col=?` otherwise; a non-whitelisted column → "input column X not allowed" `:1494-1498`).
**Design (one owner):** `viewer/erp_rest_push.js` `queryRows(base, login, type, table, keys)` — DataRow keys on the wire, then an EXACT post-filter (ids as numbers, else strings) because CHAR keys are LIKE (`_`/`%` wildcards; a literal `\` is doubled), logging
`§ERP_REST_LIKE_EXTRA table=… dropped=N`; any non-200 / non-`WindowTabData` reply THROWS (`§ERP_REST_QUERY_ERR` — a legacy fault must never read as "0 rows", which would create a duplicate). `findOrCreate` and every capex tool (`shed_twin`, `phase_progress`, `pa_probe`) use it;
`adapters.loadLegacy` simply stops sending `Filter:''`. Pilot types: Filter param → Constant `1=1`; key columns → `WS_WebServiceFieldInput` (`capex/tools/ws_capex_querykeys.sql`, UPDATE/INSERT only, idempotent; the three create-scripts now insert Filter as `C`/`1=1`).
**Keys:** C_Project `Value` (+`C_Project_ID` for reads) · C_ProjectPhase `C_Project_ID`+`Name` (+`C_ProjectPhase_ID`) · C_ProjectTask `C_ProjectPhase_ID`+`Name` · C_ProjectLine `C_ProjectPhase_ID`+`M_Product_ID` (+`C_ProjectTask_ID` when the line has a task) · M_Product `Value` · M_Product_Category `Name` · C_UOM `X12DE355` · PA_Goal `Name`+`PA_Goal_ID`.
**Mock (`tests/mock_adinterface_server.js`)** now enforces legacy's two rules (request Filter → HTTP 500 "invalid parameter Filter"; non-whitelisted query DataRow column → 500 "input column X not allowed") and binds string keys with LIKE semantics. Witness claims (each can FAIL): old client vs new mock FAILs (6 mismatches);
`§POC_LIKE_EXTRA` (`BREAK=1` → FAIL); twin end-of-chain `created +0` and `§SHED_TWIN_VERDICT PASS`. Not touched: `BIMOOTB_*` types (the plugin, other session — its `BIMOOTB_Query_<Table>` types must whitelist the same keys to be read with `BRIDGE_SVC=bimootb`); `erp/tests/witness_ws_*.js` (send Filter to other types, out of capex/viewer scope).

### 5.9 Money tiles: SUM then round once; W-FIND-PUSH-4D stub follows #2557 (2026-11-03) — spec + result; logs read
**Defect (found by `pa_probe` once the pilot held 3+ USD projects):** `tiles.js sumBy` rounded each project to the cent and then added (`cents()` per row) — the tile's own formula
(`SUM(C_Project.PlannedAmt) per C_Currency_ID`) and legacy's PA measure add first and round once: `legacy=14989562.481 tile=14989562.49`, later `19985883.288` vs `19985883.30`. Both twin sides run
`tiles.js`, so the twin could not see it; only the legacy measure (R0) could. **Fix (SQLite side, CARDINAL):** `sumBy` adds each currency's values exactly (the value's own decimal text as a BigInt
at 1e-9) and rounds half away from zero once. Every money tile goes through `sumBy` (approved, actual, committed, invoiced, the % of plan and spent denominators).
**Witness W-TILES-SUM-ONCE** (`capex/tests/witness_tiles_sum_once.js`, node): main's tiles.js FAIL 3/4 (3 × 0.004 → 0 instead of 1 cent; the pilot-shape sum 1498896249 instead of 1498896248;
-0.005 → 0) → PASS 4/4; `BREAK=1` → FAIL. **End of chain:** `pa_probe` `§PA_PROBE_VERDICT PASS judged=4 fail=0` (legacy 19985883.288 == tile 19985883.29); HHS twin
`§SHED_TWIN_VERDICT PASS tiles(match=16 diff=0 inconclusive=3) rows(match=302 diff=0)` created +0.
**Instrument fix:** `capex/tests/witness_find_push_4d.js` stubbed `ProjOrderState.openStore`; #2557 moved the push to `ProjOrderState.shared()`, so the push fell through to a real fetch
(`§PROJ_PUSH_DBERR not used`) and the witness read an empty store (FAIL 3/5 — reported in §5.8 as "fails on main"). Stub now provides `shared/persistShared` → PASS 5/5 on main + this change.

2nd push `created` all 0, no 2nd dialog · no target `§PROJ_PUSH_LEGACY skip=no-target`, fetchCalls=0 · wrong password `Error login - User invalid`, fetchCalls=1, 0 rows created, status text shows the failure, next push asks again (asks=2), cancelled dialog → `skip=no-credentials` · password absent from 939 captured lines (real and wrong).
`§W_VPL_VERDICT PASS judged=24 fail=0 project=HHS_VIEWER_PUSH_261103050809 legacyId=1000013`. Break: `BREAK=1` → `§W_VPL_ASSERT FAIL LEGACY == FOLD: phase count legacy=6 fold=6` · `§W_VPL_VERDICT FAIL judged=24 fail=1 … legacyId=1000014 (BREAK=1 — must be FAIL)`.
Pilot projects left in place (no deletes): 1000011, 1000012 (early iterations, no dates), 1000013 (the PASS run), 1000014 (the BREAK run). Each run adds one.

### 5.11 The viewer's Project-Order store opens with the ERP's boot overlays (2026-11-03) — spec + result; logs read
**Found by the headless browser check** (bim-compiler `prompts/BIM_OOTB_LEGACY_IDEMPIERE_INTEGRATION.md §8.8`, software GL, test instance): the viewer's real push from `/bimootb` was refused by legacy —
`§ERP_REST_CREATE_ERR M_Product: … null value in column "c_taxcategory_id"`. Cause: `ProjOrderState.openStore` (`viewer/proj_order_state.js:42-53`) opened the seed / OPFS store WITHOUT the ERP's own boot
overlays, which every other opener applies (`erp/idempiere.html:1155-1156`, `erp/ws_kernel.js:19-20`, `capex/tools/fold_demo.js:19`) — so the viewer's ERP data was not the ERP app's (TWIN rule).
**Fix:** `openStore` applies `AdDocValueOverlay` + `AdTaxCatOverlay` (globals in the browser, `require` in node; idempotent; logged `§S9-STORE overlays src=…`); `viewer.html` loads
`../erp/ad_docvalue_wf.js`, `ad_docvalue_overlay.js`, `ad_taxcat_overlay.js` (the ERP's order, `erp/idempiere.html:505`); sw precache + bump.
**Witness W-VIEWER-STORE-OVERLAYS** (`capex/tests/witness_viewer_store_overlays.js`, node): raw seed 41 products without a tax category (a default exists) → the store 0; `BREAK=1` (overlays hidden) → 41 → FAIL;
main's `proj_order_state.js` → 41. **End of chain (browser, test instance):** the same push then `§PROJ_PUSH_LEGACY base=http://localhost:8098 … projectId=1000003`; legacy == local fold (7/10/16,
PlannedAmt 383827.36788); the real password dialog shown prefilled (user/client/role/org/warehouse read-only, only the password typed), removed after submit, password in 0 of 697 log lines.

### 5.12 SPEC — the dashboard the way Sysnova runs theirs: a standalone app beside iDempiere (2026-11-03; written before code) — ⛔ SUPERSEDED by §5.13 (owner: ground up, same SQLite WASM framework)
**Owner:** *"i was originally proposing that the plugin is only for the BIM to ERP Project Order"* · *"If u already could guess from the info what they are using we be copying that"*. So the dashboard is
NOT in `org.idempiere.bimootb` and NOT a ZK gadget; it copies the ARCHITECTURE Sysnova's own dashboard shows (the framework is not knowable from what we have, and the page stays vanilla).
**Evidence (screenshot `~/Projects/SYSNOVA/SYSNOVADashboardScreenshot.png`, 2026-09-02 screen share; notes `BIMSYSNOVA_Project.md` §Target dashboard):** URL `http://172.16.9.25:8000/v2/#/dashboard` = a
separate web app on its own port (not iDempiere's `/webui`), plain HTTP on the LAN; `/v2/` + sidebar link "← Classic dashboard (v1)" = two versions served by one app; `#/dashboard` = a single-page app with
hash routes, sidebar Dashboard · Projects · Budget & Cost · Procurement Risks · Approval Pending (514) · Handover; footer "PMO Analytics — Live data from iDempiere" = it reads iDempiere live, so a server
part on that port reads iDempiere (a page alone could not without CORS) — HOW (database or web services) is NOT known (Q-S2). Port 8000 = the default of Python dev servers / Laravel `artisan serve`: a hint, not a fact.
**Build (bim-ootb, no framework, no new dependency):**
1. `capex/server.js` (node `http`): serves `capex/` at `/v2/` (index.html), hash routes `#/dashboard`, `#/projects`, `#/budget`, `#/procurement`, `#/approvals`, `#/handover` — the same six modules; a module with
   no stated definition shows "definition pending (client)" (§6 Q6), never a made-up table. Port from env (default 8000 like theirs; refuses to start if taken, never kills a process).
2. `GET /api/legacy` — the server reads iDempiere through its stock web services with a dedicated READ-ONLY service account given by env (`LEGACY_BASE`, `LEGACY_USER`, `LEGACY_PASS`, client/role/org):
   the SAME `adapters.loadLegacy` the snapshot tool uses (one owner; `capex/tools/legacy_snapshot.js` becomes a thin caller). Short cache (env seconds), `§DASH_SERVE` logs (row counts, ms) — the password
   never in a response, a log line or the page. No account configured → HTTP 503 `{"error":"no legacy source"}` and the page shows "no data source" (no zeros).
3. Page: a third source `fromServer('/api/legacy')` in `capex/adapters.js` (the page picks it when served by `server.js`); Source A (our SQLite twin) unchanged; `tiles.js` unchanged — one compute.
4. The iDempiere side needs only the read-only account + its read types (the CAPEX read types, Filter Constant since §5.8): later shipped as the DASHBOARD's own setup pack (iDempiere convention), never
   inside the BIM plugin. POC: the local pilot already holds them.
**Witness W-DASH-SERVE (node, local pilot):** `/api/legacy` row counts == `legacy_snapshot.js` (projects/phases/tasks/lines …); the tiles computed from the API payload == the tiles `shed_twin.js` computes
from legacy for HHS and SHED (`?project=`); `/v2/` serves index.html and the six routes; no account → 503 + "no data source"; the password string in no response body/header/log; a second server on a
taken port refuses to start. Break: `BREAK=1` drops one project from the payload → the count compare FAILs.
**Open (owner → Sysnova PM, §6 Q-S1..Q-S6):** their app's source, live or prototype, how it reads iDempiere, how it signs in, the tile definitions, where it runs.

### 5.13 SPEC — the CAPEX dashboard from the ground up, on our SQLite WASM framework (2026-11-03; written before code; supersedes §5.12)
**Owner:** *"just act as if we are doing it afresh, as it is better we do from the ground up and i prefer to be same SQLite WASM framework"* — and earlier: the plugin is only BIM → Project Order; legacy
defines dashboards as PA data ("our side is a true twin and idempiere side retains its learning curve"). Whatever Sysnova runs is not copied; their answers (§6 Q-S1..Q-S6) only feed the DEFINITIONS.
**Measured before writing:** the ERP kernel already converts iDempiere SQL to SQLite at one choke point (`erp/ad_callout.js:264 convertSql`, via `makeDB().query`); the seed holds `pa_measure` 4 /
`pa_goal` 4 / `pa_dashboardcontent` 3 and the boot patch adds `pa_measurecalc`, `pa_colorschema`, `ad_chart` (`erp/patches/build_ad_seed_patch.sh:40`); the dictionary already has the windows
Performance Measure, Performance Measure Calculation, Performance Goal, Performance Color Schema, Dashboard Content (rendered by the ONE generic AD engine — to be measured, not assumed).
**Layers (each one owner, AD-LAYER LAW — no dashboard-specific window code):**
- **L1 Data = the SQLite WASM ERP DB in the browser** (same schema as iDempiere). Local-first: the twin's own rows (BIM folds, documents) — and, for a site that runs iDempiere, a DOWN copy of its
  project tables (C_Project, C_ProjectPhase, C_ProjectTask, C_ProjectLine, C_Phase, C_Currency, AD_Org, AD_WF_Activity) by `*_UU`, idempotent, read-only on their side (§14 build order 1).
- **L2 Definitions = PA rows** (PA_MeasureCalc SQL, PA_Measure, PA_Goal, PA_ColorSchema, PA_DashboardContent) — THE SAME rows legacy runs (R0 proved legacy computes a CAPEX tile from them, no Java).
  Rows ship as data: our boot overlay / patch on our side, a migration script or 2Pack on theirs (LEGACY CONVENTION) — one definition, two engines.
- **L3 Engine = a SQLite port of MMeasure / MMeasureCalc / MGoal / MColorSchema** (`erp/pa_engine.js`, cited file:line from `~/idempiere-dev-setup/idempiere/org.adempiere.base`): SelectClause + WhereClause
  through `convertSql`, the role's access restriction as legacy adds it (`MMeasureCalc.java:612`), performance = actual ÷ target, colour from the schema marks; legacy's three behaviours twinned as decided
  in §5.5 (no compute on create; performance against the previously committed target; a failed SQL = 0 + `§PA_MEASURE_ERROR`, judged INCONCLUSIVE by the witness).
- **L4 Renderer = a home dashboard driven by PA_DashboardContent rows** in our ERP app: each content row → its goal/measure → value + colour + the formula text; no per-tile code. The visual
  vocabulary is the one the client asked for (the §3 tiles: counts, sums per currency, health ring, stage pipeline, physical vs spent, schedule / budget buckets) built as GENERIC tile kinds a row picks.
- **L5 Editor = the generic AD windows above**, in our ERP UI; an edit → re-compute → re-render → re-twin. "Without coding or AI" is then literally true.
- **Twin:** the same PA rows on both sides; legacy `PA_Goal.MeasureActual` (WS read) vs our engine's value, per goal, to the cent (`capex/tools/pa_probe.js` grows into it).
- **The `capex/` page + `tiles.js` (today's build) become the ORACLE** during the move: every PA-defined tile must equal its `tiles.js` value on the same data; when all match, `tiles.js`'s hand formulas
  are deleted (deletion budget stated). What PA cannot express (per-project EVM health, average delay) is MEASURED first: either one MeasureCalc SQL per bucket that runs in both dialects, or it stays a
  named generic tile kind in L4 — never a hidden special case.
**Build order (each its own spec line + witness before code):** G1 the PA engine on the seed + the R0 measure (Approved Budget USD) == legacy's goal on the pilot · G2 the §3 count/sum tiles as PA rows,
each == `tiles.js` · G3 the renderer from PA_DashboardContent · G4 the bucket tiles (health, schedule, budget status) — PA SQL or named tile kinds · G5 the editor windows X of N (the five above) ·
G6 the DOWN copy for a real site (needs their read-only account — §6 Q-S3/Q-S4).
**Witnesses (named before code):** W-PA-ENGINE (G1: same rows → same value as legacy, cent-exact; broken SQL → 0 + INCONCLUSIVE; BREAK=1 FAIL) · W-PA-TILES-ORACLE (G2/G4: each PA tile == `tiles.js`) ·
W-PA-RENDER (G3: the rendered value of every content row == its engine value, read from the DOM headlessly) · W-PA-EDITOR (G5: X of 5 windows open, edit a target → the tile moves on both sides).

### 5.14 SPEC — R2 / G1: `erp/pa_engine.js`, the PA engine in the SQLite kernel (2026-11-03; written before code; owner: "write the R2 spec first")
**Owner's frame (2026-11-03):** *in legacy the dashboard is the ZK PA dashboard — same L&F, users keep their learning curve; in our twin it is the same L&F, resting on the SQLite-WASM embedded DB with NO legacy WS.*
So the engine has ONE job: given the same PA rows and the same project data, produce the same value, performance and colour as legacy — in the browser, reading only the local SQLite DB. No network, no WS, no legacy.
**Issue this proves/disproves:** *"a PA-defined tile gives a different number/colour on the twin than on legacy"* (CARDINAL RULE). Today nothing in SQLite runs PA rows (measured: only `erp/team_plan.js` mentions PA; `erp/ad_charts.js` is a separate hand-written SQL→canvas panel, not PA).

**Legacy behaviour to port (source `~/idempiere-dev-setup/idempiere/org.adempiere.base/src/org/compiere/model/`; every rule below is cited and gets a `§PA_*` log line when it fires):**
| # | Rule | Legacy source | Twin implementation |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | Calculated measure SQL = `SelectClause + " " + WhereClause`; date filter ONLY when `DateColumn` set AND data type `T` (QtyAmountInTime) AND scope ≠ Total; `@ctx@` tokens parsed from context | `MMeasureCalc.getSqlPI` :153-189 | same string build; trunc DD/Y/Q/MM/D → SQLite `strftime`-based equivalent; token parse via the kernel's context |
| E2 | Role restriction appended to the SQL (client/org access) | `MMeasureCalc.addRestrictions` → `MRole.addAccessSQL` (:612) | `erp/ad_access.js` (the kernel's role access) — ⚠ to MEASURE: does it yield the same AD_Client/AD_Org predicate on the table alias as `addAccessSQL`? Witness compares, never assumes |
| E3 | Which role: goal's `AD_Role_ID`, else the user's first role for the goal org, else default role ("could result in wrong data") | `MMeasure.updateCalculatedGoals` :498-542 | same order; the default-role fallback logs `§PA_ROLE_FALLBACK` |
| E4 | `getSQLValueBD` → first column of first row as BigDecimal; SQL error or no row → null → **0** (error only logged) | `DB.java:1393-1404`, `MMeasure.java:530-535` | `BigDecimal` via `erp/bigdecimal.js` (never JS float — money tiles are SUM-then-round, §5.9); null→0 AND `§PA_MEASURE_ERROR sql=… err=…` / `§PA_MEASURE_NULL` so the witness can tell "0 because empty/failed" from "0 because zero" |
| E5 | `setMeasureActual`: stores actual, stamps `DateLastRun`, recomputes performance | `MGoal.java:416-425` | same |
| E6 | Performance = actual ÷ target, 6 dp HALF_UP; **0 when target is 0** | `MGoal.setGoalPerformance` :428-441 | BigDecimal divide, scale 6, HALF_UP |
| E7 | Percent = `(performance × 100).intValue()` (truncate toward zero) | `MGoal.getPercent` :453-457 | trunc, not round |
| E8 | Colour: **white when target is 0**, else `MColorSchema.getColor(schema, percent)` | `MGoal.getColor` :463-475 | same; returns the `AD_PrintColor` of the schema (hex from `ad_printcolor`), never an invented palette |
| E9 | Schema marks: `percent <= mark1 or mark2==0` → colour1; `<= mark2 or mark3==0` → colour2; `<= mark3 or mark4==0` → colour3; else colour4; colour id 0 falls back 3→2→1; none → black | `MColorSchema.getColor` :231-262 | line-for-line |
| E10 | WHEN a goal computes: (a) on save of a NEW goal, or changed `MeasureTarget`/`MeasureScope` (`updateGoal(true)`); (b) on dashboard render when `DateLastRun` null or older than the interval (`ZK_DASHBOARD_PERFORMANCE_REFRESH_INTERVAL`, default 1,800,000 ms) (`updateGoal(false)`). A plain row insert computes nothing | `MGoal.afterSave` :594-603, `MGoal.updateGoal` :383-415, `WPAWidget.java:54` | the engine exposes `updateGoal(force)` with the same two triggers; (a) is wired as the goal's `afterSave` in the layered registry `erp/ad_modelval.js` (`MODEL['PA_Goal']`, owner 'core') exactly as Java's model class; (b) is called by the renderer (G3). SysConfig value read from `ad_sysconfig`, not hard-coded |
| E11 | Legacy's no-trx re-read quirk (goal inside its create transaction is invisible → a NEW goal is NOT computed on create; performance uses the PREVIOUS committed target) | §5.4 (a)(b), `MGoal.getMeasureGoals` :150-163 | **Twinned, not "fixed"** (CARDINAL RULE: a difference is fixed on the SQLite side). The engine reads goals through the same committed view: the kernel's `trx`/handle isolation must reproduce "not visible inside own transaction" — to MEASURE first (does `model_layer.js` give an uncommitted-row-invisible read?); if it does not, the quirk is emulated explicitly and named `§PA_QUIRK_NOCOMPUTE_ON_CREATE` |
| E12 | Measure types: C (Calculated) is G1; R (Ratio), Q (Request), P (Project), M (Manual), A (Achievements), U (UserDefined) are NOT in G1 | `MMeasure.updateGoals` :407-430 | unsupported type → `§PA_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED` + INCONCLUSIVE, never silently 0 (breaking legacy would be the silent 0; here we are only declining to judge) |

**The PRIME-RULE tension (decided here, stated honestly):** legacy shows 0 for a failed/empty measure (E4); this lane's rule says an empty population prints "no data". The ENGINE value is the legacy value (0) so the twin matches; the engine ALSO returns `state: 'OK' | 'EMPTY' | 'ERROR'` beside it, and the witness prints INCONCLUSIVE (never PASS) for EMPTY/ERROR. How the renderer (G3) displays an `EMPTY`/`ERROR` goal is a presentation decision for G3 — it must not change the stored `MeasureActual`. (No divergence on stored values; the extra `state` is a read-out, not a different result.)

**Where the legacy SQL meets SQLite (one choke point, no per-measure patch):** `erp/ad_callout.js:264 convertSql` (via `makeDB().query`) converts iDempiere/Postgres SQL to SQLite. PA `SelectClause`/`WhereClause` are Postgres SQL typed in the legacy window; the engine hands them to `convertSql` unchanged. A construct `convertSql` cannot translate → the engine reports it as `§PA_MEASURE_ERROR … untranslatable=<token>` (a finding to fix in `convertSql`, once, for every consumer — AD-LAYER LAW rule 5), never rewrites the PA row. G1 measures how many of the legacy pilot's 7 `PA_MeasureCalc` rows (6 stock + R0) convert and run (X of N).

**Module shape (one owner, nothing dashboard-specific):** `erp/pa_engine.js`, exports `calcMeasure(db, measureId, goalCtx)`, `updateGoal(db, goalId, {force})`, `performance(goal)`, `percent(goal)`, `colorFor(db, goal)`. Pure over the SQLite handle; no DOM, no `capex/` import, no fetch. `capex/` and the ERP home (G3) are consumers. Deletion budget: G1 adds the module and removes nothing (the `tiles.js` formulas are the ORACLE until G2/G3 match; their deletion is G3's budget, stated there).

**Witness W-PA-ENGINE (`erp/tests/witness_pa_engine.js`, headless Node + `sql.js`/the kernel's own SQLite — no browser, no screenshot; `§`-lines are the evidence):**
1. **Oracle A — legacy (pilot, LOCAL, read-only WS query, owner go already given for the pilot):** the R0 measure over the HHS/SHED fold rows loaded into the SQLite DB == pilot `PA_Goal.MeasureActual` for "CAPEX Approved Budget (USD)" **to the cent on the SAME project rows** (via `legacy_snapshot.js`). ⚠ known: pilot holds 3-dp PlannedAmt (§5.8 `pa_probe` note: `legacy=19985883.288` vs tile cent-rounded) — the engine must NOT round (legacy returns the raw sum); the comparison is raw-sum == raw-sum, and the tile's cent rounding stays tiles.js's concern.
2. **Oracle B — `tiles.js`:** the same value == `tiles.js` approved_budget USD (S population, status=all) after the money rule §5.9.
3. **Oracle C — pure-function vectors from the Java source** (no pilot needed, so the witness also runs offline = the twin's own CI): E6 target 0 → 0; E7 truncation (0.0999 → 9); E8 white at target 0; E9 boundary cases (percent == mark1, == mark2, mark2==0, colour id 0 fallbacks) against the stock "Red (50) - Yellow (100) - Green" schema (mark1 50 / mark2 100 / mark3 9999) — every vector computed from the Java rule, listed in the log with its expected value.
4. **Negative (each must FAIL / be named):** a measure with broken SQL → `§PA_MEASURE_ERROR`, value 0, state ERROR, verdict **INCONCLUSIVE** for that tile; an empty population (no C_Project rows) → state EMPTY, INCONCLUSIVE (not PASS-with-0); unsupported measure type → INCONCLUSIVE; a goal inserted by plain SQL is NOT computed until E10-trigger (proves E10/E11, would print `§PA_NOT_COMPUTED`).
5. **Vacuity guard:** verdict prints `INCONCLUSIVE` when judged=0 (nothing compared) — never PASS.
6. **Break switch:** `BREAK=1` perturbs the engine's actual by +1 cent → oracle A, B must FAIL.
`§` lines: `§PA_ENGINE_LOAD measures=N goals=N schemas=N`, `§PA_CALC measure=<name> sql=<final sql> actual=<BigDecimal> state=<OK|EMPTY|ERROR>`, `§PA_PERF goal=<name> actual target performance percent color`, `§PA_ENGINE_X_OF_N convertible=X of N`, `§PA_ENGINE_VERDICT PASS|FAIL|INCONCLUSIVE judged=… fail=… inconclusive=…`.

**Embedded in legacy vs on the twin — the same rows, two engines (answers the owner's L&F point):** the PA rows are the single definition. Legacy ZK renders them as its stock PA dashboard (`DPPerformance` → `WPAWidget`, `org.adempiere.ui.zk/.../webui/dashboard/`) — users keep their learning curve; nothing of ours is in their ZK. The twin renders the SAME rows (G3) with the same vocabulary a ZK user sees (goal name, value, % of target, colour from the schema, formula text) in OUR ERP UI. The `capex/` page, if kept, is a third renderer of the same rows (an optional PA_DashboardContent `HTML` iframe card on the legacy side was read in `DashboardController.java:~788` but is UNTESTED and NOT part of this spec).

**Release model (owner, 2026-11-03: "so we maintain one, deploy twin or legacy in the final release"):** ONE maintained artefact = the PA rows (+ the engine's tests). Release target is a choice, not a fork: **legacy** = the rows shipped as an iDempiere migration script / 2Pack (stock ZK renders and computes); **twin** = the same rows in the SQLite-WASM ERP DB (boot overlay/patch), `erp/pa_engine.js` computes. A row edited once goes to both; the twin-vs-legacy parity run is the proof that choosing either gives the same numbers. No dashboard code is forked per target.
**Not in G1 (named, so they are not silently dropped):** the renderer and tile kinds (G3) · tiles as PA rows (G2) · per-project health/schedule/budget buckets, which a single `SELECT SUM/COUNT` cannot express (G4 — decided there after measuring) · the editor (G5) · `AD_Chart` · Ratio/Request/Project/Manual/Achievement measures · the DOWN copy (G6).
**Open measurements before code (each answered by a `§` line, not a guess):** (1) role-access predicate parity E2 · (2) transaction-isolation behaviour E11 · (3) `convertSql` X-of-N on the 7 pilot MeasureCalcs · (4) whether `ad_printcolor` holds the schema's four colours in the seed (colour result needs real rows).
**Status:** built — see §5.15.

### 5.15 RESULT — R2/G1 built (2026-11-03); logs read (scratch, not committed)
**Owner, same day:** *"yes of course the PA model has to be the plugin. Do it as migration script be less hassle and intuitive to devs. Proceed"* → the legacy-side PA definition ships as an iDempiere
migration script (data only, LEGACY CONVENTION) — and the twin applies **the same file** (`erp/migration_apply.js`), so there is ONE artefact for both release targets.
**The four measurements (before code):** (1) E2 — `erp/ad_access.js` has NO row-level SQL predicate (no `addAccessSQL` twin; it gates windows/processes only) → the engine ports the client+org part
of `MRole.addAccessSQL` :2074-2130 itself; table / record / dependent access and summary-org expansion are NOT ported and log `§PA_ACCESS_PARTIAL` / `§PA_ACCESS_SUMMARY_ORG` (role 102 holds 1 summary
org). (2) E11 — `ML.Trx.q` IS the committed host reader (`model_layer.js:80-82`); the pending overlay lives only in `find/get` → reading goals through `trx.q` reproduces legacy's no-trx re-read
WITHOUT emulation (Q1/Q3 below). (3) `convertSql` X of N: **5 of 7** PA_MeasureCalc rows run on the twin (6 stock + R0); fails: #100 "Invoiced Net Revenue" (`RV_C_Invoice ⋈ RV_C_InvoiceLine` — sql.js
throws with NO message; each view alone reads 20 / 56 rows) and #105 "Open Invoice Amount" (`C_Invoice_v` view absent from the twin). (4) colours: `ad_printcolor` 23 rows and
`ZK_DASHBOARD_PERFORMANCE_REFRESH_INTERVAL`=1800000 are in seed+patch. Also found: PostgreSQL `TO_TIMESTAMP` (what `DB_PostgreSQL.TO_DATE` :446-468 emits for PA date filters) had no SQLite port.
**Built (+ lines, nothing superseded yet — `tiles.js` stays the oracle until G3, whose PR states the deletion budget):** `erp/pa_engine.js` (E1–E12 as tabled in §5.14, cited inline) ·
`erp/migration_apply.js` (applies a legacy migration script through `convertSql`; `register_migration_script` → `§MIG_REGISTER_SKIPPED`: the twin has no AD_MigrationScript table) ·
`erp/callouts/sqlfn.js` `to_timestamp(text, fmt)` — only the two formats legacy writes, anything else → NULL · `erp/tests/witness_pa_engine.js`.
**W-PA-ENGINE (`NODE_PATH=<bim-ootb>/node_modules node erp/tests/witness_pa_engine.js [--offline]`):**
- M: `§MIG_APPLY file=202611031900_CAPEX-R0.sql statements=3 ok=2 failed=0 skipped=1` — the same script legacy applied, unchanged.
- V: 21 vectors from the Java rules PASS (performance 6 dp HALF_UP / target 0 → 0; percent truncates toward zero; schema 100 boundaries 50/51/100/101/9999/10000/−5; colour-id fallback; white at target 0;
  main-table synonym; access predicate `… AND C_Project.AD_Client_ID IN (0,11) AND C_Project.AD_Org_ID IN (0,11,12,50000,…)`).

### 5.20 SPEC — G4: the bucket tiles (8 health, 11 schedule, 12 budget status) as PA rows (2026-11-03, owner "Proceed"; written before code)
**Issue proved/disproved:** *"the per-project classification tiles need code: they cannot be one PA_MeasureCalc SQL that legacy and the twin both run and that equals tiles.js"*.
**Measured first:** (a) each bucket is a COUNT over C_Project with correlated sub-selects on C_ProjectPhase — one scalar, so a Calculated measure (type C / S). (b) "today": legacy converts every statement
(SYSDATE → statement_timestamp(), ConvertMap_PostgreSQL :71) and the pilot holds `adempiere.trunc(timestamp[tz])` + `adempiere.daysbetween(ts, ts) = CAST(d1 AS DATE) - CAST(d2 AS DATE)` (pg_get_functiondef);
the twin has the same ports (callouts/sqlfn.js `trunc`, `daysbetween`, `statement_timestamp` on `RUNTIME.now`) and the converter (`ad_callout.js convertSql`). ⚠ `goal_engine.evaluate` runs the measure SQL
RAW — legacy's CPreparedStatement converts it — so evaluate gains the kernel converter (the ONE choke point; a no-op for SQL without SYSDATE/DUAL/…). (c) pilot buckets today (tiles.js over the WS read):
health red 3 / none 6 · schedule on-time 2, at-risk 3, no-schedule 4, avg delay 189 d · budget over 1, not-started 8, overrun USD 318.68; green / amber / delayed / within are 0 at every date tried
(2026-11-03 … 2028-08-01) → those four are INCONCLUSIVE (never judged on a 0 nobody exercised; no fixture data is invented).
**Rules = tiles.js `compute` (:84-135), as SQL on `C_Project p` (A = `p.IsActive='Y' AND p.Processed='N'`):** PV = Σ phase PlannedAmt with `TRUNC(EndDate)<=TRUNC(SYSDATE)` · EV = Σ phase PlannedAmt with
IsComplete='Y' · AC = COALESCE(ProjectBalanceAmt,0) · DATED = a phase with StartDate and EndDate · OVERDUE = a phase with `COALESCE(IsComplete,'N')<>'Y'` and `TRUNC(EndDate)<TRUNC(SYSDATE)`.
Health: none = PV≤0 · green = PV>0, EV≥0.9·PV and (AC≤0 or EV≥0.9·AC) · red = PV>0, EV<0.9·PV and (AC≤0 or EV<0.9·AC) · amber = the rest with PV>0. Schedule: no-schedule = not DATED · at-risk = DATED, PV>0,
EV<0.9·PV · delayed = DATED, not at-risk, OVERDUE · on-time = DATED, neither · average delay = AVG over at-risk + delayed of MAX(daysbetween(TRUNC(SYSDATE), EndDate)) over the overdue phases (0 when
none). Budget: not-started = AC=0 · over = AC≠0 and AC > COALESCE(PlannedAmt,0) · within = the rest · overrun per currency = Σ(AC − COALESCE(PlannedAmt,0)) over "over". Known difference to watch: tiles.js
rounds every amount to the cent before comparing; the SQL compares raw amounts — equal unless a value sits within half a cent of a 0.9 boundary (the witness would show it).
**Rows:** `capex/legacy/migration/iD13/postgresql/202611032200_CAPEX-G4.sql` — 14 PA_MeasureCalc + PA_Measure (IDs 1000023–1000036) + 14 default PA_Goal (1000044–1000057), LEGACY CONVENTION; the twin
gets them as the bundle's next pack `capex_2pack/2Pack_1.0.1.zip` (a released pack is immutable; Incremental2PackActivator applies 1.0.1 after 1.0.0).
**Witness:** W-PA-TILES-ORACLE grows to the G4 measures: A legacy goal == twin engine (real today, the same DOWN-copied rows) == tiles.js; B the twin engine == tiles.js at shifted dates (`RUNTIME.now`)
2026-12-10 / 2027-06-01 / 2028-08-01 over the same rows (exercises the date rules legacy cannot be moved to); zero-on-every-side buckets INCONCLUSIVE; BREAK=1 FAIL. W-PA-RENDER: 27 CAPEX indicators.

### 5.22 SPEC — G5: the editor = the five stock windows, and what an edit does (2026-11-03, owner "proceed"; written before code)
**Denominator (dictionary, the same on the twin seed and the pilot):** Performance Goal (AD_Window 212, tabs Goal + Restriction) · Performance Measure Calculation (213) · Performance Measure (215) ·
Performance Color Schema (364) · Dashboard Content (50007) — N = 5. No CAPEX-specific window, field or code (AD-LAYER LAW): "the editor" is the generic AD engine over these rows.
**What legacy does on an edit (read in source, before any claim):** saving a goal whose MeasureTarget / MeasureScope changed recomputes it (`MGoal.afterSave` :594-603 → `updateGoal(true)`), reading the goals
WITHOUT the save's transaction — so performance is scored against the PREVIOUS committed target (§5.4 b) — and stores MeasureActual / GoalPerformance / DateLastRun on the row the window shows. The Home
gadget then draws the STORED performance (`WPerformanceIndicator.init` → `getPercent()`), recomputing only when DateLastRun is older than the refresh interval (`MGoal.getUserGoals` → `updateGoal(false)`).
**Expected twin gap (to be shown RED first, not assumed):** the browser twin registers no PA_Goal model class (`GoalEngine.installModel` is only called by node witnesses) and its gadget computes live
(`dashboard_gadgets.performance` → `evaluate`) — so after the same edit the window would show the old stored values and the Home a live percent legacy does not show.
**Witness W-PA-EDITOR (`capex/tests/witness_pa_editor.js`, headless CPU Chromium, real erp/idempiere.html, the CAPEX bundle installed as in W-PA-RENDER):** E1 X of 5 windows open through the menu as
GardenAdmin with grid rows == the role-visible table rows, the CAPEX rows among them (212/213/215) · E2 in Performance Goal, the goal "CAPEX Approved Budget (USD)" gets MeasureTarget 1000 through the
form + Save · E3 the saved row (as the window re-reads it) carries legacy's recompute: MeasureActual = the measure SQL, GoalPerformance = actual ÷ the PREVIOUS target (0 → 0), DateLastRun set ·
E4 the Home indicator for that goal == legacy's title rule on the stored row ("<Name>: <percent>%") · L legacy oracle on the pilot: the same edit through stock update_data (a model save, as the window

### 5.26 SPEC — G6b: the DOWN copy from legacy (2026-11-04, owner "proceed" on the small job; written before code)
**Issue:** "the twin's Home cannot equal a legacy site's own Home: nothing brings that site's rows into the twin" (§5.25 `§G6A_GAP`: Total 5 vs 12, Approved USD 4,996,921 vs 39,971,166, Approval Pending 10 vs 261).
**Decided + why (the three the RESUME left open):**
- **(b) Path = the small job (owner).** `capex/tools/legacy_snapshot.js` (exists, same stock WS reads) writes `capex/site_copy.json` beside the app; the browser only `fetch`es that file (`cache:'no-store'`, which erp/sw.js passes through, sw.js:431). No CORS, no credential in the browser, no iDempiere config. Git-ignored, so the public site has none (404 → `§DASH_SYNC none`, nothing changes). A file of its own, not `capex/legacy_snapshot.json`: that one is the `capex/` page's dev input, and a stale dev file must not silently mirror a twin.
- **(a) Landing = applied at boot into main by the capex twin plugin, NOT the signed op-log.** Measured by reading: a CRUD op runs the model layer (MProject rules, rollups: no longer a copy), signs and replicates to a Team, and scales as one op per row on every sync. The copy is the site's truth, not this twin's work. Same mechanism as the proven `§BIM_OVERLAY` (rebuilt from seed + overlays every boot, outside the Team base hash), reached by the one extension enabler (AD-LAYER LAW 6): `activate(ctx)`, `ctx.db`, after the overlays (idempiere.html:1146); no `erp/` file changes (EXTENSION-LAYER LAW).
- **(c) Role = what the pilot holds:** the 8 `Query*` types, role 102 `isreadwrite=N`, all orgs. No type added. GardenAdmin is still a full USER: a real read-only user is Sysnova's account (§6 Q-S3/Q-S4).
**Corrected from the RESUME draft, "by `*_UU`":** the key is the PK. Every FK in these tables is a PK (phases → C_Project_ID), and a mirror ends with exactly legacy's rows either way: a twin row with the same UU under another PK is not in the snapshot, so it goes. A same-PK row with another UU is counted (`uuDiffer`), legacy wins.
**Semantics (`capex/plugin/legacy_copy.mjs`, pure, node + browser):** per table, `§DASH_SYNC table=… read=N inserted=N updated=N unchanged=N deleted=N failed=N uuDiffer=N`. MIRROR for C_Project, C_ProjectPhase, C_ProjectTask, C_ProjectLine, AD_WF_Activity: rows of the snapshot's client not in it are DELETED from main (Hospital #990000, and a local BIM fold still in the OPFS store: hidden while a copy is active, it returns when the copy goes; pushed to legacy, it arrives by the next copy). UPSERT only for AD_Org, C_Phase, C_Currency (reference data, no delete). An UNAVAILABLE entity or a mirror table read as 0 rows is SKIPPED, never mirrored to empty. Only columns the twin has; a column the WS type does not whitelist stays as it is. Heap and ms logged (PERF BUDGET).
**Named, not built:** (1) the copy is whole-file per boot: fine for hundreds of rows, unmeasured at Sysnova's 2,462 projects. (2) Nothing tells the USER the copy is old (a dead cron looks like a quiet site): `§DASH_SYNC age=` logs it only. (3) M_Product and other referenced tables are not copied: a line's product shows blank in the window, no goal reads it. (4) A site whose client is not in the twin needs its tenant first (Sysnova). (5) A twin edit on a copied row shows in the tip over the copy: read-only isn't enforced.
**W-DASH-SYNC `capex/tests/witness_dash_sync.js`:** J the job writes the file; no password in it. N (node, seed + patch) apply twice: the second changes 0; a snapshot with an unavailable table or an empty mirror table changes nothing in it. H real `erp/idempiere.html`: bundle installed, reboot, `§DASH_SYNC` per table; row counts in client 11 == the pilot's (psql); **every CAPEX goal's Home title + goal_engine exact == the pilot's stored PA_Goal for the whole client, none excepted** (0 on both = INCONCLUSIVE). E **a real pilot edit**: SHED's Substructure IsComplete=Y through the existing stock type `BridgeUpdateCProjectPhase` (the RESUME said PlannedAmt: no type whitelists it; adding one is config, so not done), job again, reboot: the Home == legacy's SQL (the §5.25 oracle), and Health Green moves 0 → 1, Health Red 3 → 2 as computed by legacy; restored to N in `finally`, read back; re-sync, Home back. This also gives Green a real pilot row for the first time. `DASH_SYNC_BREAK=1` → expected + 1 → FAIL.

No `erp/` file, no pilot config, no new WS type.

So the witness now makes legacy recompute first.

### 5.27 SPEC — G7: targets as in convention; the guide shows the full HTML page (2026-11-04, owner: *"Yes targets as in convention. if it makes sense in the dashboard. The guide should show and explain the full populated dashboard HTML then publish"*; written before code)
**Issue:** "every CAPEX dial on legacy reads 0% on red whatever the number, so the dial says nothing or says something false" (measured in the pilot's real ZK Home).
**Convention, measured on GardenWorld's 4 stock goals:** MeasureTarget = a plain plan number (1000, 10, 100000); direction = one of the 2 stock schemas: higher is better 100 Red-Yellow-Green (Invoice Revenue), lower is better 101 Green-Yellow-Red (Open Invoices, Service Requests). A target makes the caption a PERCENT and hides the value (`WPerformanceIndicator.init`).
**Where it makes sense (27 → 9 with a target):** (a) Actual / Committed / Invoiced per currency against the Approved Budget, 101: 6 goals; (b) Health Green, Schedule On Time, Budget Within as a share of the Active Projects, 100: 3 goals, "22%" reads as "22% on time". **Where it does not (18, no target):** the denominators themselves (Total, Closed, Active, Approved Budget), Completed this Year, Approval Pending, Average Delay, the Overruns (no plan exists; a percent would hide what the reader came for), and the BAD buckets (Amber, Red, No EVM, Delayed, At Risk, No schedule, Over, Not started): as a share under 101, "33% of projects are red" would draw GREEN (marks 50/100), which is false; with target 1 the caption reads "300%", which says nothing. They keep the count.
**The 18 still draw a dial.** ZK has no "no dial". One new schema, client 11, "No target (Gray)", all marks AD_PrintColor 115: the needle at 0 on gray says "no target, read the caption" (the window's own help: measures without a goal are white). No colour threshold is invented: the two with thresholds are stock.
**Numbers:** this pilot's own today (Budget USD 39971166.17, BDT 30286701.64, Active 9), static like every stock target, and named as demo in the script header: a site enters its own. Ships as `202611041000_CAPEX-G7.sql` (LEGACY CONVENTION) + twin pack 1.0.2 generated from it. §6 Q1 stays open: it concerns the health rule, not these.
**Witnesses change, because a target exists now:** a target save 1→0 (W-PA-TILES, W-DASH-SYNC) would WIPE the target. ### 5.29 SPEC — the WS types as migration scripts (2026-11-04; RESUME NEXT 1, LEGACY CONVENTION "proven and due"; written before code)
**Issue:** "the CAPEX web-service types exist only as ad-hoc `DO $$` scripts plus rows other lanes left on the pilot, so a clean iDempiere cannot get them the way iDempiere ships its own changes."
**Measured before writing this (read-only):** stock DB `idempiere` = 16 WS types, same dictionary as the pilot (26,519 AD_Column), pilot = 311. None of the output columns the CAPEX types use is a custom column. Four things the RESUME line did not say:
1. **There are 6 ad-hoc files, not 5.** `querykeys` only UPDATEs/INSERTs over the others, so its result folds into their rows. Nothing was ever shipped in ad-hoc form, so no delta script is owed for it.
2. **The ad-hoc files are not enough on a clean server.** `capex/adapters.js LEGACY_TYPES` reads 8 types; `readtypes` creates 6. `QueryCProject` and `QueryADOrg` came from the bridge lane (bim-compiler `scripts/bridge/pilot/ws_changelog_read.sql`, `ws_shopday_readtypes.sql`, client 0). The push also needs `QueryMProduct`, `QueryMProductCategory`, same origin. They join the scripts.
3. **`QueryADOrg` still carries a Free `Filter`** (IDEMPIERE-1784: iDempiere refuses that on a model save; SQL skipped the rule). `querykeys` missed it. The bridge reads it WITH a filter (`ad_client.js:106`, `dict_spec_sd00.json`), so the pilot row stays; the script ships Constant `1=1`, like the other 7. This is the one row where script != pilot.
4. **Access rows for role 1000000 (ShopDay tenant, bridge lane) are not ours:** that role does not exist on a clean server. Scripts carry role 102 only.
**Decided:**
- **Rows are EXTRACTED from the pilot** (ids, `_UU`, every column, the two exceptions above), not re-authored: the pilot's rows are what the twin PASSed on. `capex/tools/ws_types_to_migration.js` writes the files, one plain INSERT per row, only non-null columns, as "Log Migration Script" writes them. No `DO $$`, no `nextidfunc`, no `gen_random_uuid`.
- **One script per ad-hoc file (5), in two folders**, because what a client may run differs:
  - `capex/legacy/ws/migration/iD13/postgresql/202611042300_CAPEX-WS-READ.sql`: the 8 dashboard read types. **The only one a site installs**: this is the film correction's "read-only setup data".
  - `capex/legacy/ws_testserver/migration/iD13/postgresql/`: `…2301_CAPEX-WS-PUSH` (writetypes + QueryCUOM/QueryMProduct/QueryMProductCategory), `…2302_CAPEX-WS-PHASE` (updatetypes), `…2303_CAPEX-WS-PA` (patypes), `…2304_CAPEX-WS-PROGRESS` (progresstypes). Write access, test server only: a site's real push is TASK 2's plugin. SyncDB runs a whole folder, so a shared folder would hand a client's server the write types.
- **Neither joins `capex/legacy/migration/`:** W-MIG-SYNC reads that folder as the release the twin applies; these types have no twin side.
- **The pilot only gets the five names registered** (`register_migration_script`, ledger row), since its rows already exist and a re-run would hit duplicate keys. `pgrep run_all` first.
- **The 6 `capex/tools/ws_capex_*.sql` are deleted** in the same PR, restore recipes moving to the new headers.
**Not in this item, named:** the scripts still hard-code client 11, role 102 and pilot ids; a site may already use those ids: RESUME NEXT 2. A site on an older iDempiere may lack a column: the FK error stops the run, as SyncDB does. `BridgeCreateInvoice/InvoiceLine/ProjectIssue`, which `pilot_progress.js` borrows, stay with the bridge lane; progress runs need them there.
**W-WS-MIG `capex/tests/witness_ws_migration.js`** (scratch DB = `CREATE DATABASE … TEMPLATE idempiere`, dropped at the end; the pilot is only read, until the ledger step):
- **F form:** every script: first statement `register_migration_script`, its own name; every statement is an INSERT, no `DO`/`nextidfunc`/`gen_random_uuid`; ids ≥ 1000000; every row carries `_UU`.
- **A apply:** the scratch DB holds 0 of the 24 types first (else VACUOUS); `psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -f`, name order, exit 0; registered once each; `update_sequences()` leaves each WS sequence past its MAX.
- **R rows:** for all 5 tables, every column except created/updated == the pilot, by primary key; the only differences allowed are exactly the two named above, and each must be seen (a rule that matches nothing is a failure).
- **S separation:** the site folder holds only queryData types, `isreadwrite=N`, and no other script; its types == `LEGACY_TYPES` values, 8 of 8.
- **E end of the chain:** a real iDempiere server on the scratch DB (the stopped `~/idempiere-bimplugin` server, `-DPropertyFile` to a scratch copy, nothing of it edited): `legacy_snapshot.js` reads 8 of 8 with `unavailable=[]`; a DataRow-key lookup on `QueryCProject` finds a project with no Filter sent; every write type does one real call, and psql sees the result; a write through a read type is refused. **Denominator = 24 types: X of 24 exercised.** An entity with 0 rows on stock is INCONCLUSIVE, not PASS.
- **P pilot:** the five names are in its ledger; its row counts did not move.
- **BREAK:** `WS_MIG_BREAK=1` drops one output row from a copy of a script before applying: R MUST FAIL. `WS_MIG_BREAK=2` applies with the read-type access row flipped to `isreadwrite=Y`: S MUST FAIL.

### 5.29 RESULT — done (2026-11-04); logs read (scratch, not committed)
**W-WS-MIG PASS, judged 57, failed 0, inconclusive 0. 24 of 24 types called on a real server built from the scripts alone.** `WS_MIG_BREAK=1` FAIL (R2: `ws_webservicefieldoutput:1002719 missing`), `WS_MIG_BREAK=2` FAIL (R2 and S2: read types flipped to `rw=Y`). Clone dropped, server stopped, both checked.
**Measured:** five scripts, 514 INSERTs: READ 257 · PUSH 190 · PHASE 6 · PA 37 · PROGRESS 24. 518 pilot rows compared, every column except created/updated equal; the only differences are the two named: 4 role-1000000 access rows left out, 1 Filter made Constant. Clean server: 16 types before, 0 of 24 present. `update_sequences()` moves all four WS sequences past their MAX. The dashboard job on that server: `§DASH_LEGACY_SNAPSHOT projects=3 phases=1 tasks=1 lines=2 orgs=10 phaseRef=13 currencies=175 wf=13 unavailable=[]`.

**Pilot:** the five names registered (ledger 1305 → 1310); no WS row touched, counts unchanged across the run. `QueryADOrg` stays Free there because the bridge reads it with a filter: **the pilot and the script differ on that one row on purpose.**
**Corrected from the spec:** the denominator is 24, not 23 (8 + 8 + 1 + 3 + 4). The first run also caught the witness printing "24 of 24" with one type missing, because a label carried a note and a line added the type by hand; fixed, so a missing type now fails E4.

### 5.30 SPEC — scripts that take a site's tenant (2026-11-04; RESUME NEXT 2; written before code)
**Issue:** "every CAPEX script hard-codes client 11, USD + BDT and the pilot's target numbers, so a real site cannot install the dashboard: its goals would land in the wrong tenant, in currencies it does not hold, with another company's budget as their target, and the ids may already be taken."
**Measured:** 82 rows carry client 11 (27 PA_MeasureCalc, 27 PA_Measure, 27 PA_Goal, 1 PA_ColorSchema), plus the 8 read types. 5 goal families come in a USD + BDT pair, 10 goals in all. 4 `UPDATE PA_Goal` in G7 hold the pilot's numbers. Stock colour schemas 100/101, print colour 115 and PA_DashboardContent 50003 are client 0, so they need no change. `WS_WebServiceType` is unique on (Value, AD_Client_ID), so types can be tenant-scoped.
**Decided:**
- **The shipped scripts stay the ONE source, untouched; a tool renders a site's scripts from them.** `capex/tools/site_migration.js` parses the same files (`MA.split`), so a change to a goal reaches every site by re-rendering, and no second copy of the rows exists.
- **It never connects to a site.** `--preflight <client>` prints a read-only SQL; the site's admin runs it and hands back the JSON: client, roles, the currencies its `C_Project` rows actually hold ("taken from the data, not chosen", §5.16), next free id per table (the larger of MAX+1, the sequence's CurrentNext, 1000000), and any type or CAPEX goal already there.
- **Output, into a folder given on the command line (never in the repo: no client name in code):** `<stamp>_CAPEX-SITE-<client>-WS-READ.sql` and `<stamp+1>_CAPEX-SITE-<client>-PA.sql`. Plain INSERTs, LEGACY CONVENTION, same format. `*_UU` = a hash of (client, shipped UU): same input, same file; two tenants on one server do not clash.
- **Client:** PA and WS rows go to the site's client. The two read types shipped at client 0 also go there, so the whole set is one tenant's and one restore. **Role:** given by the operator (`--role`), never guessed; refused when absent or not in the preflight's list.
- **Currency:** a row named `(USD)` is the template of its family and `(BDT)` is dropped; each template becomes one row per site currency (name, description, `C_Currency_ID=`). A site's currency is one its projects hold. SeqNo renumbers by 10 in shipped order. A currency token in any other row is a refusal, not a guess.
- **Targets (`--targets file`, optional):** G7's own groups, read from its UPDATEs: `{budget:{<ISO>:n}, activeProjects:n}`. Cost goals against budget take schema 101 and share goals schema 100, as G7. **No number given = target 0 on the gray schema** ("no plan behind it"): the pilot's numbers can never reach a site. A site enters its own in the Performance Goal window, as on every stock goal.
- **Refuses, naming why (exit 2):** no role · no project currency · a read type or CAPEX goal already on the site.
**Not in this item, named:** the twin's side (its tenant for a site, the 2Pack for it) stays ⛔ §6 Q-S3/Q-S4. A currency added at a site later means re-rendering, and that re-render is a new script only for the new rows: not built until a site needs it. A site with no project yet refuses: there is nothing to take a currency from.
**W-SITE-MIG `capex/tests/witness_site_migration.js`** (clone of `pilot_snap`, which has a real second tenant, ShopDay client 1000000, and its own rows above 1000000; dropped at the end):
- **G golden:** rendered for client 11, USD + BDT and the pilot's targets == the shipped rows, every column, joined by Name (ids, UU, created/updated aside; FKs compared by the name they point to). Any difference must be named. This proves the renderer loses nothing.
- **N refusals:** no role, no currency, a type already there: exit 2, no file.
- **A apply:** preflight on the clone's real tenant, render, `ON_ERROR_STOP`, in name order. Every new id above the preflight's; nothing existing moved; no client 11, no USD-BDT leftovers, none of the pilot's numbers in the files.
- **E end of the chain:** a real server on the clone (fixture, clone only: ShopDay gets 3 projects, USD 1000 + MYR 2500 + MYR 500; the two client-0 read types are removed so only the script can serve). Signed in as the tenant's own admin: `legacy_snapshot.js` reads 8 of 8 with `projects=3`, none of client 11's. **The real ZK Home of that tenant shows 27 CAPEX captions, MYR in them, no BDT.** Stored `PA_Goal`: Total 3, Approved USD 1000, Approved MYR 3000. Target given for USD only: USD cost goals on 101, MYR on gray.
- **BREAK:** `SITE_MIG_BREAK=1` renders ids one below the free band: the apply MUST FAIL on the duplicate key. `SITE_MIG_BREAK=2` renders MYR with USD's currency id: the Home's numbers MUST FAIL.

### 5.30 RESULT — done (2026-11-04); logs read (scratch, not committed)
**W-SITE-MIG PASS, judged 30, failed 0, inconclusive 0.** `SITE_MIG_BREAK=1` FAIL (`duplicate key … ws_webservicetype_id=1200020`), `SITE_MIG_BREAK=2` FAIL (E9: Approved MYR 1000, want 3000). Clone dropped, server stopped.
**The chain, on a real second tenant (ShopDay, client 1000000; USD + MYR taken from its own projects):** preflight → render → apply → sign in as its own admin. Its user is refused: `Service type QueryCProject not configured`, so the types really are the tenant's.

**No ids of another site are used:** the preflight read the tenant's own free band (`WS_WebServiceType` 1200021 and so on); PA tables started at 1000000 because this tenant's server has none. Each WS table grew by exactly what the script holds.

Not yet run against a real site: only a local tenant with a handful of rows, and its Home's numbers are all from 3 projects (G6b at a real site's size stays open, item 5). ### 5.31 SPEC — the Home reads the committed state, the rest of §5.24 (2026-11-05; RESUME NEXT 3; written after W-HOME-TIP ran red on the unfixed code, before the fix)
**Issue:** "the Home's document-status gadget reads the base only, so what a user saved never reaches its counts: legacy counts it, because its DB holds it". §5.24 fixed this for Performance and named this one open. "Activities" in that line is this same gadget: `docstatus.zul` (`activities.zul` is not ported; it stays a logged `§DASH-UNPORTED`).

**W-HOME-TIP `capex/tests/witness_home_tip.js`** (real `erp/idempiere.html`, no pilot needed): commit a note and a draft sales order through `applyOpGroup`, reboot, read the page's counts.
