### 5.31 RESULT — (a) done (2026-11-05); logs read (scratch, not committed)
**W-HOME-TIP PASS 9, failed 0.** Red before the fix: E1, E2, E3 FAIL, counts `0 → 0`. After: `0 → 1` on all three, Request stays 1. `HOME_TIP_BREAK=1` FAIL 3. Regressions: W-DASH-DOCSTATUS 6/6 · W-HOME-DASHBOARD-LIVE 18/18 · W-PA-EDITOR 7/7 · W-MIG-SYNC 7/7 · W-PA-RENDER 32/32, 6 runs of 6. `sw.js` v996. Lines: `home.js` +25 −9.
**Found + fixed on the way:** W-PA-RENDER's B1 was already flaky: it failed 1 run in 3 on clean `main`, because it read the boot log right after `networkidle`, before the restore lines arrived. It now waits for them.
**Not fixed, another lane's:** `witness_core_untouched` CU4 FAILS because `erp/plugins/mf_lens.mjs` references an overlay module (from `0e0cd312`, ERP Team wave 1).
**⛔ (b) NOT DONE, OWNER — a due goal computed at render is not stored.** Legacy stores it at render (`MGoal.updateGoal` :383-415, `measure.updateGoals()` saves). So within the 30-minute interval after a DATA change, legacy's Home shows the stored old value and the twin's shows the live one. That is a real twin difference, and it must close on the SQLite side.
Why it was not just built: the faithful port is a signed op group per due render (`modelRun` PROCESS + `applyOpGroup`, as `team_sync.js:556`). Read in the code then, MEASURED in §5.32's control since: on a Team, `DateLastRun` and `MeasureActual` would be written by every device, and the comm layer judges conflicts per column (`team_sync.js:332`, shown red at :640, `CurrentRecordModified`). Two users opening their Home would raise a conflict legacy never shows, and signed ops that have replicated cannot be taken back. Measured size: 27 goals every 30 minutes per user.
**The one question (still the owner's; §5.32 built the recommended side, 2026-11-05): when a Home render stores a goal, should the store reach the rest of the Team?** Recommended: NO, keep it on the device (a signed op marked local-only, `localOnly` already exists at `team_sync.js:369`, not yet tried for this). Each user then sees what legacy shows them, with no conflicts and no traffic. The one remaining difference: another user's render does not refresh my stored value, where on legacy it does.

### 5.32 SPEC — a due goal is stored when the Home renders (2026-11-05; RESUME NEXT 3b; written before code)
**Issue:** "legacy stores a due goal at render (`MGoal.updateGoal` :383-415) and the twin only computes it, so within the refresh interval after a data change legacy's Home shows the stored value and the twin's the live one."
**The §5.31 question was not answered:** the owner said "resume". This builds the recommended side because it is the reversible one: the ops stay on the device, so nothing has to be taken back if the owner decides the store should reach the Team. **It is my call, not the owner's words.** The one difference it leaves is named below and stays the owner's.
**Measured:** `PA_Goal` is a shared table in the Team plan (`team_plan.js` T724, BOTH, space), and it must stay shared because a target edit has to replicate. So a table rule cannot do this; the marker is per op. `translate` has the path a device-owned row already takes, `{quiet}` → `§TEAM_STATION_LOCAL`, which is neither sent nor shown as a warning and is kept through the projection rebuild. `AD_Session` already writes a signed op at sign-in with no user action, so a render-time signed op is not a new kind of thing.
**Decided:**
- **`GoalEngine.userGoals(q, s)`** becomes the one owner of `MGoal.getUserGoals`'s query (moved out of `dashboard_gadgets.performance`). **`GoalEngine.storeDue(q, write, ctx)`:** each of the user's goals that `refreshDue` calls due → `updateMeasureGoals` for its measure, once per measure, as `measure.updateGoals()` writes every goal of the measure. No second SQL builder.
- **`ModelPA.storeDue(session)`** (`erp/model_pa.js`, core): one `modelRun` PROCESS + one `applyOpGroup`, as `team_sync.js:556` runs a system write. No role check on the write, as legacy's model save. Every op carries `teamLocal: 'goal-refresh'`. Logs `§GOAL_STORE` with counts, ms and heap.
- **`team_sync.translate`:** an op with `teamLocal` returns `{quiet}`, before any table rule. A goal saved in its window carries no marker and still replicates.
- **`home.js`** calls it at the end of `render()`. The first draw stays what it is, and the value it draws for a due goal is the value stored.
**Settled by the owner's Team principle, 2026-11-05:** *"a lazy mode of team relay rather in a segmented segregation of work among team members. Where a team register a scope and others who wishes to register will be alerted. Where there is no claimant to a workspace, it just goes on until it come across a neighbour."* A refresh is derived, not claimed work, so it stays off the relay. Read by me as settling §5.31's question; the owner did not use the words "accept the difference", so this is NOT logged as a `LEGACY-QUIRK`. **The one difference left, named:** another user's render does not refresh my stored value, where on legacy it does (one DB). The interval still bounds it. On one device, or for one user, the twin now does what legacy does.
**Not in this item, named:** `Env.isReadOnlySession` has no twin equivalent, so nothing is skipped for it. Undo (`kernel_ops.undoOp`) takes the newest op whatever it is, so the first Undo straight after a Home render reverts the refresh, as it already does for the `AD_Session` op.
**W-GOAL-STORE `capex/tests/witness_goal_store.js`: the same three steps on real legacy and on the twin, captions compared exactly.** Legacy = a real server on a throwaway clone of stock, its real ZK Home. Twin = the real `erp/idempiere.html`. Goal = stock `Total Open Service Requests` (target 10, so one open request = 10%).
- **1** first render: the goal's `DateLastRun` moves from 2007 to today, and it is stored.
- **2** a new open request, render again inside the interval: the caption does NOT move, the stored row does not move.
- **3** the stored `DateLastRun` aged one hour, render again: the caption moves, the row is stored with the new count.
- **X** legacy's captions == the twin's, at every step.
- **O** the twin's refresh ops are one group, each marked `teamLocal`. A target edit made afterwards still scores from the stored row.
- `GOAL_STORE_BREAK=1` removes `ModelPA.storeDue`: step 2 MUST FAIL (that is today's twin). It must fail first on the unbuilt code too.
**W-TEAM-SO gets one arm, §TEAM-GOAL-LOCAL:** on both devices in team mode, refresh ops log `§TEAM_STATION_LOCAL` and no `pa_goal` op with that marker leaves; a target edit (no marker) does leave. No conflict is raised by two Homes rendering. Read the log.

### 5.32 RESULT — done (2026-11-05); logs read (scratch, not committed)
**W-GOAL-STORE PASS 10, failed 0, on real legacy and the real twin.** Captions, 3 steps: legacy `10% → 10% → 20%`. Red before the fix: twin `10% → 20% → 20%`. Now: twin `10% → 10% → 20%`. `GOAL_STORE_BREAK=1` FAIL 5. `§GOAL_STORE goals=4 written=4 ops=4 committed=true ms=61 heapMB=51`.
**W-TEAM-SO PASS 28, §TEAM-GOAL-LOCAL on two real devices:** a goal created on the Team and a target edit both leave and arrive (B reads 77). Both devices store it at render, 5 goals each, and nothing more leaves. No conflict. **Control, the same writes with no marker: the conflict appears on both devices**, so the marker is needed and §5.31's reason was right.
**Two of my own statements were wrong, corrected here:**
1. §5.32's spec said a goal saved in its window "still replicates". True for a goal created on the Team, or a seeded goal **on a Team with a pinned base** (`team_sync.js:347`). In a harness with no base, a seeded goal's edit stays local: `target-not-a-team-record`. That is the Team's existing rule; this change does not touch it.
2. First reading of a green "no conflict": it was vacuous, because my fixture used goal 105's id as a measure id, so nothing was stored. Fixed to measure 104; now written=5.
**LIMIT, named: a goal CREATED on the Team loses its local refresh at the next Visit.** The rebuild moves its local pk (`-42 → -57`) and keeps the op verbatim, so the op is orphaned and harmless: new pks start above every kept op, so it cannot land on another record. That goal is due again at its next render, as before this change. Seeded goals, which every CAPEX goal is, keep theirs through a Visit (witnessed). Each orphan stays in the device's op-log.
**The difference left to legacy, still the owner's:** another user's render does not refresh my stored value.

### 5.33 SQL SYSDATE was the UTC day (2026-11-05; found running §5.32's regressions, so the spec is written with the fix, after W-SQL-CLOCK ran red)
**Issue:** "SQL SYSDATE in the twin is the UTC day, where legacy's is the server's local day, so east of UTC, until the offset has passed, every measure and default reading it is a day behind." It failed W-DASH-SYNC 7 and W-G6A-HOME 5 **on clean `main`** at 07:43 +08 (Average Delay 180 vs legacy 181); both pass after 08:00, which is why it hid.
**Cause:** `erp/crud_overlay.js _coBindUdf` bound the runtime with `now: Date.now()`, undoing §5.24's `RUNTIME.now` fix whenever the overlay mounted. **Fix, one deletion:** no clock of its own. Readers checked: every `RT.now` user wants a calendar date; `ProcessedOn` reads `env.nowMillis`, the true epoch, a separate value.
**W-SQL-CLOCK `capex/tests/witness_sql_clock.js`** (two zones at the ends of the clock, so one always differs from UTC; a zone that does not differ is not counted): red `sysdate 2026-11-04 23:47` at local 2026-11-05 → `2026-11-05 13:47`, days-between `-1 → 0`. `SQL_CLOCK_BREAK=1` FAIL 3.
**In the same window (07:47 +08), after the fix:** W-DASH-SYNC 172/172 (it was FAIL 7 on `main`), W-G6A-HOME 88/88 (FAIL 5, including H1). Reach: every `SYSDATE` measure and `@SysDate@` default, not only CAPEX.
**W-DASH-SYNC changed:** its oracle is legacy just recomputed, so its twin page sets the refresh interval off; the interval itself is W-GOAL-STORE's. **W-G6A-HOME H1 was flaky, on clean `main` too:** it read the boot log before the overlay's asynchronous line arrived. It now waits: 5 runs of 5 (a sixth was killed by my own 90 s limit, exit 124, no verdict from it). My first version of this wait sat before `await open()` and did nothing; it failed 3 of 4 and was caught before merge.
**Regressions after both:** see the PR. **Not run, named:** `witness_local_time` is INCONCLUSIVE on its own (it reads `erp/pos_lens.js`, gone) · `witness_pos_chain` needs its `PC_LINES` fixture, which I did not supply, so it is no evidence · `scripts/bridge/run_all.sh` (the parallel run) not run for this clock change: run it at the next parallel-run pass.

### 5.34 What the guide now says, and what was checked for it (2026-11-05; owner: "Is it exactly as SYSNOVA Dashboard sample depicts?", "What is the SQLite twin version showing better or more?", "Yes clarify this in the guide")
**Live:** https://red1oon.github.io/BIMCompiler/CapexDashboardGuide/ §Three places (bim-compiler #373, deployed from `deploy/capex-guide-superset-3`). The public guide names no client and carries no client figure or screenshot (§14 framing); this file may.
**Against `~/Projects/SYSNOVA/SYSNOVADashboardScreenshot.png` (one 2026-09-02 screen share, cut off at the bottom; their code and formulas still unseen, Q-S1):** the same title, left menu's six items, three filters, Last Updated, seven tiles, five panels. **Not the same:** Forecast (they show ৳671.23 Cr, ours definition pending, Q3) · Committed verified/contract split (ours invoiced, Q4) · Stage names (their five, ours `C_Phase`, Q2) · single currency in Crore (ours each currency in full) · an info mark (ours the ƒ line) · icons and progress bars (ours none, not built) · "Live data" + "Classic dashboard (v1)" link (ours the job's last run, no v1).
**The twin, measured on main with a job-shaped copy:** Home 31 dials (4 stock + 27) reading the pilot's scale: Total 13, Approved USD 39,971,166, Approval 261. It shows no more than legacy, by the CARDINAL rule. `rendered=4 unported=6` of 10 gadgets: KPI1, KPI2, Views, Running Jobs, Calendar, Favorites say *Not ported yet*.
**Guide errors fixed:** Source A is `../erp/ad_seed.db` (`capex/dash.js:9`), not "the browser copy". Tenant tool and read-type script now exist.
**The editor (owner: "an editor is in the roadmap", film v5 row 10: "Change tiles and layout without code, and without AI... not built yet"):** not built. Today rich and editable are on opposite sides: the page draws what goals cannot and has no editor; the stock windows edit a goal's numbers and SQL, never the look. Page tiles do not click through to records; the one link out is BIM. For a future editor: the page binds to 8 whole tables fetched up front, so a new tile can use only columns those read types bring.
**⚠ TRAP, found, NOT fixed (small, next to pick up):** `legacy_copy.mjs applyCopy` given a trimmed snapshot (15 columns, no `AD_Client_ID`: `capex/sample/pilot_snapshot.json`, or any file the job did not name `site_copy.json`) logs `inserted=11 failed=0`, yet the rows land outside the client and the Home reads the seed (Total 2). A green log over nothing arriving. It should refuse a snapshot whose rows carry no client. W-DASH-SYNC is blind to it: it always feeds a job-shaped file.

### 5.35 SPEC — the page editor: change the look, the numbers stay bound to the data model (2026-11-05; owner: "To edit its L&F but it will always drill into the underlying data model elements", "This dashboard HTML editable for both twin and legacy to use?", "We spec out the editor?", "Go ahead"; film v5: "Change tiles and layout without code, and without AI"). **SPEC ONLY: no code until the owner says go on a stage.**
**Issue:** "the dashboard page cannot be changed without a developer, and it is not one screen for both sides: its layout and tiles are hand-written in code, and it cannot read the twin's own data."
**Measured before writing:**
- `capex/dash.js` (187 lines) hand-draws the seven tiles and five panels, one block per tile. `capex/tiles.js` (190 lines) holds the formulas. No row anywhere says what is on the page or where.
- Source A is `../erp/ad_seed.db`, a shipped file (`dash.js:9`). The page never reads the twin's own DB. It writes nothing, and the guide promises that.
- **Stock already has the shape, so no new table is needed:** `PA_DashboardContent` carries `Name`, `ColumnNo`, `Line`, `PA_Goal_ID`, `AD_Chart_ID`, `ZulFilePath`, `HTML`, `Help`, `IsShowTitle`, `AD_Role_ID`, `AD_User_ID`, with `_Trl` and `_Access` tables. `AD_Chart` + `AD_ChartDatasource` carry `ChartType` (Ring, Pie, Bar, Stacked Bar, Line, ...) and `FromClause`, `WhereClause`, `ValueColumn`, `CategoryColumn`, `SeriesColumn`. All have stock windows. The film's correction forbids a new table or column on their iDempiere (RESUME).
- **Landmine, read not assumed:** `IsShowInDashboard='N'` does NOT hide a row: it moves it to the side panel (`DashboardController.java:199`). The twin's Home ported selection keeps `IsShowInLogin='Y'` (`overlay/dashboard_home.js:30`), so `IsShowInLogin='N'` is the candidate way to keep a page row off every Home. **To be proven on a real ZK Home (E0), not trusted.**
- `overlay/dashboard_home.js:17` classifies a chart gadget, but `home.js` draws no chart: the twin's Home says *Not ported yet* for one. Building the chart reader here closes that too.
- `PA_DashboardContent`, `AD_Chart`, `AD_ChartDatasource`, `AD_SysConfig`, `PA_ColorSchema` are all shared in the Team plan (`erp/team_plan.js`), so a layout edit replicates with no new flow.

**The design in one line: the page becomes a generic renderer of rows, and the editor only ever writes those rows.**
| Layer | Owner | What changes |
|---|---|---|
| **What is on the page, where** | `PA_DashboardContent` rows (`IsShowInLogin='N'`), one per tile | New rows, shipped as a migration script + the twin's pack (LEGACY CONVENTION). `ZulFilePath` names the tile's kind, `capex:<kind>`; `ColumnNo` + `Line` its place; `Name` its title (`_Trl` translates it); `Help` its ƒ line; `_Access` who sees it |
| **Where its number comes from** | One binding per row, see the rule below | Nothing new |
| **Page-wide look** | `AD_SysConfig` rows `CAPEX_PAGE_*` (title, subtitle, accent), per client, stock window | Colours from `PA_ColorSchema` where a tile has one |
| **Calculating** | `tiles.js` (its named tiles, kept, owner 2026-11-04) · `goal_engine.js` (goals) · one new generic chart reader | No formula moves |
| **Drawing** | Generic renderers per KIND (number, money per currency, percent, ring, bars, rows, table), replacing the per-tile blocks in `dash.js` | **Deletion budget: the hand-written layout goes. Add-only = not done** |
| **Data** | The 8 row sets, as today | A third source, the twin's own DB through `__crud.withTipQuery` |

**THE BINDING RULE, the point of the design (W-PAGE-BIND):** every element drawn names its source, and it is one of exactly three: a `tiles.js` key · a `PA_Goal_ID` · an `AD_Chart_ID`. A row with none is refused and draws nothing: no free-typed number, no static HTML tile. The picker offers only what the dictionary holds (`AD_Column`) and this page's data can reach; a column outside the read types is refused with its name, never a blank. An empty population still prints *no data*, never 0. Formulas are NOT edited on the page: SQL stays in the stock windows, where legacy's own rules apply. **The editor can change how a number looks and where it sits. It has no way to change the number.**

**Both sides, one page:**
| | Legacy site | Twin |
|---|---|---|
| Runs | Beside their server, as today | Inside Kernel-ERP, as an `AD_Form` + `AD_Menu` from the capex pack (`ad_form_generic.js`), no host code (AD-LAYER LAW 6) |
| Reads rows | The job's file: the READ script gains read-only types for the layout tables | Its own DB, base + signed tip |
| Edit in stock windows | **Yes**, then the job | **Yes** |
| Edit on the page itself | Edit mode writes nothing: it **exports a migration script** for the administrator | Saves as signed ops through `applyOpGroup`; can also export the same script |

The page stays read-only against iDempiere and holds no password, so the guide's promise survives. The same rows mean the same page: W-PAGE-BOTH.
**A twin-only screen is not a twin defect:** legacy has no such page, it is an add-on on both sides. The Home panels are untouched and stay legacy's look (CARDINAL).
**Team (owner's principle, 2026-11-05):** a layout edit is real work, so it replicates; the page is a natural scope to register once registration exists (not built). Until then it is unclaimed: edit goes on, and the per-column conflict is the backstop at an edge.

**Stages, each closed by its witness (`§`-log, never a screenshot, PRIMAL LAW):**
- **E0 Probe, on a throwaway clone with a real server, no page code.** Rows with `IsShowInLogin='N'`: on no ZK Home for any user, including one who already has preferences; `ZulFilePath='capex:x'` breaks nothing; the stock window opens and saves them; the "add gadget" list, checked. `AD_SysConfig` value length measured. **If this fails: stop and ask the owner. No fallback gets invented here.**
- **E1 Rows drive the page, zero visible change.** Default rows reproduce today's page. **W-PAGE-RENDER:** on the tracked sample, every `data-tile` and `data-flat` value == today's, same order; W-CAPEX 38 and W-CAPEX-SAMPLE still PASS; hand-written layout deleted. Break: a row removed MUST FAIL.
- **E2 The twin's own data.** The form and third source. **W-PAGE-BOTH:** same rows + same data on both sources → identical flat map; a saved twin edit reaches the page; the twin page and the Home agree where they overlap.
- **E3 Edit the look.** Move, hide, rename, page title, accent, on the page with pointer events. **W-PAGE-EDIT:** drive the real page, read the stored row back, reload, assert: place changed by the asserted amount, **the flat map byte-identical** (the numbers did not move); undo returns it. A green editor witness that never edited is a §CRISIS defect. Break: an edit that also changes a formula MUST FAIL.
- **E4 Add a tile, bound.** Pick a goal, or a chart made in the stock window. The chart reader, shared with the Home's chart gadget. **W-PAGE-BIND:** unbound refused, bad column refused by name, empty prints *no data*; **chart value == the same SQL run on legacy, real server.**
- **E5 Reach a site.** Export → the script applies on a clone → the job → the legacy page shows the edit. `site_migration.js` renders the rows for a tenant. Guide + film line updated only now.

**Not in this editor, named:** editing formulas on the page · free HTML or custom CSS · per-user personal layouts (stock `PA_DashboardPreference` is the path, later) · a client logo · replacing the Home panels · tiles whose source the 8 row sets cannot reach · Forecast, Procurement, Handover (still §6 Q3, Q6). `tiles.js` stays.
**Owner's decisions, each with what this spec assumes until told otherwise:**
1. **Editing on the page at a legacy site exports a script, it does not save directly.** Saving directly would need a write type and a password in the page, and would break the guide's "writes nothing back". Recommended: keep.
2. **What "look" covers in v1:** position, title, show or hide, page title and subtitle, accent colour. Fonts, sizes and logos later. Recommended: start here.
3. **Which stage to go on.** E0 alone is cheap and decides whether this design stands. Recommended: go on E0 only.

### 5.36 SPEC — a Project selector and the BIM Document tile (2026-11-11; owner: "I reckoned the Dashboard has 'All' if blank for C_Project_ID selector (I propose if this is not put there)"; written before code)
**Measured (code read, `capex/dash.js`, `index.html`):** the filters are Org (blank = "All Organizations"), Category (blank = "All"), Status (default Active). A single project is selectable ONLY by the URL parameter `?project=<C_Project.Value>` (`dash.js:120`), shown as a read-only label `Project: <Value>` (`dash.js:174`); there is no drop-down. The BIM link is found by file name `<Value>_extracted.db` (`probeBim`, `dash.js:147-152`). No tile reads BIM Document.
**A. Project selector.** A fourth filter `Project` beside Org/Category/Status: first option blank = **All Projects** (value ''), then the projects that pass the Org/Category/Status filters (Value + Name), so the list never offers a project the other filters hide. Selecting one sets the same `PROJECT` the URL parameter sets (the URL parameter stays and pre-selects it); both sources apply the same filter (`passes`, `T.compute(d, f)`). Changing Org/Category/Status re-builds the list and resets to blank if the selected project fell out. Log: `§DASH_RENDER ... project=<Value|''>` (exists) plus `§DASH_PROJSEL options=<n> selected=<Value|''>`.
**B. Document tile (BIM Document).** `BIM Documents issued for construction / deliverables` over the filtered projects, per the BIM Document plugin's own rule (a deliverable = (project, deliverable no) at its LATEST revision; CO counts; Superseded rows are not deliverables). Source A (the twin) reads table `BIM_Document`. **Source B (legacy iDempiere) has no such table unless the plugin is installed (since 2026-11-11 it is installed on the local TEST instance only, and still needs a read type: task T4 above)**, so on B the tile prints **no data (BIM Document not installed on this server)**, never 0 % (empty population rule; TWIN rule: it is the legacy side that lacks the table, not a divergence to hide). A project with 0 deliverables prints no data. Formula line (ƒ) states the columns.
**Witness W-DASH-PROJSEL (new) + W-DASH-BIMDOC:** D1 the selector's first option is blank "All Projects" and the tiles equal the unfiltered tiles; D2 selecting project X gives the tiles `?project=X` gives (same numbers, both sources); D3 an Org filter that hides X removes X from the list and resets; D4 tile = `COUNT(latest CO)` / `COUNT(latest)` recomputed from `bim_document` independently; D5 Source B prints `no data` with the reason; D6 VACUOUS guard: a project with no deliverables is INCONCLUSIVE. Break switch: list built from ALL projects -> D3 FAILS.
**§5.36A RESULT 2026-11-11:** built as specified; `capex/tests/witness_dash_projsel.js` (D0-D4). 
**§5.36B RESULT 2026-11-11 (T4 ✅ DONE):** `capex/tests/witness_dash_bimdoc.js` 8/0 (D1 real WS on the test instance returns 10 of 10 fixture rows; D2 twin 3/6 == legacy 3/6; D3/D4 supersession recount; D5/D5p not installed; D6 empty = no data; D7 real page tile 3/6). RED first: before the code D1-D6 all FAILED; after only the READ script, D1+D2 FAILED with `Service type QueryBIMDocument not configured`. `BIMDOC_BREAK=1` (supersession ignored) FAILS D3, D4, D7 (5/9 vs 3/6). Migration `capex/legacy/ws/bimdoc/migration/iD13/postgresql/202611111200_CAPEX-WS-BIMDOC.sql` (its own subfolder: W-WS-MIG demands the site folder hold ONE all-plain-INSERT script and I broke it 6 ways putting this one beside it; this script uses a DO block, ids from the sequences, and does nothing, unrecorded, where the plugin is absent), applied on `~/idempiere-bimdoc` (with `CAPEX-WS-READ`, which that seed did not have; update_sequences, server restarted by PID). Two things found: (1) `adapters.js loadLegacy` crashed on a type the server never registered (`WindowTabData.Error 'Service type X not configured'`, a different wire form than the 500 it handled): now = unavailable; `ADAPTERS.OPTIONAL=['bimdocs']`, and W-SITE-MIG E1 / W-DASH-SYNC J3 no longer demand it. (2) `witness_dash_sync` E2 (`legacy 1 -> 1`) FAILS identically on untouched main: the pilot moved under another session, not this change. The page's tile reads `issued / deliverables`; a server without the plugin shows `no data (BIM Document not installed on this server)`. Fixture rows are committed through psql then deleted by the witness (they would not be visible to the WS otherwise).
**§5.36B DETAIL (written 2026-11-11 before code; T4).**
- **Legacy source:** migration script `capex/legacy/ws/bimdoc/migration/iD13/postgresql/202611111200_CAPEX-WS-BIMDOC.sql` registers ONE read type `QueryBIMDocument` (table `BIM_Document`, Action Read, Filter `1=1`; output = the columns the rule needs: `BIM_Document_ID, C_Project_ID, C_ProjectPhase_ID, C_ProjectTask_ID, DeliverableNo, Revision, DocStatus, IsActive, Name, Discipline, HoursBudget`). Same convention as `CAPEX-WS-READ` (client 11, role 102; column ids by sub-select on table/column NAME, so the script does not depend on the plugin's numeric ids). It is applied ONLY on the test instance `~/idempiere-bimdoc` (the pilot has no such table). A site without the plugin does not apply it: the read then fails and the entity is `unavailable` (existing mechanism, `adapters.js loadLegacy`).
- **Adapters:** `adapters.js` gets entity `bimdocs` (`COLS.bimdocs`, table `BIM_Document`, `LEGACY_TYPES.bimdocs='QueryBIMDocument'`); A reads `bim_document` (absent table = unavailable, as for any entity), B reads the WS type. `legacy_snapshot.js` carries it into the snapshot. The sample snapshot (pilot) has no `bimdocs` key: reads as unavailable = "not installed".
- **Tile (`tiles.js` `bim_documents`):** over the Org/Category/Status/Project-filtered projects: deliverable = latest revision per (C_Project_ID, DeliverableNo) among IsActive rows (the rule of `bim_document.mjs computeProgress`; ties on Revision by higher BIM_Document_ID); issued = those with DocStatus 'CO'. Flat value `<issued>/<deliverables>`; zero deliverables = vacuous = `no data`; entity unavailable = `no data (not installed)` (the page shows the reason; formula line states columns). Superseded (RE) rows are never deliverables.
- **Why this is the issue:** the dashboard has no tile that reads BIM Document, and Source B cannot read it at all (no read type): the twin and legacy cannot be compared on documents.
- **Witness `capex/tests/witness_dash_bimdoc.js` (W-DASH-BIMDOC), numbers by `§DASH_BIMDOC` lines:** D1 on the TEST instance (real server) the type answers and returns the committed fixture rows, count == `psql COUNT(*)`; D2 twin (SQLite `bim_document`, same fixture rows) tile == legacy tile for the same rows (TWIN); D3 supersession: rev 2 CO over rev 1 CO counts ONE deliverable and the superseded RE row is not counted; D4 tile == independent recount from the raw rows (`COUNT(latest CO)/COUNT(latest)`); D5 Source B without the entity prints `no data (not installed)` (the sample snapshot, page-level); D6 VACUOUS: a project with no deliverables prints `no data` and the witness says INCONCLUSIVE, never 0 %. Page-level check on the real `index.html` for D5/D6 via the Project selector. Break switch `BIMDOC_BREAK=1`: the count ignores supersession (counts every row) -> D3 and D4 FAIL. RED first: run the witness on the unmodified tree, it must FAIL (no type, no entity, no tile).

### 5.38 SPEC — T5, the Sysnova defaults as data (2026-11-11; written before code; the §6 PROPOSED DEFAULTS Q2, Q4, Q3 in that order)
**Rule:** the defaults are DATA in migration scripts (client 11, fixed ids + UUs, one artefact for legacy and the twin), never code; every one is labelled **proposed** (the PA measure's Name ends `(proposed)`; the page tile carries the word) until Sysnova confirms. The legacy side of every witness is the TEST instance `~/idempiere-bimdoc` (DB `idempiere_bimdoc`; fixture rows committed through psql, restored at the end), never the pilot.
- **Q2 stages — `202611111300_CAPEX-Q2-STAGES.sql`:** `C_ProjectType` "CAPEX" (ProjectCategory `G`, ids 1000100) with five `C_Phase` rows, SeqNo 10..50: Concept, Initiation, Planning, Execution, Closing (the owner's list in the §6 Q2 text, in that order). The project's stage stays `C_Project.C_Phase_ID` (no new column); the Stage tile already groups by `C_Phase.Name`. **W-CAPEX-STAGES:** S1 after the script the legacy DB and the twin hold the same 5 phases (name, SeqNo, ProjectType) in order; S2 a project whose C_Phase_ID is "Planning" shows Stage `Planning=1` on the page's engine (tiles.js) with the legacy rows; S3 applying the script a second time changes nothing (0 rows) on both sides; S4 VACUOUS: an empty population of projects at the stage prints no data. Break `STAGES_BREAK=1` drops one phase → S1 FAILS.
- **Q4 committed split — measures in `202611111310_CAPEX-PROPOSED-MEASURES.sql`:** *contract* = `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(GrandTotal),0) FROM C_Order WHERE IsSOTrx='N' AND DocStatus='CO' AND C_Project_ID>0 AND C_Currency_ID=100`; *verified* = the actual cost of projects that have at least one completed Project Issue (`SUM(ProjectBalanceAmt)` where EXISTS a `C_ProjectIssue` Processed='Y'): iDempiere itself adds an issue's cost to `ProjectBalanceAmt` (`MProjectIssue.updateBalanceAmt`), and `M_CostDetail.Amt` does NOT reproduce it (measured on the pilot: project 100, 95 issues, cost-detail sum -614.09 vs ProjectBalanceAmt 334.62, two acct schemas and signs), so ProjectBalanceAmt is the only honest column. It **overlaps the Actual Cost tile; the measure description says so.** Both are per currency (USD here, as the Approved Budget goals).
- **Q3 forecast:** EAC = Spent + (Budget - Done) / CPI, CPI = Done / Spent, i.e. `Spent + (Budget - Done) * Spent / Done`; Spent = `ProjectBalanceAmt`, Budget = `PlannedAmt`, Done = `SUM(C_ProjectPhase.PlannedAmt WHERE IsComplete='Y')`. A project with Spent = 0 or Done = 0 contributes nothing; no project with data = `no data`. Measure `CAPEX Forecast Cost at Completion USD (proposed)` over active projects (IsActive='Y', Processed='N'). The page's Forecast tile reads the same formula from `tiles.js` (`forecast_cost`, was "definition pending") from entities it already holds.
- **W-CAPEX-PROPOSED (Q4+Q3), per measure:** legacy = the measure's SQL (select + where, exactly as `MMeasureCalc.getSqlPI` joins them) run by PostgreSQL on the fixture; twin = `erp/goal_engine.js updateGoal` over the same rows copied down; tiles.js = the page value. legacy == twin; tiles == twin (Forecast). Expected numbers are also recounted by hand in the witness from the fixture figures (e.g. Spent 40, Budget 100, Done 20 → CPI 0.5 → EAC 40 + 80/0.5 = 200.00) so the SQL itself is judged, not only equal on both sides. Fixture covers: a project with Spent and Done (EAC), one with Done=0 (excluded), one closed (excluded), a completed PO on a project and a PO without project (excluded), a project with a processed Issue and one without. An empty population prints INCONCLUSIVE. Break `PROPOSED_BREAK=1` (engine value +1) → FAILS.
- **Not done by T5, named:** page tiles for contract and verified need `C_Order` and `C_ProjectIssue` read types on the legacy server (§6 Q9: the owner's go to register read types); the measures are on the Home as goals meanwhile. ⛔ BLOCKED on that one go.

### 5.37 SPEC — the SEQUEL film: from a fresh private set to the CFO's and the PM's dashboard (2026-11-11; owner; written before anything is recorded)
> Owner, 2026-11-11: *"a sequel that refreshes the Dashboard from a fresh DB (the private set is the example) that goes into the Project Order, and ends up in the Dashboard, and this time go through the important dashboard BI property values. The rest can be fast paced as previous movies already showed ... It is the project and business intelligence in the Dashboard that attracts the CFO and PM attention. Show also its editability, again just a fast snap. And its install page, the OSGi plugins for legacy and twin, granted fetching."*
**Privacy (binds the film, the script, this spec and every PR):** the data is a private Revit-exported set. Use the neutral-name copy ("Building A", storeys `Level NN`, sheets `Sheet NN`; the recipe is in the private lane file, which is never committed). No project, client or firm name, no sheet code, no coordinates on screen, in a caption, in the voice-over, in a file name, in a commit message or in a PR body; run the private-name grep on every diff, message and PR body before pushing. A name that merely appears in baked footage needs no masking (owner, 2026-11-07), but it is never said or captioned.
**Gate:** a film, a screencast or any GPU use needs the owner's go (clearance rule); stills of the REAL page with software GL (`capex/tools/film_shots.js`) need no GPU. **Only what is built is shown:** the page editor (§5.35) is specced, not built, so the "editability" snap is the Performance-goal editor that exists (W-PA-EDITOR); the project drop-down and the document tile appear only after T1 and T4 of the ▶ RESUME block are built.
**Length and pace (about 3 minutes, EN first; other languages only on the owner's word):**
| Beat | Seconds | What it shows | Pace |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Fresh start | 20 | the base database refreshed from the seed (`legacy_bimdoc_refresh.sh` lines), the neutral set loads in the Modeller, the push, the Project Order appears in the ERP | fast, three cuts: the earlier films already showed BIM loading into ERP, so no explaining |
| 2 The business intelligence | 100 | the dashboard on that order, **tile by tile, each value read from its own log line, each with its printed ƒ formula and its empty-state honesty** (below) | slow, the point of the film |
| 3 Editability | 15 | change a goal's target on the Home tab, the dial recomputes (W-PA-EDITOR) | one fast snap |
| 4 Install | 20 | the twin's Plugins & Releases (BIM Document and the dashboard bundle Active; the grant before the code is fetched) and the legacy side (the 2Pack applied, the OSGi bundle Active, the window and tabs in the Project window) | one fast snap each |
| 5 Close | 10 | card: what the CFO and the PM now see, and where to read the definitions (the guide) | |
**Beat 2, the BI property values to walk, in the order a CFO then a PM reads them (each is a number the film SAYS, so each must trace to a `§DASH_*` / `§FILM_SHOT` line of the run):** Approved Budget (`PlannedAmt`, per currency, never added across currencies) · Actual Cost = Spent (`ProjectBalanceAmt`, from completed Project Issues) and its share of budget · Committed Cost: contract and verified (§6 Q4 default, labelled *proposed*) · **Forecast Cost at Completion** (§6 Q3 default, labelled *proposed*; with Spent or Done at zero it says "no data", and the film shows that moment once) · Budget Status (Within / Over / Not Started, the overrun amount) · then the PM's: Project Health (Green / Amber / Red and the 90 % rule on Done against Due and Spent), Schedule Performance (On Time / Delayed / At Risk, average delay in days), Physical vs Spent, Project Stage (the §6 Q2 CAPEX stages, labelled *proposed*) · and the new pair: the project selector (blank = All Projects, then the one project) and the document tile (issued for construction / deliverables, from BIM Document). **The film says plainly** that three definitions are ours, awaiting the client's confirmation.
**Where the numbers come from (non-invent):** the order is the push of the set (phases and lines from the sequence rules and the rate pack, quantities from the quantity take-off); "Done", "Due" and "Spent" need real documents, so phases are marked complete and material is issued THROUGH iDempiere's own web services, as G8 did for the pilot, never by writing the dashboard's inputs. Prerequisite task: a progress script for the fresh order (a second run refuses, as `pilot_progress.js` does); the film's figures are then whatever that run produced, said as read.
**Witness W-FILM-CAPEX-SEQ (a script-lint, runs before any recording):** F1 every number in the narration TSV appears as a value on a `§FILM_SHOT` or `§DASH_*` line of the same run, exact; F2 no private-name or sheet-code hit in the TSV, the cards, the captions, the file names (the grep from the privacy rule, reported with its count); F3 every shown feature has its witness green on the day (the project selector, the document tile, the goal editor); F4 the three proposed definitions are captioned *proposed* wherever they are shown; F5 VACUOUS guard: an empty `§FILM_SHOT` set is INCONCLUSIVE. Break switch: change one spoken number, F1 must FAIL.
**Build (after the owner's go, and after T1, T4 and T5 of the ▶ RESUME block, so the film shows built things):** stills and cards with the PLAYBOOK C layout (`capex/tools/film_shots.js`, `capex/tools/film_cards/`, TSV `prompts/film_narration_capex_addon_*`, chapter cards), narration per `FILM_NARRATION.md` PLAYBOOK, one recorder per beat, the numbers read from the logs, never typed.

### 5.40 SPEC — the fold takes the WHOLE set, and every element without a priced box is accounted for (2026-11-11; owner: "1 as u recommended", "Need a healing script? That can be another good verb script ... for reuse in similar cases"; written before code)
**Issue 1 (measured, spec A):** `fold_demo.js` folded `SELECT DISTINCT building ... LIMIT 1`; the set holds IFC_ARC and IFC_STR as two `elements_meta.building` values, so every figure so far was the ARC half (2,165,862 of about 7.06 M USD). **Fix:** `elementQty`/`compute5D` accept a list of buildings; `fold_demo.js` folds ALL buildings of the DB into ONE project whose Value/Name is `FOLD_LABEL` (default: the first building).
**Issue 2 (measured today on `Building_A.db`):** 221 elements enter no price and the fold said nothing: 215 have no `element_transforms` row = 161 IfcRoof + 37 IfcCurtainWall + 9 IfcStair that are AGGREGATES (their parts carry the geometry and are priced as their own classes: giving a parent a box would double-count) + 5 IfcStair + 2 IfcBuildingElementProxy + 1 IfcSpace with NO geometry and no parts; and 6 have a box with a zero side. **Verb 1 `bim.coverage_audit` (built here as `fold_inputs.coverageAudit`):** every element with no usable box is classed `AGGREGATE_PRICED_VIA_PARTS` (parent in `rel_aggregates`/`element_parts` with at least one child that has a box; prints children count), `NO_GEOMETRY` (no transform, no parts) or `ZERO_DIM`; one `§COVERAGE class=... n=...` line per (building, ifc_class, class) and `§COVERAGE_VERDICT unclassified=0|N`; the fold REFUSES to finish when unclassified > 0 (a silent drop is the defect). **Verb 2 `bim.heal_bbox` (SPECIFIED, not built):** only for `ZERO_DIM`/`NO_GEOMETRY` elements that own a mesh instance (`element_instances` -> `component_geometries.vertices`): box = min/max of the vertices through the element's transform; never invented, never for an aggregate; each healed element logs `§HEAL_BBOX guid class from=mesh`; the rest stay listed as gaps ("14 elements unpriced" is a number the dashboard may show). Both are door verbs for any building DB (reuse, per the NEURO verb contract), no rule specific to this set.
**Witness W-COVERAGE (`capex/tests/witness_coverage_audit.js`, on a building DB given as argument; INCONCLUSIVE when the DB is missing or nothing is unpriced):** C1 the audit's class counts equal an INDEPENDENT recount by a different SQL (no-transform and zero-dim counts, aggregate = has children with a box); C2 `unclassified = 0`; C3 the whole-set fold's priced elements + audited-unpriced elements == all elements of `elements_meta` (nothing falls between); C4 a project folded from all buildings is greater than from ARC alone. Break `COVERAGE_BREAK=1`: the audit ignores `element_parts` (aggregates counted as NO_GEOMETRY) -> C1 FAILS.
**Film note (owner):** the film may say the whole chain is editable: rates in the rate pack tables, quantities from the documents; the viewer gets how they fit. The honest label stays: quantity-true to model geometry (bbox), rate-example (RS Means US 2024), not an actual cost estimate.

Whole-set fold (RS Means US 2024 example rates, bbox quantities): planned 7,063,322.98 USD (was 2,165,862 = the ARC half), 8 phases, 28 tasks, 78 lines, 360 elements outside any 4D task (phase Unsequenced). **Spec review (holes the findings uncover; each needs the spec text re-read, then an owner or higher-model decision):**
- **S1 (F2, §6 Q3/Q4):** the Q3 forecast formula and the Q4 'verified' definition assume Spent and Done share a basis. State it in the spec: Spent must be costed from the order's own products (cost price = the rate used in the plan) or the tiles must print 'basis differs'. Today `M_Cost` for fold products is empty, so real issues use unrelated seed costs.
- **S2 (F3, §6 Q1):** PV (and so SPI, Health, Schedule 'at risk') must be time-phased, and Health must not read green while a due phase is not complete. `tiles.js evm()` and `FORMULA.health/schedule` change together; W-PA-TILES and the bucket measures G4 (SQL twins) change with them.
- **S3 (F4):** decide whether walker-placed devices belong in 5D; if yes the fold needs their rows, if no the film and guide must say 'extracted model elements only'.
- **S4 (F5, F1):** a phrase table per tile (formula -> the sentences a narration may use) and a lint that compares; plus a spoken-number checker (words -> number, honouring 'about' / 'just over').
- **S5 (F6, F7):** a lease/lock on the shared legacy test instance, and a committed, witnessed setup (`§SETUP_VERDICT`) that a film refuses to run without; or a separate film instance/DB.
- **S6 (F12):** whether elements outside any 4D task belong in a phase; the 'phases sum to the total' identity must hold for a real reason, not because Unsequenced is 0.
**Does the dashboard contain what the Sysnova sample screen asks? (owner question 2026-11-11, answered from the spec, no work done):** in essence yes for what the sample SHOWS: the title, the six-item left menu, the Org / Category / Status filters, Last Updated, the seven summary tiles and the five panels are all there (guide 'How close the page is to the design it follows'). The differences are all named: Forecast, the contract/verified split of Committed Cost and the CAPEX Project Stage are OUR proposed definitions (data rows, labelled proposed, awaiting Sysnova); Project Health's thresholds are provisional (§6 Q1; F3 says the PV definition under it is defective); Procurement Risks and Handover are 'definition pending'; money is per currency, not one currency. We ADDED a Project selector and a BIM Documents tile the sample does not have. So: the layout and every tile are covered, but three of the tiles Sysnova will look at hardest (Forecast, Health, Committed split) rest on definitions that are proposed or, for Health, defective, and the figures behind them (F2) are not yet on one basis: coverage of the screen is complete, coverage of the MEANING is not.
**Would Sysnova's forecast be close to Planned? (owner question 2026-11-11; answered from the spec and arithmetic, Sysnova's own formula is still unknown, §6 Q3 / Q-S5 unanswered):** probably yes in the common case, under either formula we named. (a) 'Planned + overrun': forecast = Planned while the project is within budget. (b) EAC = Spent + (Budget - Done) / CPI with CPI = Done / Spent: when cost performance is about 1 (Spent about equal to the value done), EAC = Spent + (Budget - Done) = Budget, i.e. Planned again; it moves away from Planned only when CPI differs from 1. Our demo gave 7,459.45 against 7,063,322.98 planned ONLY because of F2 (CPI 946.9, a basis mismatch), not because the formula is wrong. Rule worth adding to the spec (S1): a forecast far from Planned with a CPI far from 1 is a data-basis alarm, not a result; print 'basis differs' next to it. What to ask Sysnova: their formula, and one project's Planned, Spent, Done and Forecast from their screen, so we can test ours against a real row.
### 5.43 NOTE FOR THE NEXT SESSION — forecast as "Planned OVER TIME": should the 4D schedule be captured as an extended sub-tab of each Phase? (owner idea 2026-11-11: *"Forecast could be Planned OVER time, thus the Phase timeline in 4D schedule. Project Order I assume has no time factor whereas the BIM 4D schedule source of truth already has it. Note this so new session can think whether to capture the 4D schedule as extended sub tab to each Phase."*) Written as measured facts and open questions; nothing decided or built.
**Measured today (Building A order, `foldall.db` and the persisted 4D run):**
- The Project Order has SOME time: `C_ProjectPhase` carries StartDate and EndDate (7 of 8 phases dated; 'Unsequenced' has none). `C_ProjectTask` has NO date columns at all (its columns: SeqNo, Name, Qty, PlannedAmt, CommittedAmt, M_Product_ID, ...). So below the phase the Project Order is atemporal, and each phase is only a [start, end] bar with ONE planned amount.
- The 4D source of truth (`scripts/cache_4d_run.js` run, `run.json`) holds 28 tasks, each with phase, storey, `sDays`, `eDays` (days from the project start) and the element guids it builds (e.g. `TASK_Substructure_Level_01`, days 0-1, 28 elements). The fold already uses it (`fold_inputs.schedule4D`, one `C_ProjectTask` per 4D task) but keeps only the task's NAME and planned amount, and rolls the dates up to the phase: the task-level time is thrown away at the fold.
- Consequence for the dashboard: PV (planned value to date) is computed from phases only, as a step function (a phase counts once its END has passed) -> finding F3 (SPI 65.9; a linear PV over the same phase dates gives 0.99). A real 'planned over time' curve (S-curve) needs the task-level dates the fold discards.
**Why this matters beyond F3:** the owner's reading is that the CLIENT's 'Forecast' may itself be planned-over-time (the forecast curve against the plan curve), not a single EAC number. If so, the single-figure tile we built (Q3) answers a different question and the time-phased plan is the missing input. Ask Sysnova (with §6 Q3 / Q-S5): is Forecast a number or a curve, and is it per month?
**Options for the next session to weigh (not ranked by evidence yet):**
1. **A 'Schedule' sub-tab on Phase** (an extended child tab: one row per 4D task, columns start day / end day / start date / end date / planned amount / elements), generic AD tab over a new table (e.g. `BIM_PhaseSchedule`, like `BIM_Document` was added: a plugin model + the same workbook route), filled by the fold from the 4D run. Keeps `C_ProjectTask` stock and iDempiere-compatible. PV then = sum over rows of planned x clamp((today - start)/(end - start)); the dashboard reads the table like any other entity (new optional read type, 'not installed' when absent, exactly the pattern of `bimdocs`).
2. **Dates on `C_ProjectTask`** (a column added by migration, `IsSyncDatabase=Y`): simplest for the page, but it changes a stock table the legacy side must also carry (TWIN rule: SQLite == legacy; a plugin column is allowed, a divergence is not).
3. **Do not copy the schedule; read it at the source** (the persisted 4D run) when the page needs the S-curve: no new ERP table, but the page then depends on a BIM-side file, which breaks 'every number is a row of the ERP' (the lane's own principle, §4).
4. **Phase-level only, now:** make PV linear over each PHASE's existing start/end (no new data), which already fixes F3; task-level stays a later step.
**Constraints the next session must honour:** AD-LAYER LAW (a generic AD tab, not per-window code); TWIN cardinal rule (legacy and twin carry the same rows; W-PA-TILES / PA measures G4 are the legacy SQL twins of the bucket tiles and must change with any PV change); the Project Order is created from the model and 'a changed model pushed twice yields one order' (T6) so the schedule rows must follow the same version-tag rule; the 4D run is keyed on the code files (a stale cache is impossible) so a fold must name the run key it read.
**Decisions needed (owner or higher model):** (a) Forecast as number or curve (ask Sysnova); (b) option 1 vs 2 vs 4; (c) whether a re-pushed model with a new programme replaces the rows (alpha rule: 'new documents version tagged, old simply replaced').
**Grid view for the film windows, and the legacy-parity note (owner 2026-11-11: "the screen res has to be higher ... remove the irrelevant first 3 and last 5 columns. Legacy has a tab column editor; if we don't, make note on legacy parity and just make it not visible via System.AD_Tab"):** the twin DOES have the legacy tab column editor: the toolbar More > **Customize** dialog is a port of `CustomizeGridViewPanel` (`erp/idempiere.html:5879 _openCustomize`, `erp/ad_gridtab.js:233` `customizeLists`/`tabCustSave`, log `§GRID-CUSTOM`); it writes ONE signed op to `ad_tab_customization` for the signed-in user (legacy `MTabCustomization.saveData`), so no `AD_Field`/`AD_Tab` dictionary row is edited and no divergence from legacy exists. No parity gap, nothing to hide through System. The film uses exactly that dialog (driven by `capex/tools/film_erp_shots.js customize()`, `§FILM_ERP_CUSTOM before=34 kept=[...]`). Reading of 'first 3 and last 5': in the zoomed-out grid the first three visible columns were Business Partner, BPartner (Agent), Partner Location and the trailing ones were quantity/balance/organisation columns; the still now keeps ONLY the relevant band, so it is stricter: Project grid = Search Key, Name, Currency, Planned Amount, Planned Quantity, Planned Margin, Committed Amount, Invoiced Amount, Project Balance; Phase grid = Sequence, Name, Complete, Start Date, End Date, Planned Amount, Committed Amount. Viewport 1920x1080 at device scale 1.5 (2880x1620), sighted: the text, the red outline (Planned Amount column) and the figure 7063322.98018 / the 8 phase values are legible at 1920x1080. Film now v4 (`CAPEX_Dashboard_Sequel_v4_USD_narrated_EN_2026-11-11.mp4`, 168.8 s, 4220 frames, 15/15 DETAIL, longest silence 6.8 s, -17.0 LUFS; v3 deleted as superseded). The project balance shows 0 in the ERP store because the actual documents live on the legacy side (the twin holds the plan): worth one line in the narration or the guide if a viewer asks.
**Legacy status-bar grand total, and does the twin need one? (owner 2026-11-11; read from the legacy source `org.adempiere.ui.zk/.../adwindow/StatusBar.java`, `GridView.java` and the pilot's dictionary, nothing built):** legacy has NO automatic grand total or column sum in the window status bar and none in the grid footer (`GridView.gridFooter` is the paging control only). What legacy has is **AD_StatusLine**: the status bar's EAST panel renders HTML produced by a configured SQL (`AD_StatusLine.SQLStatement`, linked to windows/tabs through `AD_StatusLineUsedIn`; the WEST part is the message line). The pilot carries 7 such rows and 14 usages: `OrderSummary` (Lines, TotalLines, GrandTotal, currency), `InvoiceSummary`, `InvoiceBatchSummary`, `ExpenseSummary` (document-level totals for the Order, Invoice, Invoice Batch and Expense windows) and the dashboard/tenant-message ones. NONE is attached to the Project window or its Phase grid, so legacy shows no total under the Phase grid either: the 'outlined column adds up to 7063322.98' is something the FILM shows, not something legacy's screen prints. **Twin gap (measured):** the twin's seed has NO `ad_statusline` / `ad_statuslineusedin` table and no code that renders a status-bar info panel from them (grep of `erp/*.js`, `erp/idempiere.html`, the seed: none), so the Order/Invoice summary line legacy shows is missing in the twin: a TWIN-rule item (same operations, same L&F) independent of this film, found while answering. Spec question S7: port AD_StatusLine (a generic rule: the status bar renders whatever AD_StatusLineUsedIn names for the tab, per the AD-LAYER LAW, not a hard-coded Order total); then, separately and as an ADDITION on both sides, decide whether a selected-rows sum is wanted (legacy does not have it).
**Clean-up 2026-11-11 (owner: "remove the working files and old videos"):** scratch `/tmp/film_seq` removed (233 MB); kept under `~/.cache/capex_film/seq_2026-11-11/`: `recipe/` (the test-instance setup, `copytypes.py`, the id-shifted PUSH/PA scripts, the project delete), `specs/` (A cost, B capture, C documents), `data/` (`foldall.db`, `legacy_all.json`), `logs/`, `shots*/`. Videos removed: the sequel v1 (rejected) and v2 (withdrawn), BIM Document EN v1 and v2 and their silent v1/v2, Think_Global_Act_Local rc1-rc3; kept: the sequel v4, the BIM Document v3 films and silent v3 masters, rc4. Two worktrees are NOT removed because their commits are unpushed: `/tmp/wt-film-seq` (bim-ootb, `feat/capex-film-seq`) and `/tmp/wt-guide-src` (bim-compiler, `docs/capex-guide-sources`).
**For a new session — is a summary status bar viable where detail rows carry amounts? (owner 2026-11-11; question, not a decision):** the idea: under any grid or tab whose rows carry money (Phase grid, Order or Invoice lines, Project lines, Budget), the status bar shows a summary: row count and the total per currency, so a viewer sees 'these eight rows = 7,063,322.98 USD' without adding. **Facts:** legacy has no such automatic total (status bar east panel = `AD_StatusLine` SQL rows via `AD_StatusLineUsedIn`; none for Project/Phase; see the note above). **Viability to assess:** (1) *Route A, legacy-compatible and DATA only:* port `AD_StatusLine` into the twin (S7), then the summary is one row per tab, e.g. 'ProjectPhaseSummary' = `SELECT COUNT(*), SUM(PlannedAmt) FROM C_ProjectPhase WHERE C_Project_ID=@C_Project_ID@` attached to the tab, shipped as a migration script that legacy and twin apply identically (the pattern of the CAPEX PA rows): no per-window code, TWIN rule kept. (2) *Route B, generic by column type:* the engine sums every column whose AD reference is Amount/Cost/Price per currency for the rows the grid shows, for ANY window with no row to configure (the AD-LAYER LAW's preferred shape); but it is an ADDITION legacy lacks, so the twin would then DIFFER from legacy unless the owner records it as an extension the legacy site can also install (a plugin), which collides with 'no accepted divergence' and needs the owner's own words. (3) *Questions:* sum what the grid SHOWS (filtered, paged) or the selected rows (iDempiere's Info windows do a selection sum); one currency per sum (never add across currencies: the dashboard's rule), so a multi-currency grid prints one figure per currency; amounts that are not additive (a unit price, a percentage, a balance) must not be summed: the AD column type alone may not tell (Price vs Amount); a parent-with-children grid (phase lines under phases) must not count a child twice; cost of the extra query on a large grid; mobile width. (4) *Test shape:* a witness that recomputes the sum from the rows independently, a break switch, and VACUOUS (no amount column, or zero rows, prints nothing, never 'total 0'). **Recommendation to test, not to take as given:** do S7 first (it is legacy parity), then add the summary as AD_StatusLine DATA rows for a few windows (Route A); revisit Route B only if the owner states it is wanted as an extension.
**OWNER PROPOSAL 2026-11-11 (settles Route B of the status-bar question above, subject to the next session's review): the summary status bar belongs to the FRIENDLY plugin.** `erp/plugins/friendly_ops.mjs` (spec bim-compiler `prompts/ERP_FRIENDLY_OPS_LENS.md`) is the opt-in lens whose own header states the parity rule: *"Installed = lens ON; stopped = the plain legacy twin"*, it names no table, window or form (every action is derived from the record on screen and the dictionary), and it already carries a count-style feature (FO-B related counts). A summary status bar fits that shape exactly: with the lens STOPPED the twin's status bar is legacy's (nothing added: the twin == legacy rule holds by default); with it ON, a kernel-only summary appears (rows shown, and the total per currency of the tab's additive amount columns), so the extension is switched, labelled and removable, not a silent divergence. **What this resolves from the open list:** the parity objection to Route B (an addition legacy lacks) is answered the way the lens already answers it for FO-M1..FO-B; the owner's own words (this proposal) are the recorded permission for this one extension. **What it leaves open for the next session:** (1) S7 (port `AD_StatusLine`) stays CORE and default-on, because legacy HAS it: the two are different things and must not be merged; (2) write the section (suggest FO-C in the lens spec) before code: the rule for 'additive' (AD reference Amount/Cost vs Price/Percent/Quantity; a balance is not summed), per-currency grouping, grid-rows-shown vs selected, parent/child double counting, VACUOUS (no amount column or no rows: print nothing, never 'total 0'); (3) a witness with the lens OFF asserting the status bar is byte-identical to legacy's (the parity half, as important as the feature half), the lens ON recomputing the sum independently, and a break switch; (4) whether the toggle is the plugin's installed state only (the lens's present rule) or also a per-user preference; (5) the Phase grid of the film is the first demo: 8 rows, 7063322.98018 USD.
**Triage order for the higher model:** F2+S1 and F3+S2 (they change what the tiles mean), then F6/F7+S5 (they decide whether any run is reproducible), then F1/F5+S4 (words), F14 (privacy), F4+S3, F12+S6; the rest are named, small or closed.
## 6. ⛔ Questions for the client (via the owner; Q1 thresholds map to `PA_ColorSchema` once R3 lands) (formula not stated, no honest iDempiere column)
Q1 Health Green/Amber/Red thresholds (we use SPI/CPI 0.9 provisional, only 0.9 is theirs) and whether health uses cost (CPI) at all.
Q2 Project Stage: how do Concept / Initiation / Planning / Execution / Closing map onto `C_Project.C_Phase_ID` (stock `C_Phase` rows are sales-cycle steps in the seed)? Or is it a custom column/`C_ProjectType`?
  **PROPOSED DEFAULT 2026-11-11:** a `C_ProjectType` "CAPEX" whose standard phases (`C_Phase`) are the five stages; the stage is `C_Project.C_Phase_ID`, no new column (the Set Project Type process is already ported). Shipped as a migration script (data). Work phases pushed from the BIM model (storey/discipline) stay separate. Question to Sysnova: the five names and their order. Order of work: Q2, then Q4, then Q3 (the forecast needs the actual cost settled).
Q3 Forecast Cost at Completion: formula (EAC = AC + (BAC-EV)/CPI? or Planned + overrun?) and its source columns.
  **PROPOSED DEFAULT 2026-11-11 (owner: "if we are fitting into idempiere convention, that should be the consistent way"; Sysnova's dashboard is a prototype, so we define each field in the guide and they review):** EAC = Spent + (Approved Budget - Done) / CPI, CPI = Done / Spent (Spent = `ProjectBalanceAmt`, Done = EV, both already on the page); Spent or Done = 0 -> `no data`. A PA measure row, so the formula is editable data. Question to Sysnova: their formula, or Budget / CPI?
Q4 Committed split "verified vs contract": which columns (`C_ProjectLine.CommittedAmt` vs invoiced? `C_Order` PO totals?).
  **PROPOSED DEFAULT 2026-11-11:** contract = completed purchase orders on the project (`C_Order` IsSOTrx=N, DocStatus CO, by `C_Project_ID`); the project's `CommittedAmt` is typed by hand and a PO never fills it (landmine above). Verified = completed `C_ProjectIssue` cost, which iDempiere itself adds to `ProjectBalanceAmt` (`MProjectIssue.java:583`), so it overlaps the Actual Cost tile: say so on the tile. Question to Sysnova: is verified delivery, or invoice?
Q5 Approval Pending: which workflow activities count (all `AD_WF_Activity` suspended? only on project documents? which AD_Table)?
Q6 Procurement Risks and Handover: definition and source columns.
Q7 "Closed" project = `Processed='Y'`? and "completed this year" date = `DateFinish`? Also the BIM link shape (`db=` form vs `building=` form).
Q8 Payment-based financial progress (their own caveat says it is not in the ERP) — which table will carry it?
Q9 Legacy read types for C_ProjectPhase / C_ProjectTask / C_ProjectLine / C_Phase / C_Currency / AD_WF_Activity: owner's go to register them on the legacy server (WS Type rows) so Source B is complete.
**For the Sysnova PM (owner's ask 2026-11-03: "their script and whether it is live or still prototype"):**
Q-S1 Please share the dashboard's source code (the v2 app at `:8000/v2`, and v1 if it still runs) — or a zip / repo access.
Q-S2 Is it LIVE on real Kazi Farms data today, or a prototype on sample/test data? (Footer says "Live data from iDempiere" — which iDempiere: production or a copy?)
  **ANSWER (Sysnova PM, via the owner, 2026-11-03): still a PROTOTYPE.** → we proceed with ours (TASK 1, §5.13).
Q-S3 How does it read iDempiere — straight from the PostgreSQL database (SQL / views), through iDempiere web services, or through a REST plugin? Read-only user?
Q-S4 How do users sign in to it — its own login, the iDempiere login, or none (LAN only)?
Q-S5 For each tile, the formula or SQL it uses (answers §6 Q1–Q8 in one go), and what "Procurement Risks" and "Handover" show.
Q-S6 Where does it run (server, port 8000, what starts it) and who maintains it.

### 5.44 SPEC — S2 narrow: Project Health is never Green while a phase is overdue (2026-11-12; written before code; answers §5.42 F3 in part)
**Issue it disproves:** a project whose Substructure phase is past its EndDate and not complete showed Health Green (F3: SPI 65.9 because the step-function PV counts only phases already due and EV counts a not-yet-due phase ticked early). **Rule (ours, labelled proposed, one question to Sysnova):** a project that the index rule would call Green is Amber when it has any phase with IsComplete='N' and EndDate < today. Amber and Red are unchanged. This is the Schedule tile's own "Delayed" test (`overdue > 0`), so the two tiles can no longer contradict each other. **Not changed:** the PV definition itself (step vs time-phased): that needs Sysnova's formula (§6 Q1), so it stays ⛔ open; changing it silently would move SPI everywhere. **Where:** `capex/tiles.js` health + FORMULA.health; legacy measures 1000023 (Green) and 1000024 (Amber) by migration `202611121000_CAPEX-S2-HEALTH-OVERDUE.sql` (UPDATE of PA_MeasureCalc.WhereClause; append-only ledger, G4 file untouched); user guide row. **Witness:** W-PA-TILES (twin engine == tiles.js on the three dates, the new script in SCRIPTS) plus W-HEALTH-OVERDUE `capex/tests/witness_health_overdue.js`: H1 a project with an overdue incomplete phase and Green indices is Amber, not Green (RED on the old tiles.js); H2 the same project with that phase ticked is Green; H3 no overdue phase leaves Green as before; `HEALTH_BREAK=1` restores the old rule and H1 must FAIL; a population with no Green-index project prints INCONCLUSIVE.

### 5.45 SPEC — S1 narrow: the Forecast prints "basis differs" when Spent and Done are not on one basis (2026-11-12; written before code; answers §5.42 F2)
**Issue it disproves:** the Forecast tile showed 7,459 against a 7,063,323 budget as if it were a result; the cause was a CPI of 946.9 (issued material at seed costs vs rate-pack budget). **Rule (ours, bound proposed):** over the active projects that enter the Forecast, if Done ÷ Spent (CPI) is < 0.5 or > 2 the tile carries `basisDiffers = <count>` and the page prints "basis differs" under the figure. The figure itself is unchanged (twin == legacy measure 1000042 stays). **Not done, ⛔ Sysnova:** costing issues from the order's own rates (needs M_Cost for fold products, a data decision). **Witness:** `capex/tests/witness_basis_differs.js` B1 CPI 946 → flagged (RED before), B2 CPI 1.0 → not flagged, B3 flatten/twin string unchanged, `BASIS_BREAK` n/a (B2 is the falsifier of an always-on flag).

### 5.46 SPEC + RESULT — F6/F7: the test instance gets a committed, witnessed setup and a gate (2026-11-12)
**Cause of F6 (found):** `bim-ootb common/door/tests/witness_legacy_plugin.js` runs `legacy.refresh` on purpose (it tests the refresh), which drops and rebuilds `idempiere_bimdoc` from the base seed; nothing locks the DB, so any CAPEX witness or film that shares it loses its data. Not a loop or a cron: no timer exists; the last journal is 2026-11-11 16:07. **Fix (ours, no change to the door lane's file):** (1) `capex/legacy/test_instance/setup_test_instance.sh` re-applies every CAPEX migration (now incl. the S2 one), the pilot-remapped WS types (`copytypes.py`, ids shifted, scripts committed), the BIM Document and Q4 read types, then `update_sequences()`; `--check` applies nothing and prints `§SETUP_VERDICT PASS|FAIL` from independent reads (6 WS types, 5 PA measures, the §5.44 test in Health Green, the BIM_Document table). (2) `capex/tests/_setup_gate.js`: W-CAPEX-PROPOSED and W-DASH-BIMDOC print INCONCLUSIVE and stop when the verdict is FAIL. **Result:** `--check` FAILED (9 missing) on the refreshed-away instance, setup run → PASS, server restarted (PA measures are cached), W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0 and W-DASH-BIMDOC 8/0 (they had 4 fails and a "not installed" before). **Still open:** the door lane could call the setup after its own refresh (a line in `legacy_cli.js`); that file is not ours.

### 5.47 SPEC + RESULT — S7: AD_StatusLine in the twin's status bar (core, default on; TWIN rule) (2026-11-12)
**Correction to §5.42's measurement:** the twin's seed patch already held `ad_statusline` (7 rows) and `ad_message`; what was missing was the link table `ad_statuslineusedin` and any code that rendered a line. **Spec (ported from legacy source, cited in the module header):** `MStatusLine.getSL` (window+tab, then window with tab NULL, then table; IsStatusLine='Y', both rows active), `parseLine` (`@Col@` from the current record, an unresolved one gives null; first row; number/choice formats get numbers, others strings; `java.text.MessageFormat`), `GridTab.getStatusLine`, and `AbstractADWindowContent :2192-2198` (only the master tab sets the info). Generic: no table name in the module or the page hook (AD-LAYER LAW). **Built:** `erp/patches/build_statuslineusedin_patch.py` (14 rows from the pilot, block in `ad_seed.db.sql`), `erp/ad_statusline.js`, a `#idmp-status-info` span + `_updateStatusInfo` in `erp/idempiere.html` (log `§STATUSLINE_UI`). **Witnesses:** `erp/tests/witness_statusline.js` 5/0 (S1: the formatter equals the REAL `java.text.MessageFormat` (java 17, Locale.US) over all 6 dictionary patterns × 5 argument vectors = 30 cases, ties/zero/negative/choice/HTML; `STATUSLINE_BREAK=1` fails S1; S2 getSL; S3 twin orders vs independent SUM/COUNT; S4 generic; S5 no key = null); `erp/tests/witness_statusline_ui.js` 5/0 on the real page: Sales Order opened by `record=<id>` for 4 orders shows each order's own line, e.g. `3 Lines - 152.00 - Total: 161.12 USD = 161.12`, the EUR order shows the converted amount by the stored active rate, Business Partner shows none. **Findings:** (1) the first Sales Order the page opens (1500003, Draft) has NULL TotalLines/GrandTotal in the seed, so it prints `null` exactly as legacy's MessageFormat would for a NULL; a data property of that seed row, not a render defect. (2) The window opens with the current record only, so stepping with Next reaches nothing; the witness uses deep links. **Not done:** Invoice/Expense windows are served by the same rows but have no UI witness of their own yet (S7 sweep "X of N windows with a usedin row", N from the dictionary: 14 usages); the selected-rows summary stays the Friendly-lens proposal (§5.42), untouched.

### 5.48 SPEC + RESULT — T6: the model version tag; a revised model REPLACES the old order (alpha rule, 2026-11-12)
**Spec (owner 2026-11-11: "new documents version tagged, old simply replaced"):** `viewer/proj_fold.js foldProjectOrder` stores a MODEL VERSION TAG in `C_Project.Description` (`BIM model version <tag>`); the tag is a 12-hex fingerprint of exactly what the fold writes from (priced rows: class, discipline, storey, count, unit, quantity, rate, task, guids; the programme: task id, phase, storey, day range, start), or `opts.modelVersion` when the caller has a better one (a source-file hash, once the extraction stores it, BIMtoProject §F gap 2). Same tag → state `same`, +0 rows. Different tag → state `replaced`: the project's lines, tasks, phases (and a still-draft `BIM PO`) are deleted and the new model folded; ids are burned first so none is reused (the signed-tombstone hazard in `_allocator`). **A project that has Project Issues (actuals) is never replaced** (`refused`, the fold stops, nothing is added beside the old rows). A project with no tag and no description is `adopted` (tag stamped, rows kept); one with its own description is `untagged` (as before). Log: `§PROJ_VERSION project= tag= state= previous= replaced=`; result field `version`. **Witness `tests/poc_proj_version.js` 8/0:** V1 new, V2 same +0, V3 a changed model (wall quantity changed, door removed, window added) → replaced, ONE project, the door line gone, the window line there, V3b PlannedAmt 27,400 = the new golden (RED control `BREAK=1`, the old behaviour: stale rows stay and PlannedAmt is 31,000 = old and new mixed, V3/V3b/V3c FAIL), V4 revised again → same, V5 actuals → refused and nothing added, V6 the tag ignores row order. **Honest limits:** (a) the seed has no `C_ProjectIssue` table, so V5 makes a minimal one in the in-memory copy; (b) **the legacy push is NOT yet replaced-on-change** (the fold's tag reaches `plan.fields` only after this item; deleting a legacy order needs delete-capable WS types): ⛔ the one question below; (c) `poc_proj_push.js` still prints `§PROJ_PUSH_CONTRACT FAIL` (`_pushToErp` absent from `navigate_find.js`) and `poc_proj_fold_proxy_disc.js` / `poc_fin_evm.js` fail identically on `main` — not caused here. **⛔ Q-T6:** should a changed model's legacy push delete the old legacy order (needs the owner's go: it removes rows from a real iDempiere), or stay add-only until the project leaves alpha?


### 5.49 SPEC — drill-down: the page is the C-level glance, every number opens the projects behind it (2026-11-12; owner: "high level single page grasp for C level officers and PMs ... details can be drilled into"; agreed design)
**Issue it disproves:** a tile or bucket shows a number and nothing behind it: the viewer cannot see WHICH projects make Health Red 3 or Actual Cost 5,367, so the glance cannot become an action. **Rule:** the page stays one screen (7 tiles + 5 panels, no new rows on it). Every count, sum and bucket is clickable and opens a side panel (the page does not navigate away) listing the projects that make it: Project, Name, currency, Approved (PlannedAmt), Spent (ProjectBalanceAmt), Due, Done, and its bucket (Health / Schedule / Budget). A project row opens that record in the system the numbers came from: legacy -> its WebUI zoom (`index.zul?Action=Zoom&TableName=C_Project&Record_ID=`, read from `AdempiereWebUI.processParameters`), twin -> the ERP Project window (`erp/idempiere.html?window=130&record=`; the Phase tab, BIM Documents and the Gantt are there). **One source:** `capex/tiles.js compute()` returns `drill`, key → project rows, filled by the SAME loops that count the tiles (no second classification, AD-layer law); keys: `total_projects`, `active_projects`, `approved_budget`, `actual_cost`, `committed_cost`, `forecast_cost`, `completed_year`, `health:<green|amber|red|none>`, `schedule:<ontime|delayed|atrisk|nosched>`, `budget:<within|over|notstarted>`, `stage:<name>`. **Witness W-DASH-DRILL (denominator = every key the tiles produce, none hand-listed):** K1 for each count key, rows listed == the number the tile shows; K2 for each money key, the listed column summed per currency == the tile's value (cents); K3 a project appears in exactly one bucket of each bucket tile and the buckets partition the active population; K4 the keys come from the tile object (a tile with a bucket the drill lacks FAILS); K5 on the real page, clicking a bucket opens the panel with that many rows, and a row's link carries `window=130&record=<id>`. `DRILL_BREAK=1` drops one project from one list: K1 must FAIL. A key whose tile is 0/vacuous prints INCONCLUSIVE for that key, never PASS. **Not in this item:** an editable page layout (§5.35), Procurement/Handover panels (definitions pending).
**RESULT §5.49 (2026-11-12): built.** `tiles.js compute()` returns `drill` (filled by the tile loops themselves); `dash.js` `openDrill` + a delegated click on `[data-drill]` (7 tiles + every Health/Schedule/Budget/Stage row = 20 clickable on the sample), side panel `#drill` (Esc/× closes); log `§DASH_DRILL_UI key= rows=`. **W-DASH-DRILL 55 judged / 0 fail / 5 inconclusive** (empty buckets): K1 list == tile for every count key at Status active/all/closed, K2 Σ money == tile (via the tile's own sum-then-round-once), K3 each active project in exactly one bucket per tile, K4 every tile bucket has a key, K5 on the real page every click opens exactly the node-computed rows and every row links to its own record; `DRILL_BREAK=1` fails K1. Link target verified in `AdempiereWebUI.java:403` (legacy `Action=Zoom&TableName=C_Project&Record_ID=`). **Limits:** the twin-source link form (`window=130&record=`) is built but the witness exercises only the legacy source; the BIM Documents tile has no project list (its count is per deliverable, not per project); Forecast lists the projects that enter the formula.

### 5.50 RESULT — the glance page: formulas and caveats behind a click (2026-11-12; owner: "high level single page grasp for C level officers and PMs"; the first drill still showed every tile carrying a formula paragraph and the Forecast a 4-line explanation: not a glance)
`dash.js note(k)` is now a collapsed `<details class="note"><summary>ƒ formula</summary>…` (the formula text is unchanged, still in the DOM, still the guide's text); the Forecast tile shows `proposed formula` and, when it applies, a one-line `⚠ basis differs` chip (the explanation is its tooltip, §5.45 unchanged); the Committed tile's "verified" line is `verified (proposed)` with the overlap note as a tooltip.
