### 5.51 SPEC — the two "definition pending" menu views get PROPOSED defaults (2026-11-12; owner: "explain each menu item on the left ... if anything not built, build them first"; rule of 2026-11-11: our defaults, labelled, one question each, so the client can read and review them)
**State measured before:** Dashboard, Projects (table), Budget & Cost (table), Approval Pending (the count of waiting workflow activities) are built; **Procurement Risks and Handover are `definition pending (client)` (§6 Q6)**: a menu item that opens an empty card. **Issue it disproves:** two of six menu entries explain nothing. **Proposed definitions (every one labelled "proposed" on the page, one question to Sysnova, Q-S6):** *Procurement Risks:* (a) **Open purchase orders** = purchase orders (`IsSOTrx='N'`) on the filtered projects whose `DocStatus` is not Completed, Closed, Voided or Reversed: count, and `SUM(GrandTotal)` per currency; (b) **Over-committed projects** = active projects whose `CommittedAmt` is above `PlannedAmt` (both known, planned > 0); (c) **Budget, nothing ordered** = active projects with `PlannedAmt` > 0 and no purchase order at all. (a) and (c) need the orders entity: "no data (not installed)" without it, never 0. *Handover:* (a) **Ready for handover** = active projects that have phases and every phase is ticked complete; (b) **Handed over** = closed projects (`Processed='Y'`); (c) the existing **BIM documents issued for construction / deliverables**. Each count opens the projects behind it (§5.49 keys `proc:openpo`, `proc:overcommitted`, `proc:nopo`, `handover:ready`, `handover:done`). **Where:** `capex/tiles.js` (one place, both sources), the two views in `capex/dash.js`, the formulas in `FORMULA` and the guide. **Not built, named:** PA measure rows for these two views (no Home-tab goal), so they have no legacy-engine twin yet; their twin == legacy check is the same read of the same rows through `tiles.js`, not a PA measure. **Witness `capex/tests/witness_proc_handover.js`:** P1-P5 hand counts on a fixture whose answers are fixed by hand (open PO count/sum, over-committed, no-PO, ready, done) — RED on the old code (the views do not exist); P6 orders entity absent -> "no data (not installed)" for (a) and (c), never 0; P7 an empty population prints INCONCLUSIVE; P8 the drill keys list exactly the counted projects; `PH_BREAK=1` counts a voided PO as open: P1 must FAIL. W-DASH-DRILL gains the five keys (K1).

### 5.52 SPEC + RESULT — the Dashboard-alone film, with the left menu explained (2026-11-12; owner: "a film just on Dashboard alone, with a slight preamble this comes from BIM to ERP Project Order, then drill in, then show how it works on both Kernel ERP and legacy iDempiere following the PA data model" · "explain each menu panel on the left" · "if anything not built, build them first")
**Built first (§5.51):** Procurement Risks and Handover were "definition pending"; they now carry proposed, labelled definitions (W-PROC-HANDOVER 8/0), and every menu view has a one-line lead (`dash.js LEAD`). **Screenplay (183.0 s, 4575 frames, 14 voiced rows, 6 plain cards):** preamble = the ERP Project grid and Phase grid of the order (7,063,322.98, 8 phases; stills of the 2026-11-11 run) → the glance (3 projects, approved, spent, health amber 1, the Forecast's "basis differs" warning said aloud) → drill: Approved Budget (3 rows), Amber (the order: a phase overdue and not complete → cannot be green; schedule delayed), Actual Cost, the ƒ formula one click away → the six menu items (Projects, Budget & Cost, Procurement Risks (proposed), Approval Pending (ten waiting), Handover (proposed; nothing ready; no drawing register on this server); the Dashboard is the glance itself) → two runtimes: a drawn PA_Measure → PA_MeasureCalc → PA_Goal → Home chain, Kernel ERP (SQLite, rows loaded as data) vs legacy iDempiere (PostgreSQL, rows applied as SQL), then the proof card: W-PA-TILES 89 compared / 0 differ and W-DASH-DRILL 59 / 0 failed, read from the run's logs. **Files:** `capex/tools/film_dash_shots.js` (stills of the REAL page, software GL, `§FILM_DASH_SHOT` + page-text `§FILM_PIXEL_PRIVACY` per shot), `capex/tools/film_dash_build.sh` (cards, PA diagram, proof card, segments), `capex/tools/film_cards/narration_dash_en.tsv`, `capex/tests/witness_film_dash.js`. **Voice:** Kokoro, local CPU, `film_narration_fit_kokoro_v3.py` (no cloud, no paid call), fit 14/14 DETAIL, 0 tone WRONG after rewording two questions. **Witness W-FILM-DASH 5/0** (N1 every spoken number-word is backed by a value in its row's source, 13 spoken; `FILM_BREAK=1` ten→eleven FAILS; N2 every source § line is on a log of the run; N3 no private-name hit and 10/10 shots have a page-text privacy line, hits=0; N4 proposed definitions and the forecast warning are said in their own rows; N5 all six menu items are covered; "two systems" is the one listed structural number). **Measured on the file `~/Videos/CAPEX_Dashboard_Alone_narrated_EN_2026-11-12.mp4`:** frames 4575 == the silent source, duration 183.000 s, longest silences 4.1-4.9 s at chapter cards and 6.6 s at the end card, -17.0 LUFS, bottom 230 px of every picture equal to the background (0.1046). **Honest limits:** the ERP stills of the order (Project grid, Phase grid) are from the 2026-11-11 run, not re-shot; the dashboard data is that run's snapshot (3 projects: the order plus two stock ones; its Handover is empty, the Procurement lines nearly empty); there is no recording of the Home tab on either system (the drawn chain and the witness counts carry that claim); the two proposed views have no PA measure yet, so they sit outside the "89 compared" figure; English only (other languages on the owner's word); no GPU, no screencast.

### 5.53 SPEC — themes: one data model, a choice of look (2026-11-12; owner: "Is it viable that these become themes where the underlying is same, for user to choose?" → "Yes"; the design is the mock-ups A/B/C proposed the same day, after research of modern portfolio dashboards)
**Issue it disproves:** the page has one fixed look; a C-level reader and a PM want different things on the same numbers, and a redesign must not risk a number. **Rule:** a theme never computes: `tiles.js compute()` (and its `drill`) stays the single source for both runtimes. Two levels. **Skin** = colour tokens only (`light`, `dark`; `data-skin` on `<body>`). **Layout** = which shared pieces are drawn and where: `classic` (today's tiles and panels: the default, unchanged) and `executive` (hero strip of four stat tiles with meters, health as one segmented bar, portfolio map, needs-attention list, stage bars). The project page with tabs (mock-up C) is not a theme; it comes after, the same in every theme. **Choice:** `?theme=classic|executive` and `?skin=light|dark`, two selects in the header, remembered in `localStorage` (a failed read/write changes nothing); per-user storage (`PA_DashboardPreference`, §5.35) is later. **Portfolio map:** one bubble per active project that has a planned amount; x = days behind (the longest overdue incomplete phase), y = budget spent as a share of plan; colour = Health bucket and never colour alone (the segmented bar and the tooltip carry the word); bubble SIZE = planned amount only when every plotted project shares one currency, otherwise equal size and the card says "size not shown: several currencies" (amounts of different currencies are never compared); projects with no planned amount are listed under the map as "not plotted: N", never dropped silently. Hover or keyboard focus shows a tooltip (value first, label second: planned, spent, days behind, the bucket and its reason from the same rows); click or Enter opens the drill panel on that project; a **Table view** button shows the same rows as a table. **Needs attention** = Red then Amber active projects with their reason taken from the rows (`N days behind` from the schedule bucket, `spent above plan` from the budget bucket; no reason is written when neither applies). **Not built, named:** trend arrows and sparklines (no history is stored), the project page tabs, per-user theme storage, the table-first analyst layout. **Witness W-THEME (`capex/tests/witness_theme.js`, real page, both layouts × both skins):** T1 every `[data-flat]` value on the page == `flatten()` of the same compute, in all four combinations; T2 the Executive health segments == the Health tile's counts and the plotted bubbles + "not plotted" == the active projects; T3 each bubble's tooltip numbers == its drill row; T4 clicking a bubble, a segment and an attention row opens the panel with exactly the node-computed rows; T5 the Table view lists the same projects as the bubbles; T6 the classic layout is byte-identical in its `[data-flat]` set to before (W-CAPEX-SAMPLE still 8/0); T7 the preference survives a reload and a blocked `localStorage` leaves the default; T8 the colour tokens of both skins pass `validate_palette.js` against their surface. `THEME_BREAK=1` moves one bubble's y by 5 points: T3 must FAIL. A layout with zero plotted projects prints INCONCLUSIVE.
**RESULT §5.53 (2026-11-12): built (step 1 of the proposal).** `dash.js`: `THEMES`/`SKINS`, `pref()` (URL param, then `localStorage`, then default; a throwing storage changes nothing), `applySkin()`, shared pieces (`meter`, `segBar`, `bubbleMap`, `showTip`, `reasonOf`), `dashboardClassic()` (the old code, renamed, unchanged) and `dashboardExecutive()`; `openDrillRows()` opens one project from a bubble, an attention row or a table row; selects `Layout` and `Skin` in the header; `tiles.js info()` gained `late` (longest overdue incomplete phase, days). **W-THEME 11 judged / 0 fail** on the real page: T1 22 numbers (classic) and 7 (executive) == `flatten()` in all four layout × skin combinations; T2 health segments == tile buckets (0/2/1/6), 7 plotted + 2 not plotted == 9 active; T3 every bubble's data, drawn y (scale taken from two gridlines) and tooltip numbers == its drill row, and `THEME_BREAK=1` (one bubble moved 5 px) FAILS it; T4 clicks open exactly 1 / 2 / 1 rows; T5 table view lists the 7; T7 preference survives a reload and blocked storage falls back; T8 segment label contrast >= 3:1 in both skins (dark label on the status fill). Regression: W-CAPEX-SAMPLE 8/0, W-DASH-PROJSEL 8/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0, W-PROC-HANDOVER 8/0, W-PA-TILES 89/0, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0. **Found while looking at the real render:** a tooltip near the bottom edge clipped (now flipped and clamped), and "committed" lost the space after the currency (now `moneyTxt`). **Honest look:** on the real pilot sample the map is sparse (almost every project has spent nothing and two are 254 and 284 days late, so the bubbles sit on the 0% line, equal size because the sample mixes USD and BDT); the design reads better on a populated portfolio, but it shows the data as it is. **Not built, named:** the project page tabs (mock-up C), the table-first analyst layout, per-user theme storage, trend arrows and sparklines (no history stored), the T8 palette-validator run (the health colours are status colours, so the check is contrast, not categorical separation).

### 5.54 SPEC — the project page: one project in narrow tabs (2026-11-12; owner: "yes, do it well"; mock-up C of the same day; replaces the ERP grid as the first place a drill lands)
**Issue it disproves:** a drill ends at a row and then jumps to an ERP window whose tabs are far wider than the screen; the reader loses the story ("why is it amber?") that the dashboard just told. **Rule:** the project page lives inside the dashboard shell at `#project/<C_Project_ID>[/<tab>]`, opened from the drill panel's project code, with a "‹ Dashboard" way back; the ERP window stays one click away as a secondary link ("Open in iDempiere", legacy: `index.zul?Action=Zoom&TableName=C_Project&Record_ID=`, twin: the Project window 130). Header: code, name and three chips (Health, Schedule, Budget buckets, each with its word). Five tabs, each narrow (no horizontal page scroll at 390 px or 1500 px), keyboard operable (Left/Right/Home/End move between tabs; the active tab is `aria-selected`): **Overview** (Approved, Spent, Due vs Done, Forecast with its "basis differs" chip, and "In plain words": statements generated from the rows, never typed); **Schedule** (the phases as bars against a "today" line, status complete / in progress / overdue / to come, plus the same phases as a table: dates, planned amount, task count); **Cost** (per phase planned against committed as bars with the figures, a totals row, and the project's committed/invoiced from its lines); **Procurement** (the project's purchase orders: number, status, total, open ones marked; "no data (not installed)" when the orders entity is not readable, never an empty list that looks like none); **Documents** (BIM deliverables of the project at their latest revision with status, issued / total; "no data (not installed)" without the module). **One source:** `capex/tiles.js projectDetail(data, filters, id)` returns everything the page draws (pure, no DOM), built from the same populations and buckets as `compute()`; the page only draws it. **Plain-words statements** (each carries the rows it came from): an overdue phase ("X is overdue: it ended <date> and is not ticked complete"), a phase ticked complete before its end date, the budget bucket (within plan with the share spent / above plan / no cost booked yet), and the forecast basis warning when it applies; a project with nothing to say says "nothing flagged", not a made-up line. **Not built, named:** editing from this page (the ERP window is where a phase is ticked), task-level rows, a Documents download, history. **Witness W-PROJECT-PAGE (`capex/tests/witness_project_page.js`):** P1 `projectDetail` numbers (planned, spent, due, done, per-phase status and late days, totals) == an independent recompute from the snapshot rows, for every project of two snapshots; P2 on the real page every tab opens and the Schedule bars == the phases with the right status classes; P3 the Cost figures == the phase and line rows and the totals row == the project's planned; P4 Procurement lists exactly the project's purchase orders (a fixture adds two), open ones marked, and says not installed when the entity is absent; P5 Documents shows the latest revision per deliverable (fixture A-101 rev1 RE + rev2 CO, C-301 DR → 2 deliverables, 1 issued); P6 every plain-words statement names rows that exist, and a project with no overdue phase gets no overdue statement; P7 no horizontal overflow on any tab at 1500 and 390 px; P8 Left/Right change the tab and "‹ Dashboard" returns; P9 the ERP link carries the source system's record id. `PP_BREAK=1` flips one drawn bar's status: P2 must FAIL. A project with no phases prints INCONCLUSIVE for P2/P3, never PASS.
**RESULT §5.54 (2026-11-12): built.** `tiles.js projectDetail(data, f, id)` (pure; phases with status/late days, money, per-phase planned/committed, totals, lines, purchase orders with open flag, documents at latest revision, generated statements with their source rows) and `dash.js projectPage()` at `#project/<id>[/<tab>]`: header with three bucket chips, five tabs (Overview, Schedule, Cost, Procurement, Documents), keyboard Left/Right/Home/End, "‹ Dashboard" back, "Open in iDempiere ↗" secondary; the drill panel's project code now opens the page and a small "ERP ↗" link opens the record in the source system; the portfolio filter bar is hidden on the project page. **W-PROJECT-PAGE 16 judged / 0 fail:** P1 `projectDetail` == an independent recompute (phase status, late days, planned, committed, due, done) for 16 projects of two snapshots (the first run caught my witness comparing by index against a differently sorted list: compared by phase name instead); P2 five tabs, chips as words (health=amber, schedule=delayed 50 days, budget within plan), overview numbers == detail, 8 Schedule bars == the 8 phases with their statuses and late days, `PP_BREAK=1` (Substructure flipped to complete) FAILS it; P3 Cost 8 rows and the totals row == the sums (7,063,322.98); P4 Procurement lists exactly the project's purchase orders (2, one open; a sales order is not one); P5 Documents shows the latest revision per deliverable (A-101 r2 CO, C-301 r1 DR → 1 of 2 issued); P6 statements == the page's list, a project with nothing overdue has no overdue statement; P7 no horizontal overflow on any of 5 tabs at 1500 and 390 px; P8 ArrowRight/End move the tab, back returns; P9 each drill row opens `#project/<id>` and its ERP link carries the source system's record id. Regression green: W-THEME 11/0, W-CAPEX-SAMPLE 8/0, W-DASH-PROJSEL 8/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0 (its link check moved to the new `a.erp`). **Found by looking at the real render:** the dashboard filter bar crowded the project page (hidden there now), and the dark skin drew the Schedule table header white (fixed). **Honest limits:** the Procurement and Documents fixtures (an extra draft order and three BIM documents) are the witness's, because the sample has no BIM Document rows and its orders carry no project; "In progress" uses the amber of the Health scale, a reader could confuse the two; no editing from this page; no task rows; no history.

### 5.55 SPEC — throw-away test data for the film and an ERP workout (2026-11-12; owner: "some test doc data that we can tear down after the film is done; get more projects in with more events and transactions; a good test of the ERP")
**Issue it disproves:** the film's dashboard shows 3 projects with nothing in most buckets (Handover empty, Procurement near empty, the map flat), and the ERP has never been asked to take a dozen projects with phases, issues and purchase orders through its web services in one go. **Rule:** `capex/tools/film_testdata.js` on the TEST instance only (`~/idempiere-bimdoc`, DB `idempiere_bimdoc`, :8108; it refuses any other base or database and never touches the pilot): `snapshot` copies the database to `idempiere_bimdoc_pretd` BEFORE any data goes in; `generate` creates 12 neutral projects (`Value TD_*`, name "<Place> (test data)": Riverside Tower, North Depot, Bridge Deck, Clinic Wing, Data Hall, Substation, Car Park, School Block, Pump Station, Office Fit-out, Warehouse 3, Plaza) with 3 to 5 phases each, two test materials per phase, phases ticked complete, stock Project Issues and completed purchase orders, all through iDempiere's own services (the `pushProjectOrder` footprint and `film_progress.js`; no SQL write); dates are relative to today so the spread is real (on track, late, over budget, not started, ready for handover); `teardown` restores the snapshot, so the instance is exactly what it was, posted documents and stock movements included (deleting rows one by one cannot promise that). **Names are neutral on purpose** (privacy rule). **Witness (the log, `§TD_*`):** G1 after `generate`, an independent SQL read-back equals the scenario: 12 projects, the phase count, the ticked-phase count, the projects with a completed issue and with a completed PO; the per-table deltas against the snapshot are printed (projects, phases, lines, issues, orders, products, m_transaction, fact_acct); any service error is a FAIL line. G2 after `teardown`, `td_projects=0` and every table count equals the snapshot's, or the verdict is FAIL; `generate` refuses to run without a snapshot or a second time. **Hazards named:** `snapshot` and `teardown` stop and restart the instance (about a minute each) and terminate its database connections; a refresh of the instance by another session (F6) also wipes the data, and the snapshot copy then survives only until `teardown` drops it.

### 5.56 SPEC + FINDINGS — two twin ≠ legacy defects found by the bigger test data (2026-11-12; TWIN CARDINAL RULE: no accepted divergence; owner: "a good test of the ERP")
**F-A (integer division).** With 12 more projects the W-CAPEX-PROPOSED run printed `legacy 4227.80, twin engine 4227.3`. Traced (instrument checked first: the same rows through plain SQLite give the same 0.5): the measure 1000042 (Q3 forecast EAC) divides `(PlannedAmt - done) * ProjectBalanceAmt / done`; PostgreSQL divides `numeric` exactly, SQLite divides INTEGERS as integers when the amounts are whole numbers. Car Park: planned 330, done 220, spent 459: legacy 459 + 110 × 459 ÷ 220 = **688.5**, twin 459 + **229** = 688. Any project whose three figures happen to be whole numbers is truncated in the twin: a silent, data-dependent divergence. `tiles.js` (JavaScript floats) is not affected, so page == legacy while the twin's PA goal said otherwise. **Fix:** a migration `202611121200_CAPEX-Q3-EAC-FLOATDIV.sql` rewrites the measure to `... * ProjectBalanceAmt * 1.0 / done` (PostgreSQL numeric stays exact, SQLite is forced to real division); `REPLACE` is run in both dialects and a second run changes nothing. **F-B (pack omission).** The twin receives the CAPEX measures through the data pack (`capex/plugin/capex_2pack_source.mjs` `PACKS`); the §5.44 migration `202611121000_CAPEX-S2-HEALTH-OVERDUE.sql` (Health Green/Amber never green with an overdue phase) was applied to legacy and to W-PA-TILES' twin directly but is in NO pack, so a twin installed from the pack still used the old Health rule while legacy used the new one. W-PA-TILES could not see it because it applies the scripts itself, not the pack. **Fix:** released packs are immutable, so a new pack **1.0.4** carries both migrations (S2 and Q3), `capex_dashboard.mjs` lists it, `2Pack_1.0.4.zip` is generated by the one Pack Out engine. **Witnesses (both RED first):** `capex/tests/witness_pack_covers.js` W-PACK-COVERS: P1 every `*.sql` migration in `capex/legacy/migration/iD13/postgresql` is carried by some pack version or is on a named allowlist with its reason; P2 each pack version has its zip; P3 the manifest lists every version's zip (the class of defect F-B); `PACK_BREAK=1` drops one migration from the newest pack: P1 must FAIL. `capex/tests/witness_q3_intdiv.js` W-Q3-INTDIV: the twin's PA engine over a project with whole-number amounts (planned 330, spent 459, phases 110/110/110 with two complete) gives the exact **688.5**, and a fractional project (planned 330.5 …) still agrees; `Q3_BREAK=1` withholds the fix migration: the whole-number case must FAIL (688). **Also changed:** `setup_test_instance.sh` applies the new migration and checks it; W-PA-RENDER B2 expects the packs 1.0.0 to 1.0.4.
**RESULT §5.55 (2026-11-12):** `film_testdata.js snapshot` (copy `idempiere_bimdoc_pretd`, counts before: projects 2, phases 0, lines 1, issues 0, orders 8, products 55, m_transaction 28, fact_acct 300), `generate` → **`§TD_GENERATE_VERDICT PASS`**: 12 projects, 42 phases (19 ticked complete), 84 project lines, 9 projects with a completed stock Project Issue (9 inventory transactions, 8 accounting facts), 5 projects with a completed purchase order and 5 with an open (draft) one, 10 orders in all, 2 test products, **0 service errors, 37 s**; every figure read back by SQL. **`teardown` was exercised once for real** (first scenario, before the plan sizes were tuned): `§TD_TEARDOWN_VERDICT PASS` with `td_projects=0` and every table count equal to the snapshot's; a second snapshot + generate then built the current data set (plan sizes tuned through quantities so spent runs from 12% to 140% of plan, five draft purchase orders added through `film_progress.js`, whose PO argument now takes a `,`-joined list and a `d:` prefix for a draft). What the dashboard reads on it (legacy read through `legacy_snapshot.js`): 14 active projects, Health green 5 / amber 1 / red 5 / no data 3, Budget within 7 / over 2 / not started 5, Procurement open POs 5 on 5 projects (725,460 per the instrument's sum is the PO totals), no PO 6, Handover ready 1. **Limit, named:** the amounts are small (plans 180 to 700 USD) because the only stock on the instance is garden products costing under 52 a unit and Project Issues are what move Actual Cost; the proportions (spent against plan) are what the film shows, not the magnitude. **The instance holds this data now; remove it with `node capex/tools/film_testdata.js teardown`.** W-CAPEX-PROPOSED's hand-computed totals assume a pristine instance (its measures count the whole instance), so it reports its hand-value checks FAIL while this data is present; its twin == legacy checks PASS. Re-run it after teardown.
**RESULT §5.56 (2026-11-12): both defects fixed, both witnesses RED first.** W-Q3-INTDIV before the fix: whole-number project twin **688** against the hand value 688.5, `X0` fix missing; after: PASS 3/0, `Q3_BREAK=1` FAILS X1. W-PACK-COVERS before: P1 FAIL naming `202611121000_CAPEX-S2-HEALTH-OVERDUE.sql`; after (pack **1.0.4** = S2 + Q3, `2Pack_1.0.4.zip` generated, `1.0.3` regenerates "unchanged"): PASS 3/0, `PACK_BREAK=1` FAILS P1. With the 12 extra projects present, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED now shows legacy == twin for all three measures (14,925.80 · 2,140.80 · 4,227.80; it printed 4,227.3 for the twin before). Also updated: `setup_test_instance.sh` (applies and checks the fix; the snapshot copy got the same migration so a teardown does not lose it), W-PA-RENDER B1/B2 for pack 1.0.4 (32/0). Pre-existing, not touched: W-PLUGIN-PACKS K4 (the mfg bundle), W-PACKOUT O6 (a libero form), W-PACKGEN (needs the System Administrator role).

### 5.57 SPEC — EntityType 'A' (Applications) for every dictionary row our plugins introduce (2026-11-12; owner: "Are we using EntityType in our legacy plugins? If not, set EntityType = 'A' for Applications" → "Yes")
**Measured before writing:** twin `bim_document.mjs` `STD.entitytype='U'` → every row of its manifest; legacy test instance (`idempiere_bimdoc`) holds BIM_Document's table/columns/window/tabs/processes/menu/list values as 'U'; CAPEX: only `PA_MeasureCalc` (Name `CAPEX%`) and `PA_ColorSchema` 1000000 carry the column among the tables our scripts insert into (PA_Measure, PA_Goal, C_Phase, WS_* have none); stock `AD_EntityType 'A'` = Applications exists, no new row. 'U' (User maintained) is the iDempiere model default (`erp/model_initial_defaults.js`, `ninja_model.js`) and stays there: only rows OUR plugins ship change.
**Rule:** a dictionary row shipped by a plugin is 'A'; 'U' is for what a user types in the client. New columns we add are born 'A'. Task-date columns (hand-off item 2) too.
**Changes:** (1) `bim_document.mjs` `STD.entitytype='A'`; the workbook `BIM_Document.xlsx` and the 2Pack/Java in bim-compiler regenerate from it (alpha rule: 1.0.0 replaced, no version bump). (2) CAPEX: migrations are append-only → NEW script `202611121500_CAPEX-ENTITYTYPE-A.sql` (UPDATE … SET EntityType='A' for the CAPEX PA_MeasureCalc + PA_ColorSchema 1000000, `AND EntityType='U'` so a re-run changes 0), carried by NEW pack 1.0.5 (1.0.4 is released, immutable).
**Witness W-ENTITYTYPE `capex/tests/witness_entitytype.js` (issue: "our plugins ship 'U' rows that look user-made and are indistinguishable from a site's own customisations"):** E1 twin: every row of every table in the BIM Document manifest that carries `entitytype` is 'A' (denominator = the manifest's own dictionary, none hand-listed). E2 workbook: the same rows read back from `BIM_Document.xlsx`. E3 legacy test instance: the same ids read by psql are 'A'; instance unreachable or rows absent = INCONCLUSIVE for E3, never PASS. E4 CAPEX scripts: after applying all migration scripts in name order to a scratch copy of the statements' effect (the new script's UPDATE covers every PA_MeasureCalc/PA_ColorSchema row the earlier scripts insert with 'U'). E5 pilot (psql): CAPEX rows are 'A', stock rows (100, 101 and the stock measures) still 'D'. Zero rows judged in an arm = INCONCLUSIVE. `ENTITY_BREAK=1` leaves one row 'U' and must FAIL.
**RESULT 2026-11-12 (W-ENTITYTYPE RED first: 6 FAIL on the unfixed state; GREEN after: PASS arms E1-E5 fail=0; `ENTITY_BREAK=1` → FAIL E1 ad_table:1):** twin manifest 110 rows / workbook 122 rows / legacy test instance 78 rows read back (rebuilt from the base seed with the regenerated 1.0.0 pack, alpha replace; 122 `<EntityType>A</EntityType>`, was 122 U) / pilot 27 PA_MeasureCalc + 1 PA_ColorSchema now 'A' (script applied by psql, re-run changes 0; stock 100/101 stay 'D'). CAPEX pack 1.0.5 carries the script (W-PACK-COVERS 3/0, W-PA-RENDER 32/0 with B1/B2 expecting 1.0.5). Regression: W-XLSX-PACK 9/0, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0, W-DASH-BIMDOC 8/0, W-BIMDOC-LEGACY 12/0. bim-compiler: `iDempiereOOTB/org.idempiere.bimdocument/META-INF/2Pack_1.0.0.zip` regenerated (branch `feat/bimdoc-entitytype-a`). **Side effect, honest:** the instance rebuild removed the §5.55 throw-away TD_* data (restore point `idempiere_bimdoc_pretd` now orphaned); regenerate with `node capex/tools/film_testdata.js snapshot` then `generate` when the film needs it. The pilot was not restarted: the change is a label, no measure value moves.

### 5.58 SPEC — task dates in the BIM Document plugin (2026-11-12; owner: "Is your reluctance to inject schedule time model into Phase as touching legacy iDempiere? It can be part of the BIM document module plugin" → build it there; hand-off item 2)
**Measured (not re-derived):** phases carry dates from the 4D programme (`proj_fold.js` phase window = min start / max end over the phase's tasks, `phWin`, line ~301); `C_ProjectTask` has no date columns (legacy and twin), so a 4D bar exists as a task row (name `<phase> — <storey>`, one per 4D task) without start/end, and the project page's Schedule tab can draw phase bars only. The BIM Document plugin already extends `C_ProjectTask`/`C_ProjectPhase` with virtual columns (`bim_document.mjs` VCOLS), so a physical pair belongs to the same plugin, EntityType 'A' (§5.57), no host code.
**Decision (owner has not chosen between the two; default taken, reversible while alpha):** two nullable physical columns `BIM_StartDate`, `BIM_EndDate` (reference Date) on `C_ProjectTask`, `IsSyncDatabase=Y`, plus the two fields in the stock Project Task tab. BIM_ prefix = never clashes with a future core column. The alternative (child table `BIM_TaskSchedule`) stays open for a later baseline-vs-actual.
**Build:** (1) `bim_document.mjs` TASKDATE columns + fields, `ensureTaskDateColumns(db)` exported and used by `activate` (ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN if absent, logged `§BIMDOC-PLUGIN columns-added`); workbook + bim-compiler 2Pack regenerated. (2) `proj_fold.js`: with a programme and the columns present, each task row gets min start / max end of the 4D task(s) folded into it, via the same `dayDate`; columns absent → notes `NO_TASK_DATE_COLUMNS`, never a guess. (3) `capex/adapters.js` tasks entity reads the two columns when the server has them (legacy: read type output fields, migration under `capex/legacy/ws/`); `tiles.js projectDetail()` returns the task rows with dates; the Schedule tab draws task bars under their phase, `no task dates on this server` when none. (4) legacy: push whitelist `WS_WebServiceFieldInput` for `BridgeCreateCProjectTask`.
**Witness W-TASKDATES `capex/tests/witness_taskdates.js` (issue: "the Schedule tab can draw phase bars only because the 4D tasks reach the order without their dates"):** T1 whole-set fold of HHS with the programme: every task row's dates == its 4D task(s) (expected read from the owner's run `sched.tasks`, not from the fold); T2 each phase window == span of its task rows (min start / max end); T3 no programme → 0 dated tasks + note; T4 fold without the columns → `NO_TASK_DATE_COLUMNS`, no throw; T5 second fold changes 0; T6 `projectDetail()` task rows == the DB rows; T7 Schedule tab bars == task rows (real page); T8 legacy read-back == twin (after the legacy half). Zero tasks judged = INCONCLUSIVE. `TASKDATES_BREAK=1` shifts one task's stored date after the fold and must FAIL T1 and T2.
**RESULT 2026-11-12 (built twin + legacy read path; the PUSH path is NOT built, named below):** W-TASKDATES 9/0 on HHS (T0-T7b; RED first: 4 FAIL + 1 INCONCLUSIVE; `TASKDATES_BREAK=1` fails T1, T2, T6): 16 task rows carry the dates of their 4D tasks (expected read from the owner's run, not the fold), 6 phase windows == the span of their task rows, a second fold adds 0, `projectDetail()` task rows == DB rows, the real Schedule tab draws 16 `rect.tbar` == the 16 rows, and a store without the columns says "no task dates on this server" with 0 bars. W-TASKDATES-LEGACY 4/0 on the rebuilt test instance (break → FAIL): the 2Pack created `bim_startdate`/`bim_enddate` (timestamp) on the real `c_projecttask` from the pack alone (AD_Column 'A', IsSyncDatabase Y); the committed fixture reads back through the stock `QueryCProjectTask` and the dashboard's own adapter with equal dates, a dateless task reads null, twin adapter == legacy adapter. Files: `bim_document.mjs` (TASK_DATE_COLUMNS, `ensureTaskDateColumns`), workbook (34 columns), `proj_fold.js`, `adapters.js`, `tiles.js` (`taskRows`), `dash.js` (Tasks card), `ws/bimdoc/…202611121600_CAPEX-WS-TASKDATES.sql` (output fields of QueryCProjectTask + input fields of BridgeCreateCProjectTask; no-op where the plugin is absent), `setup_test_instance.sh` now applies it and the §5.57 script. Regression: W-ENTITYTYPE, W-FOLD-ONE-SCHEDULE 11/0, W-PROJECT-PAGE 16/0, W-DASH-BIMDOC 8/0, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0, W-PACK-COVERS 3/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0, W-XLSX-PACK 9/0 (X1c now 34 columns, 27 physical).
**⛔ NOT BUILT, named:** the viewer's push to a real legacy (`viewer/erp_push_plan.js` task `fields` → `erp_rest_push.js` → the plugin's `BIMOOTB_ProjectTask` type) does not carry the two dates: that type belongs to the push plugin (bim-compiler `BIM_OOTB_LEGACY_IDEMPIERE_INTEGRATION.md`), its whitelist needs the two fields, and the push must send them only to a target that has the columns (a pilot without the BIM Document plugin would refuse an unknown field). The `BridgeCreateCProjectTask` whitelist in the script above covers the bridge path only. Until then a pushed legacy order has task rows without dates and its Schedule tab says so.

### 5.59 SPEC — receipts, invoices and payments as a DRILL under Procurement, not on the glance (2026-11-12; hand-off item 3; owner: "agree, and it should be well organised not to crowd the hi level dashboard but a drill into")
**Measured:** the glance (tiles) and the project page cover order, 4D phase dates, PO (open/completed), project issue (= actual cost) and the invoiced TOTAL; no receipt, vendor invoice or payment row is read. All three documents carry `C_Project_ID` and `C_Order_ID` in the twin seed and on legacy (`M_InOut`, `C_Invoice`, `C_Payment`; payment also `C_Invoice_ID`); the test instance has no read type for any of them (only `QueryCOrder`/`QueryCProjectIssue` from §5.38).
**Where it lives (organisation rule):** nothing is added to the left menu's glance, the tiles or the Executive glance. The chain is one card, "Supply chain", at the TOP of the project page's existing Procurement tab, above the PO table; the portfolio Procurement Risks view (§5.51) is unchanged. A project with no chain documents shows the PO table as today.
**Definitions (all derived from rows; each is "proposed" until Sysnova confirms, labelled as the PO table already is):** per purchase order (IsSOTrx=N): `received` = number of completed (CO, CL) vendor receipts (`M_InOut`, IsSOTrx=N) with that `C_Order_ID`; `invoiced` = SUM(GrandTotal) of completed vendor invoices with that `C_Order_ID`; `paid` = SUM(PayAmt) of completed payments with IsReceipt=N tied to the order (`C_Order_ID`) or to one of its invoices (`C_Invoice_ID`), each counted once; money is summed only in the PO's own currency, a document in another currency is counted in `otherCur` and NEVER converted or dropped silently. `stage`: `ordered` (nothing else), `received` (≥1 receipt, invoiced 0), `invoiced` (invoiced>0, paid<invoiced), `paid` (invoiced>0 and paid≥invoiced), `unfinished` (PO not completed: drafts have no stage). Documents with the project but no order link are counted as `unlinked` per kind, shown as a line under the card, never added to a PO.
**Build:** `adapters.js` three OPTIONAL entities `receipts` (`M_InOut`), `vinvoices` (`C_Invoice`), `payments` (`C_Payment`) → `unavailable` where the server lacks them (not installed, never 0); legacy read types `QueryMInOut`, `QueryCInvoice`, `QueryCPayment` as a migration under `capex/legacy/ws/chain/` (same convention as Q4); `tiles.js projectDetail().chain` = `{ rows:[{order, status, total, cur, received, invoiced, paid, stage, otherCur}], strip:{ordered,received,invoiced,paid}, unlinked:{receipts,invoices,payments} }` or `null` when any of the three is unavailable; `dash.js` the card (strip of four counts that each open the rows of that stage, then the table). The twin's own data: §5.60.
**Witness W-CHAIN `capex/tests/witness_chain.js` (issue: "the supply chain after the PO is invisible, or the page shows a chain its rows do not hold"):** C1 `projectDetail().chain` == an independent recompute from the raw rows (no tiles.js code) over a hand fixture that includes: a PO with all four stages, a draft PO, a partial payment, a payment tied only by invoice, a document in another currency, an unlinked receipt, another project's documents; C2 stage strip counts == the table's stages; C3 any entity unavailable → `chain` null and the card says "not installed"; C4 the real page: the card is inside the Procurement tab, the glance has no new element (the tile list is unchanged), strip counts == rows; C5 legacy read-back == twin on the same rows (after the legacy half). `CHAIN_BREAK=1` counts a draft receipt as received and must FAIL C1.
**Owner addition 2026-11-12 (mid-build): "in iDempiere, it can drill right up the doc panel thru its URL. It is already a legacy feature"** → every document in the card (PO, receipt, invoice, payment) is a link that opens its record in the system the numbers came from: legacy by its own WebUI zoom (`Action=Zoom&TableName=<table>&Record_ID=<id>`, the same mechanism as the project's "Open in iDempiere", no new mechanism), twin by the purchase window of the table (seed `po_window_id`: PO 181, receipt 184, invoice 183; payment 195). W-CHAIN C5.
**RESULT 2026-11-12:** W-CHAIN 11/0 (RED first: 5 FAIL on the unbuilt code; `CHAIN_BREAK=1` fails C1): C1 7 PO rows == the independent recompute (full chain, draft PO, part payment, payment tied only by invoice, other-currency invoice, unlinked documents, a sales order and another project's documents excluded), C2 strip == stages, C3 an unavailable entity → `chain` null, C4 the card is inside the Procurement tab and the glance (13 tiles, the 6 menu items) is unchanged, C5 20 of 20 links well formed. W-CHAIN-LEGACY 3/0 on the test instance (break → FAIL): a committed PO/receipt/draft receipt/invoice/payment fixture reads back through the new `QueryMInOut`, `QueryCInvoice`, `QueryCPayment` types (`capex/legacy/ws/chain/…202611121700_CAPEX-WS-CHAIN.sql`, applied by `setup_test_instance.sh`) and `projectDetail().chain` over the legacy rows gives received 1 (draft not counted), invoiced 500, paid 500, stage paid. Regression: W-PROJECT-PAGE 16/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0, W-PROC-HANDOVER 8/0, W-CAPEX-SAMPLE 8/0, W-DASH-PROJSEL 8/0, W-THEME 11/0, W-DASH-BIMDOC 8/0, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0. **Not yet:** the twin holds no receipt/invoice/payment rows of its own: §5.60 (the mock transactions on the single kernel) is the next item; the pilot has no read types for these three (a site applies the chain script, as for Q4).

### 5.60 SPEC + FINDINGS — the mock supply chain built INSIDE the twin (2026-11-12; hand-off item 3; owner: "if anything not built, build them first")
**Measured by a probe in node (the shared harness `erp/tests/dd_common.js` `boot()` → `H.save(table, fields)` / `H.docAction(table, id, 'CO')`, the model layer, no browser; probe kept as `capex/tools/probe_twin_chain.js`) — read these before writing code:**
1. The twin completes a project-tied chain through its own model layer: C_Order (IsSOTrx=N, C_Project_ID) → M_InOut (doctype 122 "MM Receipt", C_Order_ID, C_Project_ID) → C_Invoice (C_Order_ID, C_Project_ID) → C_Payment (IsReceipt=N, C_Invoice_ID, C_Order_ID, C_Project_ID) all reach DocStatus CO. Needed: `H.A.RUNTIME.bind(H.q)` (or `A.bind`) before the first C_OrderLine save, else `MOrderLine_setTaxIfMissing` throws on a null DB (`§LOGINCTX build` then appears); a product that is ON the price list (m_productprice of a version of list 102), else `ProductNotOnPriceListException`; receipt doctype 122, NOT 147 (`createConfirmation (MInOut.java) not ported — named`); invoice `C_PaymentTerm` "Immediate" (105), else `@ErrorPaymentSchedule@`; complete the invoice BEFORE the payment (an early payment posts an allocation error "Gain/Loss - Invoice not posted yet").
2. **RETRACTED 2026-11-12 (same session, read the source before building on it):** the first reading said the twin has a pricing defect because a line saved with only `priceentered=50` has `priceactual=0`. Legacy does the same: `MOrderLine` initialises `PriceActual` to zero (`MOrderLine.java:164`) and derives it only in `setPrice()` (programmatic) or by a UI callout; `film_progress.js` therefore passes `PriceActual` explicitly on legacy. A model-level save is not a UI edit, so the twin (no callouts at model save) == legacy. Builders pass `priceactual` (and `priceentered`) explicitly, as the legacy path does. No defect, no build step (a).
3. **RETRACTED + CORRECTED 2026-11-12 (same session):** the first reading said the twin has no `c_projectissue` table. WRONG: I looked at the raw `erp/ad_seed.db` file; the base patch `erp/patches/ad_seed.db.sql:592` creates it (27 columns == the dictionary, W-PROJECT-ISSUE P1). **The real gap: no DocAction class** (`§MODEL-WF ... abort="no DocAction class registered for c_projectissue"`), so a Project Issue could be saved but never completed on the twin: no stock movement, no project balance, no actual cost. Lesson (recorded for the next reader): measure a twin's tables on the PATCHED database (`dd_common.boot()`), never on the seed file alone.
4. Invoice posting names a gap: `§MODEL-POST table=c_invoice posted=N reason="API service-product line (MCostDetail.createInvoice) not ported — named"`. The documents are Completed; the books are not posted for non-stocked product lines. Use a stocked product line where the posting must show, or port MCostDetail.createInvoice; say which in the film.
**Build order (spec line first, witness RED first, one PR each):** (b) ✅ DONE 2026-11-12 Project Issue DocAction in the twin, W-PROJECT-ISSUE 7/0 incl. twin == legacy (see RESULT below); (c) ✅ DONE 2026-11-12 shared scenario module `capex/tools/td_scenario.js` (the 12-project `SC`, `PH`, `PRODUCTS`, `day()` lifted out of `film_testdata.js`, which then imports it: one scenario, two builders; a delete in film_testdata); (d) ✅ DONE 2026-11-12 `capex/tools/twin_mockchain.js`: boots the harness, creates the 12 projects/phases/lines/ticks through the model layer, the PO → receipt → invoice → payment chain per project to a STAGE the scenario names (ordered / received / invoiced / part-paid / paid, so the Supply chain card shows all stages), issues, and EXPORTS the store (`db.export()`) to `~/.cache/capex_film/twin_mock/erp_twin.db` which the dashboard's Source A already loads (`adapters.loadSqlite`); the twin keeps nothing across reloads of the browser ERP unless its OPFS persistence is used: this file is the persistence, teardown = delete it (the seed is untouched); (e) ✅ DONE 2026-11-12 W-TWIN-CHAIN 5/0 (the glance tiles twin == legacy for the projects both sides hold is NOT done: see RESULT): the exported store through `adapters.fromSqlite` + `tiles.projectDetail().chain` == an independent SQL recompute of the same file for all 12 projects, strip counts == the scenario's declared stages, and the glance tiles twin == legacy for the projects both sides hold (`film_testdata.js generate` on the test instance: needs `snapshot` first). The chain documents' LEGACY side has no web-service create types (only reads, §5.59): its rows would be written by psql, which proves reads only: say so and do not claim a created-both-sides parallel run for receipts/invoices/payments until those types exist.
**Why not now:** the Project Issue port is a core build (item 3 above) and must not be rushed at the end of a long session.
**RESULT build step (b) 2026-11-12:** `erp/model_projectissue.js` = MProjectIssue (`MProjectIssue.java` 178-296 doComplete, 298-345 doReverse, 543-595 updateBalanceAmt, getNextLine 148-153): a stocked issue takes the storage rows in material-policy order, writes the `W+` M_Transaction (-qty) and adds the cost to `C_Project.ProjectBalanceAmt` through the twin's own `ModelCost.getProductCosts`; a non-stocked product completes with no transaction; Reverse Correct/Accrual makes the negative twin row, links both and sets RE. Wired into `idempiere.html` (after model_inventory) and `sw.js` (precache, CACHE_VERSION v1062). W-PROJECT-ISSUE **7/0**: P1 27 columns == dictionary, P2 product 139 qty 3 at locator 101 on project 100: CO, balance +22.95 == 3 x 7.65 (unit cost read from m_cost by SQL), on-hand -3, one W+ -3; P2b lines 10, 20; P3 non-stocked; P4 reversal restores balance (-22.95) and stock (+3, a new dated storage row as `MStorageOnHand.add` does), original RE, reversal linked; **P5 the same issue through legacy's web services (BridgeCreateProjectIssue + BridgeCompleteProjectIssue) on the test instance moves balance +22.95 and on-hand -3: equal to the twin**. `PI_BREAK=1` (no model) reproduces the defect: P2-P4 FAIL "no DocAction class registered". **Not ported, named in the file header:** the AD_Process `ProjectIssue` variants (receipt / expense / project line), period-closed back-date check, ASI material-policy date, the m_inoutline / s_timeexpenseline cost branches; posting `PJI` still reports `no Doc class ported` (books not posted for issues). P5 leaves a completed issue on the disposable test instance (project 100 balance +22.95): a refresh from the base seed removes it.
**RESULT build steps (c)(d)(e) 2026-11-12:** `capex/tools/td_scenario.js` now holds the 12-project scenario once (`SC`, `PH`, `PRODUCTS`, plus `CHAIN` = the stage each completed purchase order reaches, `STRIP` = what that implies); `film_testdata.js` imports it (-8 lines, the legacy generator ignores CHAIN). `capex/tools/twin_mockchain.js` builds the 12 projects in the twin through the model layer (`dd_common.boot`): project, phases with relative dates, lines, ticked phases, completed Project Issues (§5.60 step b), draft and completed purchase orders (PriceActual passed explicitly, as legacy's path does), and per completed order the chain its CHAIN names (receipt doctype 122, invoice with the Immediate term, payment after the invoice), then exports the store (default `~/.cache/capex_film/twin_mock/erp_twin.db`; Source A loads it with `adapters.loadSqlite`; teardown = delete the file; `--today`, `--out`, `--only`). **W-TWIN-CHAIN 5/0** (it builds the store itself, no file trusted): W1 chain rows == an independent SQL recompute for all 12 projects (10 purchase orders, 5 completed); W2 the portfolio strip {ordered 1, received 1, invoiced 1, paid 2} == the scenario's declaration, each project's completed stages == its declared CHAIN; W3 ticked phases and completed issues per project == the scenario; W4 no chain document of ours lacks its project; W5 the store opens with 12 TD projects. `TWINCHAIN_BREAK=1` (delete the receipt of the "received" order) → W2 FAIL strip ordered 2, received 0. **Honest limits:** (1) the twin's Spent per project is NOT the legacy TD number: the twin's receipts (qty x PO price 50) move product 139's moving-average cost, so issues after the first receipt cost more than 7.65 (e.g. TD_DEPOT 10 units = 384.50); legacy's TD data has no receipts. A twin == legacy compare of Spent must use a scenario without receipts of the issued product, or give the receipts their own product: decide before the film shows both sides. (2) twin == legacy of the glance tiles for the 12 projects is NOT run (needs `film_testdata.js snapshot` + `generate` on the test instance and a tiles compare); (3) the dashboard has not been pointed at the exported file yet (the next item: a `?db=` open of the store + the film stills).

### 5.61 SPEC — the dashboard reads the twin's own store (2026-11-12; owner: "we are filming just the Dashboard, if clicking on it shows twin side which is same as legacy in parity, that is fine. It is a POC")
**Decision:** the film shows Source A (the twin) only; the §5.60 Spent drift against legacy (receipts move the average cost) is accepted for this POC and stays recorded there. **Build:** `twin_mockchain.js` exports a store the page opens with `?db=/<file>` (the existing `CFG.erpDb`, `adapters.loadSqlite`); it now loads `model_project.js` (without it a line's PlannedAmt stayed 0 and the Approved-budget roll-up never ran: found by opening the store in the page) and removes the seed's three demo projects with their documents from the EXPORT (counted in `§TWIN_MOCK`; `--keep-seed` opts out), because "Total projects" counts inactive ones too.
**Witness W-DASH-TWIN `capex/tests/witness_dash_twin.js` (issue: "the film's dashboard shows numbers the twin's rows do not hold, or the seed's demo projects, or a glance changed by the chain drill"):** the store is built by the builder, served to the REAL page, and the shipped `§DASH_TILE` lines are read (primary evidence): D1 `total_projects` == the count of TD projects in the store (12) and the Project drop-down lists exactly those; D2 `approved_budget` == SUM(C_Project.PlannedAmt) and `actual_cost` == SUM(ProjectBalanceAmt) of the TD projects by SQL, in the project currency; D3 `proc_openpo` count == the non-completed purchase orders of TD projects by SQL; D4 the health tile sums to 12 and shows more than one colour (a spread, else the film has nothing to show: INCONCLUSIVE not PASS); D5 the project page of the BRIDGE project (`#project/<id>/procurement`) shows the Supply chain card with its declared stage and no page error; D6 no console error on load. `DASHTWIN_BREAK=1` adds 1000 to one project's PlannedAmt in the served copy only: D2 must FAIL.

### 5.64 STUDY — unravelling `tiles.js` into AD data: PA row, AD_Process, or HTML? (2026-11-13; owner: "we need to NOT break AD modelling ... we are concerned about maintainability. If AD_Process needed, then note it is go into the BIM plugin already done.")
**Question:** `capex/tiles.js` (324 lines) holds hand-written JS formulas. Should they become (a) PA rows, (b) an AD_Process that houses the code, or (c) code embedded in the HTML?
**Measured (read-only, origin/main 2026-11-13):** the 10 CAPEX migrations under `capex/legacy/migration/iD13/postgresql/` already insert **30 `PA_MeasureCalc` rows**, so most tiles are ALREADY SQL data and exist twice (in PA and in `tiles.js`, guarded by W-PA-TILES). Already PA, no code needed: total/closed, active, approved, actual, committed, invoiced (USD+BDT), completed this year, approvals, Health Green/Amber/Red/No-EVM (per-project EVM done in SQL), Schedule On time/At risk/Delayed/No data/Average delay, Budget Within/Over/Not started/Overrun, Forecast (USD only), Contract committed, Verified cost. Missing as PA: Stage breakdown, Physical vs Spent, BIM documents, Procurement (open PO, over-committed, no PO), Handover (ready, done), Last updated, Forecast BDT, the drill lists, the project page, the supply-chain strip. 0 `AD_Chart` rows.
**Legacy's own homes for each kind (cited):**
| Kind in `tiles.js` | Legacy mechanism, no Java | Source |
|---|---|---|
| one number (count, sum, ratio, EVM bucket, EAC) | `PA_MeasureCalc` Calculated measure (SQL) | `MMeasureCalc.getSqlPI` :153-189 |
| a breakdown (Stage per C_Phase, a bar per bucket) | `AD_Chart` + `AD_ChartDatasource` (`CategoryColumn`, `SeriesColumn`) | stock windows, §5.35 |
| the rows behind a number (drill) | the measure's own WHERE as a query, zoom from the goal | `MMeasureCalc.getQuery` :265 |
| logic SQL truly cannot express | `PA_Measure` type U, `CalculationClass` = a Java `MeasureInterface` class or `@script:` AD_Rule | `MMeasure.updateUserDefined` :643-746 |
**Finding:** every formula in `tiles.js` today is expressible as SQL. That includes the "basis differs" CPI band, latest-revision deliverables (`NOT EXISTS` a higher revision) and handover (`NOT EXISTS` an incomplete phase). So **no AD_Process and no measure class is needed**. What remains in `tiles.js` after the move is presentation only: per-currency display, empty state (`no data`), formatting. That belongs in the generic renderer (`goal_engine` + `dashboard_gadgets`), not in a formula file.
**(c) HTML is rejected:** `PA_DashboardContent.HTML` is static markup, read in `DashboardController.java:~788` and untested (§5.13). JS inside it would be a third private engine. It would not run on legacy's SQL, could not be witnessed twin == legacy, and could not be edited in a stock window. That is the least maintainable choice.
**(b) only if a future tile cannot be SQL:** a type-U measure whose `CalculationClass` is a Java `MeasureInterface` class shipped in the **BIM Document plugin** (bim-compiler `iDempiereOOTB/org.idempiere.bimdocument`, beside `BIMDocumentOpen` / `BIMDocumentRegisterExport`). The twin port is registered in `erp/plugins/bim_document.mjs` the same way as the plugin's existing process classes. Not an `@script:` AD_Rule: the twin's `erp/ad_rule.js` by design never evaluates a script body, and `goal_engine` declines type U today (E12, INCONCLUSIVE). The BIM documents tile's measure belongs in that plugin's 2Pack in any case, because it reads `BIM_Document`.
**Maintainability cost found:** PA measures are one number each, so each currency is its own row (USD and BDT are hand-listed, and Forecast has USD only). A new currency means new rows. That is legacy's convention; an `AD_Chart` with `SeriesColumn = C_Currency_ID` is the one-row alternative for money breakdowns.
**✅ OWNER REOPENED + APPROVED 2026-11-13 ("yes agree"; supersedes the 2026-11-04 "keep `tiles.js`"): the formulas move from `tiles.js` into AD data (PA_MeasureCalc / AD_Chart rows) that legacy and the twin both run, witnessed twin == legacy; the page keeps only the generic renderer. Build order:** (1) add the ~10 missing measures and one Stage `AD_Chart` as a NEW CAPEX migration and pack 1.0.6, with the BIM documents measure in the BIM Document pack. (2) Port `MMeasureCalc.getQuery` into `goal_engine` so the drill reads the measure's own WHERE. (3) The page reads goal values, not `tiles.js`. (4) Keep W-PA-TILES as the gate while both exist, then delete `tiles.js`'s formulas (deletion budget in the PR). Witnesses: X of N tiles sourced from a PA row, N from the page's tile list, never hand-listed; a break run that leaves one tile on `tiles.js` must FAIL.

### 5.65 SPEC — the UAT Guide for the pilot client's testers (2026-11-13; written before any page or shot; NOT started, queued after the §5.64 move)
**Owner (verbatim, 2026-11-13):** *"a more detailed UAT Guide for SYSNOVA which can be published also that contains only revelant step by step for testers to confirm each step thru well formated with screenshot and mouse pointer like a training manual for 2D DXF opening in Modeller, thru arrange, convert, opening in Viewer, export to ERP, but ERP setup side also of models where needed thru to Project Order handling, PO cycle, issuance of materials to site, then Dashboard usage."* · *"UAT Guide can include preliminary how to setup BIM repos on own server, and plugin install to their iDempiere"* · *"Also BIM setup part also besides ERP model setup ie 4D Schedule and 5D rates JSON editing to fit their local scenes. Advice examples of editing process, save to apply before running TimeMachine to visualize"*
**Deliverable:** bim-compiler `docs/CapexUATGuide.md` (+ `docs/img/uat/`), published ONLY through `scripts/safe_gh_deploy.sh`, then fetched back with a cache-buster. The client is participating and supplied their own information, so their name may appear on the cover (owner 2026-11-11 relaxation); the page title stays neutral ("CAPEX UAT Guide"), and the privacy grep runs on every diff.
**Form (training manual):** numbered steps. Each step has four parts: **Do** (one action) · **picture** (the real page with a pointer on the control) · **Confirm** (what the tester reads on screen: a label, count or value) · **Pass / Fail / Note** box. A sign-off sheet ends each chapter. Steps are only those the tester needs, with no developer wording (Developer's note stays in `CapexDashboardGuide.md`).
**Pictures are teaching material, never our evidence (PRIMAL LAW):** made by script from the real page (the `capex/tools/film_shots.js` / `film_dash_shots.js` pattern, software GL, no GPU, no screencast). The pointer is drawn at the target element's bounding box read from the DOM, never placed by hand. Each step's **Confirm** value is the same value a witness asserts in its `§` log, and the step cites that witness. Pictures are regenerated whenever that witness re-runs.
**Chapters and where each step is EXTRACTED from (no step invented; anything not built prints "not built yet" with its § reference):**
| # | Chapter | Sources to extract from |
|---|---|---|
| 0a | Host the BIM apps on your own server (static site, no app server; offline/PWA cache) | bim-compiler `docs/SYSTEMS_INSTALLER_GUIDE.md`, `docs/OFFLINE_INSTALL_GUIDE.md`; bim-ootb deploy notes |
| 0b | Install our plugins into your iDempiere: the CAPEX 2Pack (`capex/plugin/capex_2pack/2Pack_1.0.x.zip`), the read/write web-service scripts (`capex/legacy/ws/**/migration`), the BIM Document plugin (bim-compiler `iDempiereOOTB/org.idempiere.bimdocument`), with your tenant via `capex/tools/site_migration.js`; restart (PA measures are cached) | `capex/legacy/test_instance/setup_test_instance.sh` (the witnessed recipe, W-CAPEX-PROPOSED 17/0) turned into operator steps; §5.29, §5.30 |
| 0c | ERP model setup where needed: vendor, products, price list, warehouse, document types, project category | `docs/ERP_FirstSetup.md` Parts A–D; §5.60 (the twin scenario's master data) |
| 0d | BIM setup to your local scene: **Settings › Edit Project JSON** › *5D Rate Pack (active pack)*, *4D Schedule Template (phases, durations)*, *4D Sequence & Labour Rules* (`viewer/panels.js` `_jsonRegistry`, the standard editor `common/settings_editor.js`; files `viewer/rates/<pack>.json`, `4D_template.json`, `sequence_rules.json`). Worked examples with values copied from the shipped files: change one unit rate in the active pack; change one phase's duration in the 4D template. Then **save (applies as a stored override; Reset restores the shipped file) BEFORE running TimeMachine**. Confirm: the Gantt end date and the 5D total move by the amount the edit implies | `cache_4d_run.js` keys on `4D_template.json`/`rates.js` (CLAUDE.md law 5); W-PROPSHEET; the 4D/5D witnesses named in `4D_MODEL_INTEGRITY.md` §I |
| 1 | Modeller: open a 2D DXF, arrange, convert to 3D | bim-compiler `docs/DXF2BIMGuide.md` "Step by step in the Modeller", `docs/ModellerGuide.md` |
| 2 | Viewer: open the converted model; TimeMachine (4D) and cost (5D) | `docs/BIMUserGuide.md` |

**Witness W-UAT-GUIDE (before publishing):** every step has a picture file, a Confirm line and a cited witness that exists and last ran PASS. Every pointer box lies inside its picture and on an element visible at capture time. Every chapter has a sign-off sheet. 0 steps judged = INCONCLUSIVE. Break runs, each of which must FAIL: a step whose cited witness is missing; a pointer box moved off its element.

### 5.66 BUILD SPEC — the §5.64 move, in three PRs (2026-11-13; owner "Proceed"; written before code)
**Measured first (cited):** legacy builds a measure's SQL as Select + Where (+ date) → `addRestrictions` → **`Env.parseContext(ctx, 0, sql, false, false)` when the SQL holds `@`** (`MMeasureCalc.getSqlPI` :153-187; an unparsable tag empties the SQL). Login puts the accounting-schema currency in **`$C_Currency_ID`** (`Login.java:569`, `Env.C_CURRENCY_ID` `Env.java:97`). The drill is `MMeasureCalc.getQuery` :265: `SELECT <KeyColumn> ` + the Select clause from `FROM ` + Where + restrictions + context → the key ids. The twin's `erp/goal_engine.js evaluate` has the SQL build, restrictions and access, but **no context parse and no key query**. The twin's one `Env.parseContext` port is `erp/ad_callout.js:178`.
**Decisions (defaults, stated; the owner may overrule any one):**
- **D1 one path for both sources.** The adapter rows of the page's source (A = the twin store, B = legacy over stock web services; today's `adapters.js`) are loaded into an in-memory SQLite "page DB" (same table and column names). `goal_engine` runs each tile's PA row there. Legacy's own engine runs the same row on its server; W-PA-TILES keeps proving the two equal.
- **D2 filters are the population, not the formula.** Org, Category, Project and Status choose which projects (and their child rows) are in the page DB. The SQL is never rewritten. This is legacy's meaning of a goal restriction (it narrows rows). **Named change:** today Total, Active and Completed-this-year ignore the Status filter (`tiles.js` population P); under D2 they follow it, like every other tile.
- **D3 money is one row for every currency.** A new money measure filters on `C_Currency_ID=@$C_Currency_ID@`. Legacy's Home shows it in the login's currency; the page evaluates it once per currency present. The released per-currency USD/BDT rows stay untouched (packs are immutable, the pilot's Home uses them). Retiring them is an owner decision, named and not done.
- **D4 the tile → row binding is data.** For now it is a list of measure UUs per tile key (no formula). §5.35 later moves it into `PA_DashboardContent`.
- **Not moved in this build (named):** the Stage breakdown (needs an `AD_Chart` reader in the twin; until then the Stage tile stays on `tiles.js` and is listed as such). Also the project page and supply-chain strip: they are record views, not formulas.
**PR A — the engine, generic (no CAPEX code).** In `goal_engine`: (1) context parse at legacy's point with the kernel's `Env.parseContext` (an unparsable tag → empty SQL → `ok:false`, value 0, as legacy); (2) `evaluateCalc(q, calcId, ctx)`, a measure-level evaluation without a goal, which `evaluate` itself uses (one SQL builder); (3) `queryKeys(q, calcId, ctx)`, the `getQuery` port. **Witness W-PA-CTX** `erp/tests/witness_pa_ctx.js` (node, the twin scenario). (a) A `@$C_Currency_ID@` measure gives, per currency, the same sum as the plain SQL. (b) An unparsable tag → `ok:false`, 0. (c) For every CAPEX `PA_MeasureCalc` whose Select is `COUNT(*)` (denominator from the dictionary, not a hand list), `queryKeys` length == the count. 0 rows judged → INCONCLUSIVE. `BREAK=1` skips the parse → FAIL.
**PR B — the rows and the page path.** A new migration + pack 1.0.6 with the missing measures: the generic money rows (D3), Physical, Spent, Procurement ×3, Handover ×2, Last updated and Forecast-generic. The BIM documents measure goes in the BIM Document plugin's pack. Then `capex/pa_tiles.js` builds the page DB + binding and returns the SAME flat map as `tiles.flatten`. **Witness W-PA-FLAT:** `pa_tiles` flat == `tiles` flat, key by key, over the sample, the twin scenario and the test instance, under each filter combination. Keys that differ only by D2 are listed and must be exactly the named ones. A break measure +1 → FAIL.
**PR C — the switch and the deletion.** `dash.js` reads `pa_tiles`. `tiles.js` keeps only what is listed as not moved, and the PR states lines +/−. W-PA-TILES, W-DASH-* and W-PA-FLAT are re-run and their logs read.
**PR A RESULT (2026-11-13; logs read, scratch not committed):** `erp/goal_engine.js` now has one measure-SQL builder `calcSql`, which `evaluate` itself uses. It adds the context parse (`parseCtx`, through the kernel's `Env.parseContext`), `evaluateCalc` and `queryKeys`. Request-type measures are NOT parsed, because legacy `MRequestType.getSqlPI` :417-457 does not parse them. **W-PA-CTX `§PA_CTX_VERDICT PASS judged=21 fail=0 inconclusive=0`:** (a) a `@$C_Currency_ID@` row over C_Order == the plain SUM for currencies 100 / 102 / 301; the 301 order's GrandTotal is NULL, so its value is 0 with state EMPTY. (a2) its drill == that currency's orders. (b) an unparsable tag → ERROR, 0. (c) 16 of 16 CAPEX COUNT measures have drill rows == count (12 non-zero). Break `PA_CTX_BREAK=1` → `FAIL fail=4`. First run read INCONCLUSIVE, which was the witness reporting its own blind spot: the migrations had not applied (`no such function: TO_TIMESTAMP`, the host's SQL functions were not registered) and purchase orders hold one currency; both fixed in the witness. **No regression:** W-GOAL-ENGINE 5/0 · W-PA-ENGINE 23/0 · W-PA-RENDER 32/0 · W-HEALTH-OVERDUE 4/0 · W-Q3-INTDIV 3/0 · W-GOAL-STORE 10/0 · W-PA-TILES (pilot :8088) `PASS judged=89 fail=0 inconclusive=7`.
**PR B RESULT (2026-11-13; logs read, scratch not committed):**
- **Rows.** `202611131300_CAPEX-PAGE-MEASURES.sql`: 18 measures, ids 1000070-1000088, EntityType 'A'. Money rows use `@$C_Currency_ID@`. Pack 1.0.6, plugin 1.0.6; released 1.0.5 regenerates `unchanged`. Applied to the local pilot (then restarted: the measure cache held the old SQL) and to the test instance (`setup_test_instance.sh` now lists it; `§SETUP_VERDICT PASS`).
- **Four rows end in `ROUND(..., 6)`** (share of plan, forecast, physical, spent). Legacy's numeric division stored 24 digits (`0.000008570955837088603012`), which SQLite REAL cannot hold. Both sides now store 6 places, so the twin is fixed rather than a divergence accepted.
- **Page path.** `capex/pa_tiles.js`: the filtered population goes into an in-memory page DB, and each tile slot (bound by `PA_MeasureCalc_UU`) runs through `goal_engine.evaluateCalc`. It returns `tiles.js compute()`'s own shape, with the not-moved slots taken from it. `adapters.js` gains entity `pacalc` (`PA_MeasureCalc`, optional; legacy type name `QueryPAMeasureCalc`, whose WS script is PR C's).
- **Two engine defects found and fixed.** (1) The goal path dropped the login context: `updateMeasureGoals` now passes `ctx.env`, and `model_pa.js ctxFor` supplies the session's `AdCallout.sessionContext` (`$C_Currency_ID` from `ad_callout.js:934`, mirror of `Login.java:569`). (2) An SQL emptied by an unparsable tag now reads `SQL failed`, so the goal stores 0 as legacy's `DB.getSQLValueBD` error path does.
- **`tiles.js` oracle defect found and fixed.** Forecast and Overrun summed per-project cents (the §5.9 class): the snapshot forecast read 338.27 where exact decimal arithmetic gives 338.2750564… → 338.28. Both now sum the amounts and round once.
- `erp/sw.js` is v1063; `goal_engine.js` and `model_pa.js` are `?v=2`; `audit_sw_precache` 268/0.

**Witnesses:**
- **W-PA-FLAT** `PASS judged=15 fail=0 inconclusive=4`: twin scenario + legacy snapshot × 9 filters each. The 4 INCONCLUSIVE are the twin's empty closed/inactive populations; only D2-named keys differ, under Status ≠ all. Break → `FAIL fail=6`.
- **W-PA-TILES (pilot)** `PASS judged=101 fail=0 inconclusive=13`: the 18 new rows legacy == twin engine; the 0-on-both ones are INCONCLUSIVE. Break → `FAIL fail=114`.
- **W-PA-CTX** 27/0.
- **Unchanged PASS:** W-GOAL-ENGINE 5/0, W-PA-ENGINE 23/0, W-GOAL-STORE 10/0, W-PA-RENDER 32/0 (B1/B2 now expect 1.0.6), W-HOME-TIP 9/0, W-PA-EDITOR 7/0, W-PACK-COVERS 3/0, W-ENTITYTYPE E1-E5, W-TILES-SUM-ONCE 4/0, W-Q3-INTDIV 3/0, W-BASIS-DIFFERS 3/0, W-CAPEX-SAMPLE 8/0, W-PROC-HANDOVER 8/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0, W-DASH-PROJSEL 8/0, W-THEME 11/0, W-PROJECT-PAGE 16/0, W-DASH-TWIN 7/0, W-TWIN-CHAIN 5/0.

**Not ours, named:**
- W-CAPEX `FAIL 8` is stale and was already failing before this change: it expects Forecast `PENDING` (defined since §5.38) and an unavailable-entity list that predates bimdocs, orders and issues.
- W-G6A-HOME is INCONCLUSIVE: there is no persisted 4D run for HHS/SHED on this code key.
- W-CAPEX-PROPOSED was not run: the test instance holds the TD film data, which makes its hand totals FAIL by design (hand-off).

**PR C needs:** the `QueryPAMeasureCalc` WS script, `dash.js` → `pa_tiles`, and the drill from `queryKeys`. Under D2, the drill must follow the PA population.
**D2 REVISED (2026-11-13, before PR C; reason measured):** the page's DEFAULT Status is "Active" (`dash.js fillSelects`), so the first D2 would have changed numbers users read today in the default view (Total Projects would show the active count). Now each slot's binding states its population (`pa_tiles.js POP`: 'P' = Org/Category/Project only, as today for Total/Closed/Active/Closing/Completed-this-year/Forecast/Over-committed/No-PO; others also apply Status). This is still data, still no formula rewritten. W-PA-FLAT now allows **no** difference under any filter.

**PR C1 RESULT (2026-11-13) — the page reads PA rows; logs read:**
- **Legacy read type.** `capex/legacy/ws/pacalc/.../202611131400_CAPEX-WS-PACALC.sql` adds `QueryPAMeasureCalc`. It is applied to the pilot and the test instance (`setup_test_instance.sh` lists it; `§SETUP_VERDICT PASS`; both restarted). The pilot serves 51 rows.
- **Page.** `capex/index.html` loads the kernel's `layered_registry`, `bigdecimal`, `db_export_safe`, `ad_callout`, `callouts/sqlfn`, `goal_engine` and `pa_tiles`. `dash.js compute()` → `CapexPaTiles.compute`; `window.__capex.pa` shows judged/errors. A source without the CAPEX rows falls back to `tiles.js` and says so (`§PA_TILES_PAGE fallback`).
- **Twin scenario.** The builder (`twin_mockchain.js`) now installs the plugin's rows, as a twin with the plugin started holds them (`§TWIN_MOCK_CAPEX_PACK ok=139 failed=0`).
- **Witnesses:**
  - W-DASH-TWIN `PASS 8/0`; new **D8**: the numbers came from the store's PA rows (`judged=37 errors=[]`). Break `DASH_TWIN_PA_BREAK=1` (store without the rows) → D8 FAIL.
  - W-DASH-BIMDOC 8/0: the live test instance, `§DASH_PA source=legacy judged=35 errors=0`.
  - Also PASS: W-PA-FLAT 15/0, W-PA-CTX 27/0, W-TWIN-CHAIN 5/0, W-CAPEX-SAMPLE 8/0, W-DASH-DRILL 59/0, W-TASKDATES 9/0, W-DASH-PROJSEL 8/0, W-CHAIN 11/0, W-THEME 11/0, W-PROJECT-PAGE 16/0, W-SITE-MIG 30/0, and W-PA-TILES 101/0.
- **W-PA-TILES now removes the page-measure goals it creates on the pilot** (`§PA_TILES_CLEANUP 18`). Page measures are not Home goals. Left behind, they made W-DASH-SYNC FAIL 20 ("absent from the Home"), because legacy's Home showed goals the twin's did not. That was a look-and-feel change made by a witness.
- **W-DASH-SYNC** `judged=168 fail=1`. **E2 is open, not explained:** after the pilot edit (SHED Substructure IsComplete=Y) legacy's own Health Green stayed 0 → 0. It is legacy computing its unchanged row 1000023 (W-PA-TILES legacy == twin on it). To investigate before C2: is it the date window of SHED's phases on 2026-11-13?
**PR C2 (next):** delete the moved formulas from `tiles.js` compute (the drill buckets come from `GoalEngine.queryKeys` of the bound slot); `tiles.js` keeps Stage, BIM documents, Last updated, the drill row view, `projectDetail`, availability and formatting. Deletion budget stated in the PR.
**W-DASH-SYNC E2 EXPLAINED (2026-11-13, psql read of the pilot):** not a defect, a premise that expired. SHED's phase EndDates come from the 4D fold (Substructure 2026-11-02 … Finishes 2026-11-10). On 2026-11-04, when the witness was written, only Substructure was due, so ticking it gave SPI = EV/PV = 1 → Green. On 2026-11-13 every phase is due (PV ≈ 15.1 M), so ticking Substructure alone (68,811.67) leaves SPI ≈ 0.005 → Red, and Green stays 0 on BOTH sides (the E arm's Home == legacy checks all PASS after the edit). Witness fix (not done, W-DASH-SYNC's own lane §5.25): tick every phase whose EndDate ≤ today, or pick the project and phase from today's data, so the premise is chosen from the data, not from a date that passes.

### 5.67 SPEC — the film's test data as ONE 2Pack, for a client's GardenWorld AND the Kernel ERP twin (2026-11-13; written before code; NOT started)
**Owner (verbatim, 2026-11-13):** *"can we also include the mock test data setup in GardenWorld client use in last film as a 2Pack.zip SYSNOVA can pack into GW to view as they may not have much project data activity yet. This can be used by the teams to examine usability. The test data can remain in their GW"* · *"Thus also a twin data set for our kernel ERP. It be good if it uses twin 2Pack import parity"* · on how the documents get made (after first choosing a Java process): *"rephrase, to run the pocesses we can spell out in the UAT document"* · *"it acts as a hands-on exercise"*.
**Decided by those words:**
- **No Java process.** The pack carries data rows only.
- **Every document is made by the testers by hand,** following numbered steps in the UAT Guide (§5.65 chapters 4–6): purchase order, material receipt, vendor invoice, payment, issue to the project. Each step runs iDempiere's own document actions, so books and stock are right on both sides.
- **The data stays in their GardenWorld** (no teardown).
- **The SAME zip is packed into legacy and into the twin** (twin Pack In = `erp/pipo_packin.js`, the `GenericPOElementHandler` port), and a witness proves the two land the same rows.
**ONE definition:** `capex/tools/td_scenario.js`, the 12 neutral projects the film used (`SC`, `PRODUCTS`, `CHAIN`). It is read, never retyped. Its offsets are relative to a day. The pack bakes absolute dates for the day it is built (`--today`), so the dashboard's spread (green / amber / red / over budget / ready for handover) holds near that day and drifts as the calendar passes. That is the same mechanism that expired W-DASH-SYNC E2. The cover note states the build day; rebuild the pack before shipping it.
**In the pack (Data elements, written by the ONE Pack Out engine `erp/tools/gen_2pack.js` → `pipo_packgen.js` → `pipo_packout.js`):**
- the two test products `TD_MAT_A`/`TD_MAT_B` and their purchase price-list rows;
- the 12 `C_Project` rows, Value `TD_*`, names ending "(test data)";
- their `C_ProjectPhase` rows with dates, the ticked phases `IsComplete='Y'` (a field, not a document);
- the `C_ProjectTask` rows;
- the `C_ProjectLine` rows with product, quantity and planned price.
- GardenWorld's own rows (business partner, warehouse, price list, product 139, currency) are REFERENCES by UU (`{ table, uu }`), never copied. A site whose GardenWorld lacks one of them gets PackIn's own "not found" error, not a guess.
**Not in the pack (named):** orders, receipts, invoices, payments, project issues (the hands-on exercise). `ProjectBalanceAmt` and the other amounts iDempiere computes start at 0, so the Spent / Health / Budget tiles fill as the testers complete documents. The film's `CHAIN` stages become the exercise's target states ("make TD_RIVERSIDE's order Paid, TD_DATAHALL's Received, ...").
**Deliverable:** `capex/plugin/testdata_2pack/2Pack_TD_<version>.zip` + its generation source `capex/plugin/testdata_2pack_source.mjs` (the `capex_2pack_source.mjs` pattern). EntityType 'A'. Install on legacy: System Admin › Pack In (or `RUN_ApplyPackInFromFolder.sh`); on the twin: Plugins & Releases › install by URL, or Pack In.
**Witness W-TD-PACK (before shipping):**
- (1) Pack In into the test instance (legacy) and into the twin.
- (2) Per table, the rows read back by UU are equal on both sides, every column the pack carries, and equal to `td_scenario.js` at the build day.
- (3) The dashboard on each side shows 12 projects and their phase dates; amounts are 0 until documents exist.
- (4) Pack In twice changes nothing.
- 0 rows judged → INCONCLUSIVE.
- Break runs, each MUST FAIL: the twin skips one table; one phase date is shifted.
- The UAT Guide's exercise steps are then witnessed by the §5.65 W-UAT-GUIDE, step by step.
**§5.67 MEASURED (2026-11-13, iDempiere source + test DB; owner in the loop: *"2Packs must be packed in by specific client admin"*):**
- **Which client.** A data 2Pack lands in the client that packs it in. New rows take the session's client. For a client > 0 every UUID goes through `POFinder.getTargetUUID` (`AD_Package_UUID_Map`; `PoFiller.java:386-392`, `POFinder.java:45-56`), so the same zip can be packed into several clients, each getting its own copy. Folder Pack In picks the client from the file name, `<yyyymmddHHMM>_<ClientValue>_<info>.zip`; `ALL-CLIENTS` = every active non-system client (`PackInFolder.java:35-46`, `RUN_ApplyPackInFromFolder.sh`).
- **Where rows may live.** `AD_Table.AccessLevel` is 3 (Client+Organization) for C_Project, phase, line, task, M_Product and category, so these are tenant rows, never System (PA_MeasureCalc / PA_Goal are 6). The pack references GardenWorld's own rows (price list version 103 by official id, org 11), so it is named for GardenWorld.
- **Currency.** `C_Project.C_Currency_ID` is mandatory with no dictionary default; `MProject.beforeSave` (:408-422) sets it from the project's price list version, and Pack In saves through the model class (`GenericPOElementHandler`: `table.getPO`). So the pack leaves `C_Currency_ID` out (an IDEMPIERE-1563 column exclusion) and the currency is the one of the client's price list: *"as the user sets"*.
