### 5.69 SPEC — G1: the twin's Pack In takes a client's DATA 2Pack the way legacy does (2026-11-13; written before code; owner on where the rows live: *"Base DB + replay"*)
**The issue:** the §5.67 zip packs into legacy (12/42/84/84/2, currency from the price list, 4 `M_Product_Acct` rows, a second run changes nothing) but the twin's `pipo_packin.apply` is the dictionary path: it keeps the pack's client, has no `AD_Package_UUID_Map`, and writes SQL with no model class, so `C_Project.C_Currency_ID` stays empty and `MProduct.afterSave` never runs.
**Where the rows live (owner's choice):** the base DB, as plugin packs do (`plugin_overlay._packApply`: one atomic pass on a `recording(db)`, the delta + the zip bytes kept, replayed on every load). Teamwork sync does not carry them; each site packs the zip in, as on legacy.
**What changes, generic (no table names in the engine):**
- `apply(db, xml, { clientId, model: { ML, env } })`. `clientId > 0` = the client-data path (`Env.getAD_Client_ID(ctx)`):
  - `POFinder.getTargetUUID` (:45-56): a pack UUID is looked up in `AD_Package_UUID_Map` for this client first, on the record AND on every uuid reference (`ReferenceUtils.java:54`).
  - `POFinder.findPO` (:80-108): a row found by UUID in another client > 0 is left alone: a new UUID is generated, mapped (`updateUUIDMap` :64-78) and the record is created. Found by ID in another client: refused with legacy's message.
  - `PoFiller.autoFill` :386-392: new rows take the session client and the target UUID.
- `model` given = every New / Update goes through `ModelLayer.run(q, env, SAVE)` (`PO.saveEx`: initial defaults, beforeSave, the validators, afterSave). The group's ops (CRUD_CREATE / UPDATE / DELETE, `{__opRef}` keys) are written into the same DB inside the Pack In savepoint, keys from `AD_Sequence` (`MSequence.getNextID`), audit columns from the session. A refused save fails the whole pack (legacy: the PackIn transaction rolls back).
- No `model` = today's dictionary path, unchanged (W-PACKIN, W-PLUGIN-PACKS must not move).
**Witness W-TD-PACK-TWIN** (`capex/tests/witness_td_pack_twin.js`, headless browser on `erp/idempiere.html`, the shipped zip, read with the system `unzip` for the independent counts):
- T1 rows per table by target UUID == the pack's record count per table (independent scan of `PackOut.xml`); every row's `AD_Client_ID` = 11.
- T2 every column the pack carries == the row (references resolved to the target ids), per row; 0 rows judged = INCONCLUSIVE.
- T3 the model layer ran: every project's `C_Currency_ID` == its price list's currency (not in the pack), and `M_Product_Acct` rows exist for the 2 products (legacy wrote 4).
- T4 the same zip again: every record `Same`, counts unchanged.
- T5 remap: a copy of one project moved to another client before the run → the run creates a NEW project for client 11 with a new UUID and one `AD_Package_UUID_Map` row; a further run finds it through the map (`Same`).
- Break runs, each MUST FAIL: `WTD_BREAK=skiptable` (the c_projecttask records removed from the XML fed to the engine, the independent count still from the zip) → T1; `WTD_BREAK=shiftdate` (one phase StartDate shifted a day in the XML, the comparison against the zip's) → T2; `WTD_BREAK=nomodel` (no `model`) → T3.
**Not in this step (next, named):** the client-admin Pack In entry in the twin (item (b)), keeping the delta for replay, the legacy half of W-TD-PACK (twin rows == legacy rows), G2 mandatory check.
**§5.69 RESULT (2026-11-13; logs read, scratch not committed):**
- **W-TD-PACK-TWIN `PASS 6/0`** on the shipped zip (225 records):
  - T1: 1/2/12/42/84/84 rows, all client 11.
  - T2: 4,564 values equal.
  - T3: currency 100 from the price list on 12 of 12 projects; `M_Product_Acct` 2+2; model side rows `m_product_acct 4, m_cost 4, m_product_category_acct 2, ad_treenodepr 2` (`§PACKIN ... modelOps=405`).
  - T4: re-walk as legacy, T4b: a third run changes nothing.
  - T5: remap 1 map row; the new project takes its 5 phases and 10 lines.
  - Breaks, each FAIL: `skiptable` T1, `shiftdate` T2 (`StartDate pack=2026-03-28 twin=2026-03-01`), `nomodel` T2+T3 (currency null, 0 acct rows).
- **Defects found and fixed on the way:**
  - (1) `AD_Package_UUID_Map` was in the dictionary with no physical table. It is now created by the boot overlay `ad_import_tables.js ensurePipo`, NOT in `patches/ad_seed.db.sql`, because a line there moves the Team BASE hash (`idempiere.html` note at the overlay calls). A first try regenerated the whole seed patch from the live DB and dropped 11,664 lines: reverted, never committed.
  - (2) The page's tables return mixed-case column names, so a found row read as all-undefined. Every re-run then failed with a duplicate key, and the cross-client check never fired: the run moved client 12's project back to client 11. The found row is now lower-cased at the lookup (`lcRow`).

The likely cause is `PO.is_ValueChanged` :802, which uses Java `equals` (BigDecimal scale); not proven. Next: read which column legacy sees changed, then port that rule.

### 5.70 SPEC — (b): a client admin packs the data 2Pack in from the twin's own Pack In window, kept across reloads (2026-11-13; owner "Proceed"; written before code)
**The issue:** after §5.69 the engine takes a client's data pack, but nothing in the twin lets a user run it. The twin packs in only at plugin Start (System). Legacy's way: a client admin opens window 50005 **Pack In** (AD_Package_Imp_Proc, AccessLevel 6), types a Name, attaches the zip, and presses the **Pack In** button (AD_Process 50008, `org.adempiere.pipo.PackIn` → `PackInProcess.doIt` :79-158). GardenWorld Admin (role 102) has window and process access in the seed.
**What legacy does (read, `PackInProcess.java`, `PackIn.importXML` :153-188, `PackInHandler` :171-207):**
- No attachment, or no entry 0 → returns *"Please attach the pack out archive before running the process"*.
- Otherwise it unzips entry 0, reads `dict/PackOut.xml` and walks it under the SESSION context, so the client-data path of §5.69 applies.
- It writes `AD_Package_Imp` in the session client and org, with `AD_Package_Imp_Proc_ID` set, plus the `_Inst` / `_Detail` rows.
- Success returns `Processed=<n> Un-Resolved=<n>`; unresolved elements throw.
- Finally it sets `DateProcessed` and `P_Msg` on the Pack In record.
**Build (generic, no table named in the host):**
- `erp/processes/PackIn.js`: the `PackInProcess` port. `prepare` takes Record_ID. `doIt` reads the record and its attachment through the process transaction (the same `AdAttachment.lookup` the window uses); no attachment → legacy's message. The rest is asynchronous in a browser (attachment bytes, `DecompressionStream`), so `doIt` hands it to the host as the process's continuation.
- **ONE new generic seam (`ad_process.js` + `idempiere.html`):**
  - A ported process may set `pi.then = () => Promise<{ ok, summary, commitOps }>`.
  - The host awaits it BEFORE it draws the result card, so the user sees the final message as on legacy.
  - The host commits its `commitOps` the same way as a process's own ops.
  - No continuation = today's path, unchanged.
- `PluginEngine.packInClient(...)` (`plugin_overlay.js`, beside the plugin packs it mirrors):
  - attachment entry 0 → `PackOut.xml`;
  - one atomic `apply` on a `recording(rawDb)` with `clientId` + `model`, then `recordImport` in the session client/org with `AD_Package_Imp_Proc_ID`;
  - the zip bytes and the delta are KEPT under `client:<AD_Client_ID>:<name>` in the same store as the plugin packs;
  - returns `Processed=… Un-Resolved=…` and the update of `DateProcessed` / `P_Msg` as ops.
- **Replay:** `_packsReplay` replays a client entry's delta like any pack. When the fingerprint changed, it re-applies the kept zip through the client path (`clientId` + `model`), not the dictionary path.
**Witness W-TD-PACKIN-WINDOW** (`capex/tests/witness_td_packin_window.js`, headless `erp/idempiere.html?login=GardenAdmin`, the shipped zip, driven through the window's own seams):
- W1: logged in as GardenWorld Admin (client 11, role 102); window 50005 opens for this role.
- W2: a Pack In record saved with no attachment → the process returns legacy's message and writes no project.
- W3: the zip attached through the attachment module → Run → summary `Processed=225 Un-Resolved=0`; 12 projects / 42 / 84 / 84 rows, client 11; `AD_Package_Imp` row client 11 with the record's id; the record's `DateProcessed` set and `P_Msg` == the summary.
- W4: reload and log in again → the same rows are there (`§PACKIN-REPLAY bundle=client:11:…`), not twice.
- 0 rows judged = INCONCLUSIVE.
- Break runs, each MUST FAIL: `WTW_BREAK=noreplay` (the kept entry is deleted before the reload) → W4; `WTW_BREAK=nothen` (the host ignores the continuation) → W3.
**§5.70 RESULT (2026-11-13; logs read, scratch not committed):**
- **W-TD-PACKIN-WINDOW `PASS 4/0`** (`?login`-free: the real login dialog, GardenWorld → GardenAdmin; the menu → window 50005):
  - W1: client 11, role 102, menu 50006 opens the window.
  - W2: a record with no attachment → card *"Please attach the pack out archive before running the process"*, 0 projects.
  - W3: zip attached → card `Processed=225 Un-Resolved=0`; 12/42/84/84 rows in client 11; `AD_Package_Imp` 1000002 client 11 `Completed successfully` with `AD_Package_Imp_Proc_ID` = the record; `DateProcessed 2026-11-13 09:03:32` (the local day) and `P_Msg` == the card. `§PACKIN-CLIENT ... statements=635 ms=672`.
  - W4: reload + login → the same 12/42/84/84, once, from `§PACKIN-REPLAY bundle=client:11:… mode=delta statements=635 ms=10`.
- **Breaks, each FAIL:** `noreplay` → W4 (0 rows after the reload); `nothen` → W2+W3 (the card shows the provisional `Pack In … ...`, nothing written).
- **Seen while building:** a process with no parameters runs at once from the window's button (no parameter dialog), as on legacy. The record is a tip row (id −6); its attachment, the `AD_Package_Imp_Proc_ID` and the `P_Msg` update all address it by that id.
- **Regressions (same set as §5.69):**
  - W-TD-PACK-TWIN 6/0, W-PACKIN GREEN 6/0.
  - NC1-UI 13/0: a live record-process button, the changed host path.

### 5.71 SPEC — (c) W-TD-PACK: the same zip lands the same rows on legacy and on the twin (2026-11-13; written before code; §5.67 W-TD-PACK points 1, 2, 4; point 3, the dashboard, is the next step)
**The issue:** §5.69/§5.70 prove the twin against the zip, and §5.67 proved legacy's counts. Nothing has compared the two sides row by row (TWIN CARDINAL RULE).
**Witness W-TD-PACK** (`capex/tests/witness_td_pack.js`, one run, both sides, the shipped zip):
- **Legacy half:**
  - stop the test instance;
  - take a database copy `idempiere_bimdoc_wtdbak`;
  - `RUN_ApplyPackInFromFolder.sh` on a folder holding ONLY the zip (folder Pack In = the client named in the file name, GardenWorld);
  - read every row of the pack's tables by UUID (`row_to_json`);
  - restore the database from the copy and start the instance;
  - `setup_test_instance.sh --check` must print `§SETUP_VERDICT PASS`.
  - A failure at any step still restores (try/finally).
- **Twin half:** headless `erp/idempiere.html`, real login as GardenAdmin, the shipped path `PluginEngine.packInClient` (the one the Pack In window calls), then the same rows read through the page.
- **Compared, per table, per UUID:**
  - P1: the same set of UUIDs on both sides == the zip's records per table (independent scan).
  - P2: every column the pack carries is equal, after normalising numbers and dates. A uuid reference is compared as the UUID of the row it points at; ids differ by design, since each side numbers rows from its own sequences. Not compared: keys and audit columns (`created`/`updated`/`createdby`/`updatedby`).
  - P3: what the model layer writes outside the pack is equal: `C_Project.C_Currency_ID` per project, the `M_Product_Acct` row count per product, the `M_Cost` row count per product.
  - 0 rows judged = INCONCLUSIVE.
- **Break runs, each MUST FAIL:** `WTP_BREAK=twinshift` (one phase StartDate shifted by a day in the zip given to the TWIN only) → P2; `WTP_BREAK=nomodel` (the twin walk without the model layer) → P3.
- A break run needs only the twin half, so it reads the legacy rows from the previous run's JSON (`~/.cache/capex_film/wtd_pack/legacy_rows.json`, written by a full run). The file records the zip's sha256, and a stale one is refused.
